  function rollOneTimeEvents(gameState2) {
    gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents ?? (gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents = []);
    const fired = new Set(gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents);
    for (const h of HISTORICAL_EVENTS) {
      if (gameState2.year === h.year && !fired.has(h.id)) {
        h.apply(gameState2);
        gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents.push(h.id);
      }
    }
    for (const h of HYPOTHETICAL_EVENTS) {
      if (gameState2.year >= h.minYear && !fired.has(h.id) && randomUnitV253() < h.chancePerQuarter) {
        h.apply(gameState2);
        gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents.push(h.id);
      }
    }
    for (const h of ALT_HISTORY_EVENTS) {
      const inWindow = gameState2.year >= h.minYear && (h.maxYear == null || gameState2.year <= h.maxYear);
      if (inWindow && !fired.has(h.id) && randomUnitV253() < h.chancePerQuarter) {
        h.apply(gameState2);
        gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents.push(h.id);
      }
      if (!inWindow && h.maxYear != null && gameState2.year > h.maxYear && !fired.has(h.id)) {
        gameState2.firedOneTimeEvents.push(h.id);
      }
    }
  }
  var FUTURE_GEOPOLITICS_MIN_YEAR = 2027;
  var FUTURE_GEOPOLITICS_CHANCE_PER_QUARTER = 0.012;
  var FUTURE_GEOPOLITICS_ARCHETYPES = [
    { type: "normalization", label: "\u{1F54A}\uFE0F \uAD6D\uAD50 \uC815\uC0C1\uD654", isImprovement: true, magnitudeMin: 35, magnitudeMax: 65 },
    { type: "trade_bloc", label: "\u{1F91D} \uACBD\uC81C\uD611\uB825\uCCB4 \uACB0\uC131", isImprovement: true, magnitudeMin: 15, magnitudeMax: 30 },
    { type: "rupture", label: "\u{1F494} \uC678\uAD50 \uB2E8\uC808", isImprovement: false, magnitudeMin: -50, magnitudeMax: -25 },

  ];
  function rollFutureGeopolitics(gameState2) {
    if (gameState2.year < FUTURE_GEOPOLITICS_MIN_YEAR) return null;
    if (randomUnitV253() >= FUTURE_GEOPOLITICS_CHANCE_PER_QUARTER) return null;
    const a = pick(HOST_CANDIDATE_COUNTRIES);
    const others = HOST_CANDIDATE_COUNTRIES.filter((c) => c !== a);
    const b = pick(others);
    const currentScore = getRelationScore(a, b, gameState2.year, gameState2);
    const viable = FUTURE_GEOPOLITICS_ARCHETYPES.filter(
      (ar) => ar.isImprovement ? currentScore < 85 : currentScore > 10
    );
    if (viable.length === 0) return null;
    const archetype = pick(viable);
    const delta = archetype.magnitudeMin + randomUnitV253() * (archetype.magnitudeMax - archetype.magnitudeMin);
    const newScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(currentScore + delta)));
    setRelationOverride(gameState2, a, b, newScore);
    const e = {
      type: "future_geopolitics",
      scope: "global",
      demandMultiplier: 1,
      costMultiplier: 1,
      remainingQuarters: 1,
      label: `\u{1F310} [\uAC00\uC0C1\uC5ED\uC0AC] ${gameState2.year}\uB144 ${a}-${b} ${archetype.label}: \uC591\uAD6D\uAD00\uACC4 \uC601\uAD6C \uBCC0\uD654(${Math.round(currentScore)}\u2192${newScore}) \u2014 \uC2AC\uB86F \uAD50\uC12D \uAC00\uB2A5\uC131\uC5D0 \uACC4\uC18D \uC601\uD5A5`
    };
    pushEvent(gameState2, e);
    return e;
  }
  var WAR_MIN_YEAR = 2027;
  var WAR_START_CHANCE_PER_QUARTER = 6e-3;
  var WAR_ELIGIBLE_RELATION_MAX = 40;
  var WAR_MIN_DURATION_QUARTERS = 2;
  var WAR_PEACE_CHANCE_PER_QUARTER = 0.12;
  var WAR_POST_PEACE_RELATION = [20, 40];
  function rollWarStart(gameState2) {
    if (gameState2.year < WAR_MIN_YEAR) return null;
    gameState2.activeWars ?? (gameState2.activeWars = []);
    if (randomUnitV253() >= WAR_START_CHANCE_PER_QUARTER) return null;
    const a = pick(HOST_CANDIDATE_COUNTRIES);
    const scored = HOST_CANDIDATE_COUNTRIES.filter((c) => c !== a).map((b2) => ({ b: b2, badness: Math.max(1, 100 - getRelationScore(a, b2, gameState2.year, gameState2)) }));
    const total = scored.reduce((s, x) => s + x.badness ** 2, 0);
    let roll = randomUnitV253() * total;
    let chosenB = null;
    for (const x of scored) {
      roll -= x.badness ** 2;
      if (roll <= 0) {
        chosenB = x.b;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!chosenB) return null;
    const b = chosenB;
    const currentScore = getRelationScore(a, b, gameState2.year, gameState2);
    if (currentScore > WAR_ELIGIBLE_RELATION_MAX) return null;
    const key = [a, b].sort().join("-");
    if (gameState2.activeWars.some((w) => w.key === key)) return null;
    gameState2.activeWars.push({ key, a, b, startYear: gameState2.year, startQuarter: gameState2.quarter });
    setRelationOverride(gameState2, a, b, 2);
    const e = {
      type: "war_start",
      scope: "global",
      demandMultiplier: 1,
      costMultiplier: 1,
      remainingQuarters: 1,
      label: `\u2694\uFE0F [\uAC00\uC0C1\uC5ED\uC0AC] ${gameState2.year}\uB144 ${a}-${b} \uC804\uC7C1 \uBC1C\uBC1C(\uAE30\uC874 \uAD00\uACC4\uB3C4 ${currentScore}) \u2014 \uC591\uAD6D\uAC04 \uB178\uC120 \uC0AC\uC2E4\uC0C1 \uC804\uBA74 \uC911\uB2E8`
    };
    pushEvent(gameState2, e);
    return e;
  }
  function rollWarEnd(gameState2) {
    if (!gameState2.activeWars || gameState2.activeWars.length === 0) return null;
    for (let i = 0; i < gameState2.activeWars.length; i++) {
      const war = gameState2.activeWars[i];
      const elapsedQuarters = (gameState2.year - war.startYear) * 4 + (gameState2.quarter - war.startQuarter);
      if (elapsedQuarters < WAR_MIN_DURATION_QUARTERS) continue;
      if (randomUnitV253() >= WAR_PEACE_CHANCE_PER_QUARTER) continue;
      const [min, max] = WAR_POST_PEACE_RELATION;
      const newScore = Math.round(min + randomUnitV253() * (max - min));
      setRelationOverride(gameState2, war.a, war.b, newScore);
      gameState2.activeWars.splice(i, 1);
      const e = {
        type: "war_end",
        scope: "global",
        demandMultiplier: 1,
        costMultiplier: 1,
        remainingQuarters: 1,
        label: `\u{1F54A}\uFE0F [\uAC00\uC0C1\uC5ED\uC0AC] ${gameState2.year}\uB144 ${war.a}-${war.b} \uD734\uC804/\uC885\uC804 \u2014 \uAD00\uACC4 \uC77C\uBD80 \uD68C\uBCF5(${newScore}\uC810, \uC644\uC804 \uC815\uC0C1\uD654\uB294 \uC544\uB2D8)`
      };
      pushEvent(gameState2, e);
      return e;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function checkIndustryMilestones(gameState2, { airports, aircraftList }) {

  }
  function rollNewEvents(gameState2, { airlines, airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, airports, aircraftList } = {}) {

    const warStart = rollWarStart(gameState2);

      const e = { type: "regional_boom", scope: `region:${region}`, demandMultiplier: 1.3, costMultiplier: 1, remainingQuarters: 3, label: `${region} \uC9C0\uC5ED \uACBD\uAE30\uD638\uD669: \uC218\uC694 +30% (3\uBD84\uAE30)` };

      triggered.push(e);
    }
    if (randomUnitV253() < 0.05) {
      const region = pick(REGIONS);
      const e = { type: "regional_disaster", scope: `region:${region}`, demandMultiplier: 0.5, costMultiplier: 1, remainingQuarters: 2, label: `${region} \uC9C0\uC5ED \uC790\uC5F0\uC7AC\uD574: \uC218\uC694 -50% (2\uBD84\uAE30)` };
      pushEvent(gameState2, e);
      triggered.push(e);
    }

      const countries = [...new Set(airlines.map((a2) => a2.country).filter(Boolean))];
      const a = pick(countries);
      const others = countries.filter((c) => c !== a);
      if (others.length > 0) {
        const b = pick(others);
        const isDispute = randomUnitV253() < 0.6;
        const delta = isDispute ? -(15 + randomUnitV253() * 15) : 10 + randomUnitV253() * 15;
        const quarters = isDispute ? 2 + Math.floor(randomUnitV253() * 3) : 3 + Math.floor(randomUnitV253() * 3);
        adjustRelationTemporarily(gameState2, a, b, delta, quarters);
        const e = {
          type: "diplomatic",
          scope: "global",
          demandMultiplier: 1,
          costMultiplier: 1,
          remainingQuarters: 1,
          label: isDispute ? `\u{1F54A}\uFE0F \uC678\uAD50 \uAC08\uB4F1: ${a}-${b} \uAD00\uACC4 \uC77C\uC2DC \uC545\uD654 (${quarters}\uBD84\uAE30, \uC2AC\uB86F \uAD50\uC12D \uC5B4\uB824\uC6CC\uC9D0)` : `\u{1F91D} \uC815\uC0C1\uD68C\uB2F4 \uC131\uC0AC: ${a}-${b} \uAD00\uACC4 \uC77C\uC2DC \uAC1C\uC120 (${quarters}\uBD84\uAE30, \uC2AC\uB86F \uAD50\uC12D \uC218\uC6D4\uD574\uC9D0)`

        pushEvent(gameState2, e);
        triggered.push(e);
      }
    }
    for (const airline of airlines ?? []) {
      if (airline.routes.length === 0) continue;
      if (randomUnitV253() < 0.04 * welfareLaborDisputeRiskMultiplier(airline)) {
        const e = { type: "strike", scope: `airline:${airline.id}`, demandMultiplier: 0.7, costMultiplier: 1.5, remainingQuarters: 1, label: `${airline.name} \uB178\uB3D9\uC7C1\uC758: \uC218\uC694 -30% / \uBE44\uC6A9 +50% (1\uBD84\uAE30)` };
        pushEvent(gameState2, e);
        triggered.push(e);
      }
      if (randomUnitV253() < 0.02 * maintenanceCapabilityRiskMultiplier(airline)) {
        const compensation = 3e6 + randomUnitV253() * 7e6;
        airline.cash -= compensation;
        gameState2.extraordinaryEventLedgerV253 ?? (gameState2.extraordinaryEventLedgerV253 = []);
        gameState2.extraordinaryEventLedgerV253.push({

          type: "accident",
          scope: `airline:${airline.id}`,

        };
        pushEvent(gameState2, e);
        triggered.push(e);
      }
    }
    return triggered;
  }
  function groundRandomAircraft(airline, quarters) {
    const available = airline.fleet.filter((f) => !f.groundedUntilQuarterCount);
    const n = Math.min(1, available.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      available[i].groundedUntilQuarterCount = quarters;
    }
    return n;
  }
  function decayEvents(gameState2) {
    gameState2.activeEvents ?? (gameState2.activeEvents = []);
    gameState2.activeEvents = gameState2.activeEvents.map((e) => ({ ...e, remainingQuarters: e.remainingQuarters - 1 })).filter((e) => e.remainingQuarters > 0);
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines ?? []) {
      for (const f of airline.fleet) {
        if (f.groundedUntilQuarterCount) {
          f.groundedUntilQuarterCount -= 1;
          if (f.groundedUntilQuarterCount <= 0) delete f.groundedUntilQuarterCount;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  function getEventModifiers(gameState2, { airlineId, airportA, airportB }) {
    let demandMultiplier = 1;
    let costMultiplier = 1;
    for (const e of gameState2.activeEvents ?? []) {
      const matches = e.scope === "global" || e.scope === `airline:${airlineId}` || e.scope === `region:${airportA.region}` || e.scope === `region:${airportB.region}` || e.scope === `airport:${airportA.iata}` || e.scope === `airport:${airportB.iata}`;
      if (matches) {
        if (e.type === "covid19") {
          const domestic = airportA.country === airportB.country;
          demandMultiplier *= domestic ? (e.domesticDemandMultiplier ?? e.demandMultiplier) : (e.internationalDemandMultiplier ?? e.demandMultiplier);
        } else demandMultiplier *= e.demandMultiplier;
        costMultiplier *= e.costMultiplier;
      }
    }
    for (const war of gameState2.activeWars ?? []) {
      const touchesWar = airportA.country === war.a && airportB.country === war.b || airportA.country === war.b && airportB.country === war.a;
      if (touchesWar) demandMultiplier *= 0.03;
    }
    return { demandMultiplier, costMultiplier };
  }
  // src/core/route.js
  var PURPOSES = ["business", "leisure", "vfr_essential"];
  var SELF_PER_OD_LIMIT = 5;
  var ALLIANCE_PER_OD_LIMIT = 3;
  var GLOBAL_CANDIDATE_LIMIT = 300;
  var SELF_INCIDENT_LIMIT = 12;
  var ALLIANCE_OWN_INCIDENT_LIMIT = 10;
  var ALLIANCE_PARTNER_INCIDENT_LIMIT = 24;
  function pairKey2(a, b) {
    return [a, b].sort().join("-");
  }
  function addType(target, routeId, values) {
    target[routeId] ?? (target[routeId] = { business: 0, leisure: 0, vfr_essential: 0 });
    for (const k of PURPOSES) target[routeId][k] += values[k] ?? 0;
  }
  function totals(typeMap) {
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(typeMap).map(([id, v]) => [id, PURPOSES.reduce((sum, k) => sum + (v[k] ?? 0), 0)]));
  }
  function maxDetourRatio(year) {
    if (year < 1970) return 2;
    if (year < 1990) return 1.8;
    if (year < 2010) return 1.6;
    return 1.5;
  }
  function detourScore(ratio, year) {
    const max = maxDetourRatio(year);
    if (!Number.isFinite(ratio) || ratio < 1 || ratio > max) return 0;
    const t = (ratio - 1) / Math.max(0.01, max - 1);
    return Math.max(0.12, 1 - t * 0.88);
  }
  function transferRoleFactor(airport, year) {
    const role = airportRoleAtYear(airport, year);
    return {
      transfer_hub: 1,
      primary_gateway: 0.96,
      domestic_gateway: 0.78,
      secondary_airport: 0.62,
      regional_airport: 0.48,
      limited_civil: 0.22,
      inactive: 0
    }[role] ?? 0.5;
  }
  function captureRates(mode) {
    return mode === "self" ? { business: 0.28, leisure: 0.45, vfr_essential: 0.5 } : { business: 0.18, leisure: 0.3, vfr_essential: 0.34 };
  }
  function routeCapacity(route, aircraftById2) {
    const aircraft = aircraftById2?.[route.aircraftId];
    if (!aircraft || route.routeType === "cargo") return Infinity;
    return Math.max(0, aircraft.seats * (route.flightsPerWeekOneWay ?? 0) * 2 * 0.62);
  }
  function routeWeight(route, aircraftById2) {
    const aircraft = aircraftById2?.[route.aircraftId];
    const seats = aircraft?.seats ?? 100;
    return seats * Math.max(1, route.flightsPerWeekOneWay ?? 1);
  }
  function topIncident(routes, limit, aircraftById2) {
    if (routes.length <= limit) return routes;
    return [...routes].sort((a, b) => routeWeight(b, aircraftById2) - routeWeight(a, aircraftById2)).slice(0, limit);
  }
  function candidateSkeleton({ mode, hub, route1, route2, endpointA, endpointB, gameState: gameState2, airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }) {
    const hubAirport = airportsByIata2[hub];
    const A = airportsByIata2[endpointA];
    const B = airportsByIata2[endpointB];
    if (!hubAirport || !A || !B || metroId(A) === metroId(B)) return null;
    const directDistance = distanceBetweenAirports(A, B);
    const leg1 = route1.distanceKm ?? distanceBetweenAirports(A, hubAirport);
    const leg2 = route2.distanceKm ?? distanceBetweenAirports(hubAirport, B);
    const ratio = (leg1 + leg2) / Math.max(1, directDistance);
    const detour = detourScore(ratio, gameState2.year);
    if (detour <= 0) return null;
    const transferQuality = transferConvenienceIndex(hubAirport, gameState2.year) * transferRoleFactor(hubAirport, gameState2.year);
    if (transferQuality <= 0) return null;
    const score = detour * transferQuality * (mode === "self" ? 1 : 0.72);
    return {
      mode,
      hub,
      route1,
      route2,
      endpointA,
      endpointB,
      A,
      B,
      directDistance,
      odKey: pairKey2(metroId(A), metroId(B)),
      detour,
      transferQuality,
      score,
      key: `${mode}|${hub}|${route1.id}|${route2.id}|${pairKey2(endpointA, endpointB)}`
    };
  }
  function materializeCandidate(candidate, gameState2, airportsByIata2) {
    const demand = estimateWeeklyDemandBreakdown(
      candidate.A,
      candidate.B,
      candidate.directDistance,
      gameState2.year,
      gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[candidate.A.iata] ?? 0,
      gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[candidate.B.iata] ?? 0,
      gameState2,
      { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }
    );
    const rates = captureRates(candidate.mode);
    const byType = {};
    for (const k of PURPOSES) {
      const purposeTransferPenalty = k === "business" ? 0.78 : k === "leisure" ? 1 : 1.06;
      byType[k] = (demand.byType[k] ?? 0) * rates[k] * candidate.detour * candidate.transferQuality * purposeTransferPenalty;
    }
    const { A, B, directDistance, detour, transferQuality, ...publicCandidate } = candidate;
    return { ...publicCandidate, byType, metroByType: demand.metroByType };
  }
  function shortlistCandidates(skeletons) {
    const byOd = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const candidate of skeletons) {
      if (!byOd.has(candidate.odKey)) byOd.set(candidate.odKey, []);
      byOd.get(candidate.odKey).push(candidate);
    }
    const selected = [];
    for (const group of byOd.values()) {
      const self = group.filter((candidate) => candidate.mode === "self").sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, SELF_PER_OD_LIMIT);
      const alliance = group.filter((candidate) => candidate.mode === "alliance").sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, ALLIANCE_PER_OD_LIMIT);
      selected.push(...self, ...alliance);
    }
    return selected.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, GLOBAL_CANDIDATE_LIMIT);
  }
  function computeNetworkConnectingDemand(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, aircraftById: aircraftById2 = null, includeSelf = true, includeAlliance = true } = {}) {
    var _a, _b, _c, _d, _e;
    const selfType = {};
    const allianceType = {};
    if (!airline?.routes?.length || !airportsByIata2) {
      return { selfBonus: {}, allianceBonus: {}, selfByType: {}, allianceByType: {}, itineraries: [] };
    }
    const passengerRoutes = airline.routes.filter((r) => (r.routeType ?? "passenger") === "passenger");
    const ownByAirport = {};
    for (const r of passengerRoutes) {
      (ownByAirport[_a = r.a] ?? (ownByAirport[_a] = [])).push(r);
      (ownByAirport[_b = r.b] ?? (ownByAirport[_b] = [])).push(r);
    }
    const ownDirectMetroPairs = new Set(passengerRoutes.map((r) => {
      const A = airportsByIata2[r.a], B = airportsByIata2[r.b];
      return A && B ? pairKey2(metroId(A), metroId(B)) : pairKey2(r.a, r.b);
    }));
    const skeletons = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const addSkeleton = (candidate) => {
      if (candidate && !seen.has(candidate.key)) {
        skeletons.push(candidate);
        seen.add(candidate.key);
      }
    };
    if (includeSelf) {
      for (const [hub, allIncident] of Object.entries(ownByAirport)) {
        const incident = topIncident(allIncident, SELF_INCIDENT_LIMIT, aircraftById2);
        for (let i = 0; i < incident.length; i++) {
          for (let j = i + 1; j < incident.length; j++) {
            const r1 = incident[i], r2 = incident[j];
            const e1 = r1.a === hub ? r1.b : r1.a;
            const e2 = r2.a === hub ? r2.b : r2.a;
            if (e1 === e2) continue;
            const A = airportsByIata2[e1], B = airportsByIata2[e2];
            if (!A || !B || ownDirectMetroPairs.has(pairKey2(metroId(A), metroId(B)))) continue;
            addSkeleton(candidateSkeleton({ mode: "self", hub, route1: r1, route2: r2, endpointA: e1, endpointB: e2, gameState: gameState2, airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (includeAlliance && airline.alliance) {
      const partners = alliancePartners(airline, gameState2);
      const partnerByAirport = {};
      for (const partner of partners) {
        for (const r of partner.routes ?? []) {
          if ((r.routeType ?? "passenger") !== "passenger") continue;
          (partnerByAirport[_c = r.a] ?? (partnerByAirport[_c] = [])).push(r);
          (partnerByAirport[_d = r.b] ?? (partnerByAirport[_d] = [])).push(r);
        }
      }
      for (const [hub, allMyIncident] of Object.entries(ownByAirport)) {
        const myIncident = topIncident(allMyIncident, ALLIANCE_OWN_INCIDENT_LIMIT, aircraftById2);
        const partnerIncident = topIncident(partnerByAirport[hub] ?? [], ALLIANCE_PARTNER_INCIDENT_LIMIT, aircraftById2);
        for (const myRoute of myIncident) {
          const myEnd = myRoute.a === hub ? myRoute.b : myRoute.a;
          for (const partnerRoute of partnerIncident) {
            const partnerEnd = partnerRoute.a === hub ? partnerRoute.b : partnerRoute.a;
            if (myEnd === partnerEnd) continue;
            const A = airportsByIata2[myEnd], B = airportsByIata2[partnerEnd];
            if (!A || !B || ownDirectMetroPairs.has(pairKey2(metroId(A), metroId(B)))) continue;
            addSkeleton(candidateSkeleton({ mode: "alliance", hub, route1: myRoute, route2: partnerRoute, endpointA: myEnd, endpointB: partnerEnd, gameState: gameState2, airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    const candidates = shortlistCandidates(skeletons).map((candidate) => materializeCandidate(candidate, gameState2, airportsByIata2));
    const byOd = {};
    for (const candidate of candidates) (byOd[_e = candidate.odKey] ?? (byOd[_e] = [])).push(candidate);
    const remainingCapacity = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      for (const route of [candidate.route1, candidate.route2]) {
        if (!remainingCapacity.has(route)) remainingCapacity.set(route, routeCapacity(route, aircraftById2));
      }
    }
    const accepted = [];
    for (const group of Object.values(byOd)) {
      group.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
      const hasSelf = group.some((candidate) => candidate.mode === "self");
      const capRate = captureRates(hasSelf ? "self" : "alliance");
      const metroCap = {};
      for (const k of PURPOSES) {
        const metroTotal = Math.max(...group.map((candidate) => candidate.metroByType?.[k] ?? 0));
        metroCap[k] = metroTotal * capRate[k];
      }
      const proposedTotals = {};
      for (const k of PURPOSES) proposedTotals[k] = group.reduce((sum, candidate) => sum + (candidate.byType[k] ?? 0), 0);
      for (const candidate of group) {
        const proposed = {};
        for (const k of PURPOSES) {
          const scale = proposedTotals[k] > 0 ? Math.min(1, metroCap[k] / proposedTotals[k]) : 0;
          proposed[k] = (candidate.byType[k] ?? 0) * scale;
        }
        const totalProposed = Object.values(proposed).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
        const cap1 = remainingCapacity.get(candidate.route1) ?? Infinity;
        const cap2 = remainingCapacity.get(candidate.route2) ?? Infinity;
        const capacityScale = totalProposed > 0 ? Math.min(1, cap1 / totalProposed, cap2 / totalProposed) : 0;
        const allocated = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(proposed).map(([k, value]) => [k, value * capacityScale]));
        const allocatedTotal = Object.values(allocated).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
        if (allocatedTotal <= 0) continue;
        remainingCapacity.set(candidate.route1, cap1 - allocatedTotal);
        remainingCapacity.set(candidate.route2, cap2 - allocatedTotal);
        if (candidate.mode === "self") {
          addType(selfType, candidate.route1.id, allocated);
          addType(selfType, candidate.route2.id, allocated);
        } else {
          addType(allianceType, candidate.route1.id, allocated);
        }
        accepted.push({ ...candidate, allocatedByType: allocated, allocatedTotal });
      }
    }
    return {
      selfBonus: totals(selfType),
      allianceBonus: totals(allianceType),
      selfByType: selfType,
      allianceByType: allianceType,
      itineraries: accepted
    };
  }

  // src/core/networkAnalytics.js
  function safeNumber(value) {
    return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
  }
  function routeIdOf(routeOrId) {
    return typeof routeOrId === "string" ? routeOrId : routeOrId?.id;
  }
  function annotateRouteNetworkEconomics(routeReports, networkDemand, { allianceWeight = 0.6 } = {}) {
    const byId = new Map(routeReports.map((report) => [report.routeId, report]));
    for (const report of routeReports) {
      if ((report.routeType ?? "passenger") !== "passenger") {
        report.directProfit = safeNumber(report.profit);
        report.selfConnectionProfit = 0;
        report.allianceConnectionProfit = 0;
        report.downstreamSelfContribution = 0;
        report.networkContributionProfit = safeNumber(report.profit);
        continue;
      }
      const totalPax = Math.max(0, safeNumber(report.paxCarried));
      const selfPax = Math.max(0, safeNumber(report.connectingPax));
      const alliancePax = Math.max(0, safeNumber(report.allianceConnectingPax));
      const connectionPax = selfPax + alliancePax;
      const connectionShare = totalPax > 0 ? Math.min(1, connectionPax / totalPax) : 0;
      const passengerRevenue = Math.max(0, safeNumber(report.revenue) - safeNumber(report.cargoRevenue));
      const inferredSga = Math.max(
        0,
        safeNumber(report.operatingCost) - safeNumber(report.fuelCrewMaintCost) - safeNumber(report.landingFeesCost) - safeNumber(report.mealCost) - safeNumber(report.cargoHandlingCost)
      );
      const connectionIncrementalProfit = passengerRevenue * connectionShare - safeNumber(report.mealCost) * connectionShare - inferredSga * connectionShare;
      const selfShare = connectionPax > 0 ? selfPax / connectionPax : 0;
      const allianceShare = connectionPax > 0 ? alliancePax / connectionPax : 0;
      report.selfConnectionProfit = connectionIncrementalProfit * selfShare;
      report.allianceConnectionProfit = connectionIncrementalProfit * allianceShare;
      report.directProfit = safeNumber(report.profit) - report.selfConnectionProfit - report.allianceConnectionProfit;
      report.downstreamSelfContribution = 0;
    }
    const selfItineraries = (networkDemand?.itineraries ?? []).filter((it) => it.mode === "self");
    for (const itinerary of selfItineraries) {
      const firstId = routeIdOf(itinerary.route1);
      const secondId = routeIdOf(itinerary.route2);
      const first = byId.get(firstId);
      const second = byId.get(secondId);
      if (!first || !second) continue;
      const quarterPassengers = Math.max(0, safeNumber(itinerary.allocatedTotal)) * 13;
      const firstShare = safeNumber(first.connectingPax) > 0 ? Math.min(1, quarterPassengers / safeNumber(first.connectingPax)) : 0;
      const secondShare = safeNumber(second.connectingPax) > 0 ? Math.min(1, quarterPassengers / safeNumber(second.connectingPax)) : 0;
      first.downstreamSelfContribution += safeNumber(second.selfConnectionProfit) * secondShare;
      second.downstreamSelfContribution += safeNumber(first.selfConnectionProfit) * firstShare;
    }
    for (const report of routeReports) {
      report.networkContributionProfit = safeNumber(report.directProfit) + safeNumber(report.selfConnectionProfit) + allianceWeight * safeNumber(report.allianceConnectionProfit) + safeNumber(report.downstreamSelfContribution);
    }
    return routeReports;
  }
  function summarizeNetworkFromStatement(airline, statement = airline?.history?.at?.(-1)) {
    const routes = statement?.routes ?? [];
    const summary = {
      localPax: 0,
      selfConnectingPax: 0,
      allianceConnectingPax: 0,
      directProfit: 0,
      selfConnectionProfit: 0,
      allianceConnectionProfit: 0,
      downstreamSelfContribution: 0,
      networkContributionProfit: 0,
      hubs: [],
      topRoutes: []
    };
    const hubMap = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const report of routes) {
      summary.localPax += safeNumber(report.localPax);
      summary.selfConnectingPax += safeNumber(report.connectingPax);
      summary.allianceConnectingPax += safeNumber(report.allianceConnectingPax);
      summary.directProfit += safeNumber(report.directProfit ?? report.profit);
      summary.selfConnectionProfit += safeNumber(report.selfConnectionProfit);
      summary.allianceConnectionProfit += safeNumber(report.allianceConnectionProfit);
      summary.downstreamSelfContribution += safeNumber(report.downstreamSelfContribution);
      summary.networkContributionProfit += safeNumber(report.networkContributionProfit ?? report.profit);
      for (const airport of [report.a, report.b]) {
        if (!hubMap.has(airport)) {
          hubMap.set(airport, {
            airport,
            routes: 0,
            localPax: 0,
            selfConnectingPax: 0,
            allianceConnectingPax: 0,
            networkContributionProfit: 0,
            destinations: /* @__PURE__ */ new Set()
          });
        }
        const hub = hubMap.get(airport);
        hub.routes += 1;
        hub.localPax += safeNumber(report.localPax) / 2;
        hub.selfConnectingPax += safeNumber(report.connectingPax) / 2;
        hub.allianceConnectingPax += safeNumber(report.allianceConnectingPax) / 2;
        hub.networkContributionProfit += safeNumber(report.networkContributionProfit ?? report.profit) / 2;
        hub.destinations.add(report.a === airport ? report.b : report.a);
      }
    }
    summary.hubs = [...hubMap.values()].map((hub) => ({ ...hub, destinations: hub.destinations.size })).sort(
      (a, b) => b.selfConnectingPax + b.allianceConnectingPax - (a.selfConnectingPax + a.allianceConnectingPax) || b.networkContributionProfit - a.networkContributionProfit
    );
    summary.topRoutes = [...routes].sort((a, b) => safeNumber(b.networkContributionProfit ?? b.profit) - safeNumber(a.networkContributionProfit ?? a.profit)).slice(0, 10);
    return summary;
  }
  function updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, patch = {}) {
    gameState2.networkPerformance ?? (gameState2.networkPerformance = {
      candidateEvaluations: 0,
      exactNetworkEvaluations: 0,
      planningCacheHits: 0,
      planningCacheMisses: 0,
      simultaneousTwoLegPlansOpened: 0,
      aiNetworkClosures: 0,
      activeOdMarkets: 0,
      selfItineraries: 0,
      allianceItineraries: 0
    });
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(patch)) {
      gameState2.networkPerformance[key] = safeNumber(gameState2.networkPerformance[key]) + safeNumber(value);
    }
    return gameState2.networkPerformance;
  }

  // src/core/slots.js
  var CAPACITY_BY_TIER = { 5: 220, 4: 120, 3: 50, 2: 18, 1: 8 };
  function slotPoolTypeForAirports(airportA, airportB) {
    return airportA?.country && airportA.country === airportB?.country ? "domestic" : "international";
  }
  function airportSupportsSlotPool(airport, slotType) {
    const scope = airport?.service_scope ?? "both";
    if (slotType === "domestic") return scope !== "international_only";
    return scope !== "domestic_only";
  }
  function slotCapacity(gameState2, airport, slotType = null) {
    const base = CAPACITY_BY_TIER[airport.hub_tier] ?? 50;
    const override = gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride?.[airport.iata] ?? {};
    const legacyBonus = typeof override === "number" ? override : 0;
    const pool = (type) => {
      if (!airportSupportsSlotPool(airport, type)) return 0;
      const ratio = type === "domestic" ? 0.65 : 0.75;
      const poolBase = Math.max(2, Math.round(base * ratio));
      const bonus = typeof override === "object" ? (override[type] ?? 0) : Math.round(legacyBonus * ratio);
      return poolBase + bonus;
    };
    if (slotType === "domestic" || slotType === "international") return pool(slotType);
    return pool("domestic") + pool("international");
  }
  function ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, iata, slotType = null) {
    gameState2.airportSlots ?? (gameState2.airportSlots = {});
    const existing = gameState2.airportSlots[iata] ?? {};
    if (!existing.domestic || !existing.international) {
      const legacyActive = existing.active ?? {};
      const legacyReserved = existing.reserved ?? {};
      gameState2.airportSlots[iata] = {
        domestic: { active: { ...legacyActive }, reserved: { ...legacyReserved } },
        international: { active: {}, reserved: {} }
      };
    }
    const rec = gameState2.airportSlots[iata];
    rec.domestic ?? (rec.domestic = { active: {}, reserved: {} });
    rec.international ?? (rec.international = { active: {}, reserved: {} });
    return slotType ? rec[slotType] : rec;
  }
  function sumValues(obj) {
    return Object.values(obj).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  }
  function slotsUsed(gameState2, iata, slotType = null) {
    if (slotType) {
      const rec = ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, iata, slotType);
      return sumValues(rec.active) + sumValues(rec.reserved);
    }
    return slotsUsed(gameState2, iata, "domestic") + slotsUsed(gameState2, iata, "international");
  }
  function availableSlots(gameState2, airport, slotType = null) {
    if (slotType) return slotCapacity(gameState2, airport, slotType) - slotsUsed(gameState2, airport.iata, slotType);
    return slotCapacity(gameState2, airport) - slotsUsed(gameState2, airport.iata);
  }
  function slotsHeldBy(gameState2, iata, airlineId, slotType = null) {
    if (slotType) {
      const rec = ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, iata, slotType);
      return (rec.active[airlineId] ?? 0) + (rec.reserved[airlineId] ?? 0);
    }
    return slotsHeldBy(gameState2, iata, airlineId, "domestic") + slotsHeldBy(gameState2, iata, airlineId, "international");
  }
  function activeSlotsHeldBy(gameState2, iata, airlineId, slotType = null) {
    if (slotType) return ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, iata, slotType).active[airlineId] ?? 0;
    return activeSlotsHeldBy(gameState2, iata, airlineId, "domestic") + activeSlotsHeldBy(gameState2, iata, airlineId, "international");
  }
  function slotsRequiredForFrequency(flightsPerWeekOneWay) {
    return flightsPerWeekOneWay * 2;
  }
  function slotAcquisitionCost(airport) {
    return 4e4 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 35e3;
  }
  function estimateNegotiationTurns(airline, airport, gameState2, { hasTravelAgency = false } = {}) {
    const relation = getRelationScore(airline.country, airport.country, gameState2.year, gameState2);
    if (relation < 15) {
      return { turns: Infinity, relation, blocked: true };
    }
    let penalty = 0;
    if (relation < 30) penalty = 3;
    else if (relation < 55) penalty = 2;
    else if (relation < 65) penalty = 1;
    const agencyBonus = hasTravelAgency ? 1 : 0;
    const turns = Math.max(0, penalty - agencyBonus);
    return { turns, relation, blocked: false };
  }
  function requestSlots(airline, airport, slotsNeeded, gameState2, { slotType = "international" } = {}) {
    if (slotsNeeded <= 0) return { turns: 0, cost: 0, immediate: true, slotType };
    const free = availableSlots(gameState2, airport, slotType);
    if (slotsNeeded > free) {
      throw new Error(`${airport.iata}: ${slotType === "domestic" ? "국내선" : "국제선"} 슬롯 부족 (필요 ${slotsNeeded}, 여유 ${free}/${slotCapacity(gameState2, airport, slotType)}) — 남는 슬롯은 선착순 배정되며 국가는 혼잡이 지속되면 자동 증설합니다.`);
    }
    const rec = ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, airport.iata, slotType);
    rec.active[airline.id] = (rec.active[airline.id] ?? 0) + slotsNeeded;
    return { turns: 0, cost: 0, immediate: true, slotType };
  }
  function releaseSlots(gameState2, airline, iata, slotsToRelease, slotType = "international") {
    const rec = ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, iata, slotType);
    const held = rec.active[airline.id] ?? 0;
    rec.active[airline.id] = Math.max(0, held - slotsToRelease);
  }
  function processSlotRequests(gameState2) {
    gameState2.pendingSlotRequests ?? (gameState2.pendingSlotRequests = []);
    const granted = [];
    const stillPending = [];
    for (const req of gameState2.pendingSlotRequests) {
      const slotType = req.slotType ?? "international";
      const airport = airportsByIata?.[req.iata];
      if (airport && availableSlots(gameState2, airport, slotType) >= req.slots) {
        const rec = ensureSlotRecord(gameState2, req.iata, slotType);
        rec.active[req.airlineId] = (rec.active[req.airlineId] ?? 0) + req.slots;
        granted.push(req);
      } else stillPending.push(req);
    }
    gameState2.pendingSlotRequests = stillPending;
    return granted;
  }
  function airportExpansionCost(airport) {
    return 5e6 + (airport.hub_tier ?? 3) * 4e6;
  }
  function requestAirportExpansion(airline, airport, gameState2) {
    throw new Error(`${airport.iata}: 공항 증설은 항공사가 부담하지 않습니다. 국내선 또는 국제선 슬롯 사용률이 90% 이상인 상태가 4분기 지속되면 국가가 전액 부담하여 자동 착수합니다.`);
  }
  function processExpansions(gameState2) {
    gameState2.pendingExpansions ?? (gameState2.pendingExpansions = []);
    const completed = [];
    const stillPending = [];
    for (const exp of gameState2.pendingExpansions) {
      exp.turnsRemaining -= 1;
      if (exp.turnsRemaining <= 0) {
        gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride ?? (gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride = {});
        const current = gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride[exp.iata];
        if (typeof current !== "object" || current == null) gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride[exp.iata] = { domestic: 0, international: 0 };
        gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride[exp.iata][exp.slotType] = (gameState2.airportSlotCapacityOverride[exp.iata][exp.slotType] ?? 0) + exp.addedCapacity;
        completed.push(exp);
      } else stillPending.push(exp);
    }
    gameState2.pendingExpansions = stillPending;
    return completed;
  }
  // src/core/aircraftMarket.js
  var ANNUAL_DEPRECIATION = 0.94;
  var SALVAGE_FLOOR = 0.15;
  function depreciationFactor(ageYears) {
    return Math.max(SALVAGE_FLOOR, Math.pow(ANNUAL_DEPRECIATION, Math.max(0, ageYears)));
  }
  function aircraftValueAtAge(aircraft, ageYears) {
    return aircraft.price_musd * depreciationFactor(ageYears);
  }
  function currentFleetEntryValue(aircraft, fleetEntry, currentYear) {
    const age = currentYear - fleetEntry.acquiredYear + (fleetEntry.ageYearsAtAcquisition ?? 0);
    return aircraftValueAtAge(aircraft, age);
  }
  function fleetEntryAge(fleetEntry, currentYear) {
    return Math.max(0, currentYear - fleetEntry.acquiredYear + (fleetEntry.ageYearsAtAcquisition ?? 0));
  }
  function fleetAverageAge(airline, aircraftId, currentYear) {
    const entries = airline.fleet.filter((f) => f.aircraftId === aircraftId);
    if (entries.length === 0) return 0;
    const total = entries.reduce((sum, f) => sum + fleetEntryAge(f, currentYear), 0);
    return total / entries.length;
  }
  function routeConditionAge(airline, route, currentYear) {
    if (route.assignedTails && route.assignedTails.length > 0) {
      const entries = route.assignedTails.map((tn) => airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tn)).filter(Boolean);
      if (entries.length > 0) {
        const total = entries.reduce((sum, f) => sum + fleetEntryAge(f, currentYear), 0);
        return total / entries.length;
      }
    }
    return fleetAverageAge(airline, route.aircraftId, currentYear);
  }
  function conditionOpcostMultiplier(avgAgeYears) {
    return Math.min(1.25, 1 + avgAgeYears * 8e-3);
  }
  function conditionUtilizationMultiplier(avgAgeYears) {
    return Math.max(0.8, 1 - avgAgeYears * 5e-3);
  }
  function buyUsedAircraft(airline, aircraft, ageYears, { tailNumber, year, gameState: gameState2 = null, checkNationality = true } = {}) {
    if (checkNationality && !canPurchaseAircraft(airline.country, aircraft.country, year, gameState2)) {
      throw new Error(
        `${aircraft.manufacturer} ${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) \uAD6C\uB9E4\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4 (${airline.country}-${normalizeManufacturerCountry(aircraft.country)} \uC678\uAD50\uAD00\uACC4\uC0C1 \uB3C4\uC785\uC774 \uBD88\uAC00\uB2A5\uD55C \uC2DC\uAE30).`
      );
    }
    const priceMusd = aircraftValueAtAge(aircraft, ageYears);
    const cost = priceMusd * 1e6;
    if (airline.cash < cost) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: \uC911\uACE0 ${aircraft.model}(${ageYears}\uB144\uC2DD) \uAD6C\uB9E4\uC5D0 $${Math.round(cost).toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694`);
    }
    airline.cash -= cost;
    const entry = {
      aircraftId: aircraft.id,
      tailNumber,
      ownership: "owned",
      acquiredYear: year,
      ageYearsAtAcquisition: ageYears,
      condition: "used"
    };
    airline.fleet.push(entry);
    return { entry, priceMusd };
  }
  function ensureManufacturerRelations(airline) {
    airline.manufacturerRelations ?? (airline.manufacturerRelations = {});
    return airline.manufacturerRelations;
  }
  function getManufacturerGoodwill(airline, manufacturer) {
    return airline.manufacturerRelations?.[manufacturer] ?? 20;
  }
  function addManufacturerGoodwill(airline, manufacturer, amount) {
    const rel = ensureManufacturerRelations(airline);
    rel[manufacturer] = Math.min(100, (rel[manufacturer] ?? 20) + amount);
    return rel[manufacturer];
  }
  function goodwillDiscount(goodwill) {
    return Math.min(0.12, Math.max(0, (goodwill - 20) / 80 * 0.12));
  }
  function bulkOrderDiscount(count) {
    if (count >= 20) return 0.15;
    if (count >= 10) return 0.1;
    if (count >= 5) return 0.05;
    return 0;
  }
  function buyAircraftBulk(airline, aircraft, count, { year, tailPrefix, gameState: gameState2 = null, checkNationality = true } = {}) {
    if (checkNationality && !canPurchaseAircraft(airline.country, aircraft.country, year, gameState2)) {
      throw new Error(
        `${aircraft.manufacturer} ${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) \uAD6C\uB9E4\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4 (${airline.country}-${normalizeManufacturerCountry(aircraft.country)} \uC678\uAD50\uAD00\uACC4\uC0C1 \uB3C4\uC785\uC774 \uBD88\uAC00\uB2A5\uD55C \uC2DC\uAE30).`
      );
    }
    if (count < 1) throw new Error("\uBC1C\uC8FC \uB300\uC218\uB294 1 \uC774\uC0C1\uC774\uC5B4\uC57C \uD568");
    if (!canBuyNewAircraft(aircraft, year)) {
      throw new Error(`${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) ${aircraft.production_end_year}\uB144\uC5D0 \uB2E8\uC885\uB418\uC5B4 \uC2E0\uC870 \uB300\uB7C9\uBC1C\uC8FC\uAC00 \uBD88\uAC00\uB2A5\uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4(\uC911\uACE0 \uAD6C\uB9E4\uB098 \uB9AC\uC2A4\uB294 \uAC00\uB2A5).`);
    }
    const owned = airline.fleet.filter((f) => f.aircraftId === aircraft.id).length;
    if (owned + count > 99) {
      throw new Error(`${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) \uAE30\uC885\uB2F9 \uBCF4\uC720 \uC0C1\uD55C(99\uB300)\uC744 \uCD08\uACFC\uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4(\uD604\uC7AC ${owned}\uB300 + \uBC1C\uC8FC ${count}\uB300).`);
    }
    const goodwill = getManufacturerGoodwill(airline, aircraft.manufacturer);
    const discount = Math.min(0.35, goodwillDiscount(goodwill) + bulkOrderDiscount(count));
    const unitPriceMusd = aircraft.price_musd * (1 - discount);
    const totalCost = unitPriceMusd * 1e6 * count;
    if (airline.cash < totalCost) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: ${aircraft.model} ${count}\uB300 \uBC1C\uC8FC\uC5D0 $${Math.round(totalCost).toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694(\uD560\uC778 ${(discount * 100).toFixed(0)}% \uC801\uC6A9\uB428)`);
    }
    airline.cash -= totalCost;
    const prefix = tailPrefix ?? airline.iataCode ?? airline.id;
    const entries = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const entry = { aircraftId: aircraft.id, tailNumber: allocateTailNumberV253(airline, gameState2, prefix), ownership: "owned", acquiredYear: year };
      airline.fleet.push(entry);
      entries.push(entry);
    }
    const goodwillAfter = addManufacturerGoodwill(airline, aircraft.manufacturer, 3 + Math.min(15, count));
    return { entries, unitPriceMusd, totalCost, discount, goodwillAfter };
  }

  // src/core/finance.js
  var DEFAULT_ANNUAL_RATE_PERCENT = 6;
  var DEFAULT_TERM_YEARS = 10;
  var LOAN_TO_VALUE_CAP = 0.7;
  function quarterlyPayment(principal, annualRatePercent, termYears) {
    const q = annualRatePercent / 100 / 4;
    const n = termYears * 4;
    if (q === 0) return principal / n;
    return principal * q / (1 - Math.pow(1 + q, -n));
  }
  function totalFleetValue(airline, aircraftById2, currentYear) {
    return airline.fleet.filter((f) => f.ownership === "owned").reduce((sum, f) => {
      const aircraft = aircraftById2[f.aircraftId];
      if (!aircraft) return sum;
      return sum + currentFleetEntryValue(aircraft, f, currentYear) * 1e6;
    }, 0);
  }
  function totalOutstandingDebt(airline) {
    return (airline.loans ?? []).reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.remainingPrincipal, 0);
  }
  function availableLoanCapacity(airline, aircraftById2, currentYear) {
    const collateral = totalFleetValue(airline, aircraftById2, currentYear) * LOAN_TO_VALUE_CAP;
    return Math.max(0, collateral - totalOutstandingDebt(airline));
  }
  function takeLoan(airline, amountUsd, { year, annualRatePercent = DEFAULT_ANNUAL_RATE_PERCENT, termYears = DEFAULT_TERM_YEARS, aircraftById: aircraftById2 } = {}) {
    if (amountUsd <= 0) throw new Error("\uB300\uCD9C\uC561\uC740 0\uBCF4\uB2E4 \uCEE4\uC57C \uD568");
    if (aircraftById2) {
      const capacity = availableLoanCapacity(airline, aircraftById2, year);
      if (amountUsd > capacity) {
        throw new Error(`\uB300\uCD9C \uD55C\uB3C4 \uCD08\uACFC: \uBCF4\uC720 \uAE30\uC7AC \uB2F4\uBCF4\uAC00\uCE58 \uAE30\uC900 \uCD5C\uB300 $${Math.round(capacity).toLocaleString()}\uAE4C\uC9C0 \uAC00\uB2A5`);
      }
    }
    airline.loans ?? (airline.loans = []);
    const loan = {
      id: `loan-${airline.loans.length + 1}-${year}`,
      principal: amountUsd,
      remainingPrincipal: amountUsd,
      annualRatePercent,
      termYears,
      startYear: year,
      quarterlyPaymentAmount: quarterlyPayment(amountUsd, annualRatePercent, termYears)
    };
    airline.loans.push(loan);
    airline.cash += amountUsd;
    return loan;
  }
  function processLoanPayments(airline) {
    airline.loans ?? (airline.loans = []);
    let totalPayment = 0;
    let interestPayment = 0;
    let principalPayment = 0;
    const remaining = [];
    for (const loan of airline.loans) {
      if (loan.remainingPrincipal <= 1) continue;
      const interestPortion = loan.remainingPrincipal * (loan.annualRatePercent / 100 / 4);
      const scheduled = Math.min(loan.quarterlyPaymentAmount, loan.remainingPrincipal + interestPortion);
      const principalPortion = Math.max(0, scheduled - interestPortion);
      loan.remainingPrincipal = Math.max(0, loan.remainingPrincipal - principalPortion);
      totalPayment += scheduled;
      interestPayment += interestPortion;
      principalPayment += principalPortion;
      if (loan.remainingPrincipal > 1) remaining.push(loan);
    }
    airline.loans = remaining;
    airline.cash -= totalPayment;
    return { totalPayment, interestPayment, principalPayment };
  }
  function prepayLoan(airline, loanId) {
    airline.loans ?? (airline.loans = []);
    const loan = airline.loans.find((l) => l.id === loanId);
    if (!loan) throw new Error(`\uB300\uCD9C\uC744 \uCC3E\uC744 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC74C: ${loanId}`);
    if (airline.cash < loan.remainingPrincipal) {
      throw new Error(`\uC794\uC561 \uBD80\uC871: \uC870\uAE30\uC0C1\uD658\uC5D0 $${Math.round(loan.remainingPrincipal).toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694`);
    }
    airline.cash -= loan.remainingPrincipal;
    airline.loans = airline.loans.filter((l) => l.id !== loanId);
  }

  function legalCountryOfAirport(iata) {
    const airport = window.__AIR_EMPIRE_INLINE_DATA__?.airports?.find((item) => item.iata === iata);
    return airport?.country ?? null;
  }
  function isEuAviationCountry(country, year) {
    const entry = EU_AVIATION_ENTRY_YEAR[country];
    if (entry == null || year < entry) return false;
    const exit = EU_AVIATION_EXIT_YEAR[country];
    return exit == null || year < exit;
  }
  function isEeaAviationCountry(country, year) {
    if (isEuAviationCountry(country, year)) return true;
    const entry = EEA_NON_EU_ENTRY_YEAR[country];
    return entry != null && year >= entry;
  }
  function isAustraliaNzSingleMarketCountry(country, year) {
    return year >= 1996 && (country === "AU" || country === "NZ");
  }
  function sameQualifiedOwnershipMarket(ownerCountry, targetCountry, year) {
    if (!ownerCountry || !targetCountry) return false;
    if (ownerCountry === targetCountry) return true;
    if (isEeaAviationCountry(ownerCountry, year) && isEeaAviationCountry(targetCountry, year)) return true;
    if (isAustraliaNzSingleMarketCountry(ownerCountry, year) && isAustraliaNzSingleMarketCountry(targetCountry, year)) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function sameFullTrafficMarket(countryA, countryB, operatorCountry, year, cabotage = false) {
    if (isEeaAviationCountry(countryA, year) && isEeaAviationCountry(countryB, year) && isEeaAviationCountry(operatorCountry, year)) {
      return !cabotage || year >= 1997;
    }
    if (isAustraliaNzSingleMarketCountry(countryA, year) && isAustraliaNzSingleMarketCountry(countryB, year) && isAustraliaNzSingleMarketCountry(operatorCountry, year)) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function getCountryOwnershipRule(country, year) {
    if (isEeaAviationCountry(country, year)) {
      return {
        sourceStatus: "verified_regional",
        maxTotalForeign: 49.9,
        maxSingleForeignAirline: 49.9,
        foreignControlAllowed: false,
        regionalQualifiedControlAllowed: true,
        foreignSubsidiaryAllowed: true
      };
    }
    switch (country) {
      case "US":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 25, maxSingleForeignAirline: 25, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "CA":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 49, maxSingleForeignAirline: 25, maxForeignAirlinesAggregate: 25, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "BR":
        return year >= 2019
          ? { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 100, foreignControlAllowed: true, localEntityRequired: true, foreignSubsidiaryAllowed: true }
          : { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 20, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "CN":
        return year >= 2002
          ? { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 49, maxSingleForeignAirline: 25, foreignControlAllowed: false }
          : { sourceStatus: year >= 1994 ? "historical_partial" : "historical_closed", maxTotalForeign: year >= 1994 ? 25 : 0, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "KR":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact_current", maxTotalForeign: 49, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "JP":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact_current", maxTotalForeign: 33.3, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "TW":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 49, maxSingleForeignAirline: 25, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "NZ":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 49, maxSingleForeignAirline: 25, maxForeignAirlinesAggregate: 35, foreignControlAllowed: false, regionalQualifiedControlAllowed: true, foreignSubsidiaryAllowed: true };
      case "RU":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 49, foreignControlAllowed: false, localCeoRequired: true, maxForeignManagementFraction: 1 / 3 };
      case "KZ":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact_current", maxTotalForeign: 49, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "ZA":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact_domestic", maxTotalForeign: 25, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "DO":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_exact", maxTotalForeign: 49, foreignControlAllowed: false, internationalLocalMinimum: 35, domesticLocalMinimum: 51 };
      case "PH":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_general", maxTotalForeign: 40, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "IN":
      case "ID":
      case "TH":
      case "MX":
      case "AR":
      case "AU":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_general", maxTotalForeign: 49, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "IL":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_control", maxTotalForeign: 49, foreignControlAllowed: false };
      case "HK":
      case "SG":
      case "MY":
      case "PA":
        return { sourceStatus: "verified_non_numeric_control", maxTotalForeign: UNKNOWN_FOREIGN_MINORITY_CAP, foreignControlAllowed: false, actualControlReview: true };
      default:
        return { sourceStatus: "conservative_unknown", maxTotalForeign: UNKNOWN_FOREIGN_MINORITY_CAP, foreignControlAllowed: false, actualControlReview: true };
    }
  }
  function timelineEntryForAirline(airlineId, year) {
    const timeline = AIRLINE_LEGAL_TIMELINES[airlineId] ?? [];
    return timeline.find((entry) => year >= (entry.from ?? -Infinity) && year < (entry.to ?? Infinity)) ?? null;
  }
  function ownerGroupAtYear(airlineId, year) {
    const rows = AIRLINE_OWNER_GROUP_TIMELINES[airlineId] ?? [];
    let result = null;
    for (const row of rows) if (year >= row.from) result = row.ownerGroupId;
    return result;
  }
  function getAirlineLegalStatus(airline, year) {
    const base = {
      targetTradable: true,
      controlTransactionAllowed: true,
      stateControlBlock: false,
      minorityOnly: false,
      maxControlStake: 100,
      mustPreserveEntityCrossBorder: true,
      operationalStatus: "ACTIVE",
      ownerGroupId: airline?.ownerGroupId ?? ownerGroupAtYear(airline?.id, year) ?? airline?.id,
      sourceStatus: "game_default"
    };
    if (!airline) return base;
    if (STATE_BLOCK_AIRLINE_IDS.has(airline.id)) {
      Object.assign(base, {
        targetTradable: false,
        controlTransactionAllowed: false,
        stateControlBlock: true,
        sourceStatus: "research_state_block"
      });
    }
    if (MINORITY_ONLY_AIRLINE_IDS.has(airline.id)) {
      Object.assign(base, {
        targetTradable: true,
        controlTransactionAllowed: false,
        minorityOnly: true,
        maxControlStake: 49.9,
        sourceStatus: "research_minority_only"
      });
    }
    const timeline = timelineEntryForAirline(airline.id, year);
    if (timeline) Object.assign(base, timeline, { sourceStatus: "research_timeline" });
    if (airline.legalOperationalStatus && airline.legalOperationalStatus !== "ACTIVE") {
      base.operationalStatus = airline.legalOperationalStatus;
      base.targetTradable = false;
    }
    return base;
  }
  function annotateAirlineLegalIdentity(airline, year) {
    if (!airline) return airline;
    airline.operatingCountry ?? (airline.operatingCountry = airline.country);
    airline.aocCountry ?? (airline.aocCountry = airline.country);
    airline.ownerGroupId ?? (airline.ownerGroupId = ownerGroupAtYear(airline.id, year) ?? airline.parentAirlineId ?? airline.id);
    airline.legalEngineVersion = LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION;
    const status = getAirlineLegalStatus(airline, year);
    airline.legalOperationalStatus = status.operationalStatus;
    return airline;
  }
  function foreignStakeSummary(targetAirline, gameState2) {
    const holders = gameState2?.stakes?.[targetAirline.id] ?? {};
    let totalForeign = 0;
    let foreignAirlineAggregate = 0;
    const byHolder = {};
    for (const [holderId, percentRaw] of Object.entries(holders)) {
      const percent = Math.max(0, Number(percentRaw) || 0);
      const holder = gameState2.airlines?.find((a) => a.id === holderId);
      if (!holder || holder.country === targetAirline.country) continue;
      totalForeign += percent;
      foreignAirlineAggregate += percent;
      byHolder[holderId] = percent;
    }
    return { totalForeign, foreignAirlineAggregate, byHolder };
  }
  function evaluateStakePurchase(acquirer, targetAirline, requestedPercent, gameState2) {
    if (!acquirer || !targetAirline) return { ok: false, reason: "항공사를 찾을 수 없습니다.", maxPurchasable: 0 };
    if (acquirer.id === targetAirline.id) return { ok: false, reason: "자기 자신의 지분은 살 수 없습니다.", maxPurchasable: 0 };
    const year = gameState2?.year ?? (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getFullYear();
    const status = getAirlineLegalStatus(targetAirline, year);
    if (!status.targetTradable) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        reason: status.stateControlBlock
          ? `${targetAirline.name}은(는) 해당 시점의 국영·공공 지배 항공사여서 일반 주식매입 대상이 아닙니다.`
          : `${targetAirline.name}은(는) 해당 시점에 일반 매입이 허용되지 않습니다(${status.operationalStatus}).`,
        maxPurchasable: 0,
        status
      };
    }
    const currentStake = getStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, acquirer.id);
    const remaining = Math.max(0, 100 - currentStake);
    if (remaining <= 0) return { ok: false, reason: "이미 지분을 전부 보유하고 있습니다.", maxPurchasable: 0, status };
    const sameCountry = acquirer.country === targetAirline.country;
    const regionalQualified = sameQualifiedOwnershipMarket(acquirer.country, targetAirline.country, year);
    const rule = getCountryOwnershipRule(targetAirline.country, year);
    let maxPurchasable = remaining;
    if (!sameCountry && !(regionalQualified && rule.regionalQualifiedControlAllowed)) {
      const summary = foreignStakeSummary(targetAirline, gameState2);
      const ownForeignStake = summary.byHolder[acquirer.id] ?? 0;
      const totalForeignExcludingOwn = Math.max(0, summary.totalForeign - ownForeignStake);
      maxPurchasable = Math.min(maxPurchasable, Math.max(0, (rule.maxTotalForeign ?? UNKNOWN_FOREIGN_MINORITY_CAP) - totalForeignExcludingOwn - currentStake));
      if (rule.maxSingleForeignAirline != null) {
        maxPurchasable = Math.min(maxPurchasable, Math.max(0, rule.maxSingleForeignAirline - currentStake));
      }
      if (rule.maxForeignAirlinesAggregate != null) {
        const aggregateExcludingOwn = Math.max(0, summary.foreignAirlineAggregate - ownForeignStake);
        maxPurchasable = Math.min(maxPurchasable, Math.max(0, rule.maxForeignAirlinesAggregate - aggregateExcludingOwn - currentStake));
      }
      if (!rule.foreignControlAllowed) {
        maxPurchasable = Math.min(maxPurchasable, Math.max(0, 49.9 - currentStake));
      }
    }
    if (status.minorityOnly || !status.controlTransactionAllowed) {
      maxPurchasable = Math.min(maxPurchasable, Math.max(0, (status.maxControlStake ?? 49.9) - currentStake));
    }
    if (!(maxPurchasable > 1e-6)) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        reason: `${targetAirline.country}의 외자·실질지배 제한 때문에 추가 지분을 취득할 수 없습니다.`,
        maxPurchasable: 0,
        status,
        rule
      };
    }
    const actual = Math.min(Math.max(0, requestedPercent), maxPurchasable);
    return {
      ok: actual > 0,
      maxPurchasable,
      actualPercent: actual,
      capped: actual + 1e-6 < requestedPercent,
      status,
      rule,
      sameCountry,
      regionalQualified
    };
  }
  function evaluateControlAcquisition(acquirer, targetAirline, gameState2) {
    const year = gameState2?.year ?? (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getFullYear();
    const status = getAirlineLegalStatus(targetAirline, year);
    if (!status.controlTransactionAllowed) {
      return { ok: false, reason: `${targetAirline.name}의 지배권 취득은 해당 시점에 허용되지 않습니다.` };
    }
    const sameCountry = acquirer.country === targetAirline.country;
    const rule = getCountryOwnershipRule(targetAirline.country, year);
    const regionalQualified = sameQualifiedOwnershipMarket(acquirer.country, targetAirline.country, year);
    if (!sameCountry && !(regionalQualified && rule.regionalQualifiedControlAllowed) && !rule.foreignControlAllowed) {
      return { ok: false, reason: `${targetAirline.country} 항공사는 외국 항공사의 실질적 지배를 받을 수 없습니다. 현지 소수지분 투자만 가능합니다.` };
    }
    return { ok: true, sameCountry, regionalQualified, rule, status };
  }
  function evaluateAbsorptionMerger(acquirer, targetAirline, gameState2) {
    const control = evaluateControlAcquisition(acquirer, targetAirline, gameState2);
    if (!control.ok) return control;
    if (acquirer.country !== targetAirline.country) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        reason: `국경간 인수에서는 ${targetAirline.name}의 ${targetAirline.country} 법인·AOC를 유지해야 합니다. 완전흡수 대신 현지 자회사 편입을 사용하세요.`
      };
    }
    return { ok: true, control };
  }
  function evaluateSubsidiaryFormation(parent, subsidiaryCountry, year) {
    if (parent.country === subsidiaryCountry) return { ok: true, local: true };
    const rule = getCountryOwnershipRule(subsidiaryCountry, year);
    const regionalQualified = sameQualifiedOwnershipMarket(parent.country, subsidiaryCountry, year);
    if (regionalQualified && rule.regionalQualifiedControlAllowed) return { ok: true, regionalQualified: true };
    if (rule.foreignSubsidiaryAllowed && rule.foreignControlAllowed) return { ok: true, foreignControlled: true };
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: `${subsidiaryCountry}의 항공사 소유·실질지배 요건상 ${parent.country} 항공사가 현지 항공사를 100% 자회사로 새로 설립할 수 없습니다. 현지 합작 또는 소수지분 투자가 필요합니다.`
    };
  }
  function trafficRightsGrantKey(operatorCountry, countryA, countryB, freedom) {
    return `${operatorCountry}|${[countryA, countryB].sort().join("-")}|F${freedom}`;
  }
  function hasExplicitTrafficRightsGrant(gameState2, operatorCountry, countryA, countryB, freedom, opts = {}) {
    if (opts.fifthFreedomGranted || opts.cabotageGranted) return true;
    const key = trafficRightsGrantKey(operatorCountry, countryA, countryB, freedom);
    const grant = gameState2?.trafficRightsGrants?.[key];
    if (!grant) return false;
    const year = gameState2.year;
    return year >= (grant.fromYear ?? -Infinity) && year < (grant.toYear ?? Infinity);
  }
  function resolveRouteTrafficRights(airline, airportA, airportB, gameState2, opts = {}) {
    const year = gameState2?.year ?? opts.currentYear ?? (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getFullYear();
    const operatorCountry = airline.aocCountry ?? airline.operatingCountry ?? airline.country;
    const countryA = airportA?.country;
    const countryB = airportB?.country;
    if (!operatorCountry || !countryA || !countryB) {
      return { allowed: true, freedom: null, localOdMultiplier: 1, sourceStatus: "missing_country" };
    }
    if (opts.technicalStopOnly) {
      return {
        allowed: true,
        freedom: 2,
        trafficRight: "SECOND_TECHNICAL_STOP",
        localOdMultiplier: 0,
        continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
        technicalStopOnly: true,
        sourceStatus: "technical_stop"
      };
    }
    if (countryA === operatorCountry && countryB === operatorCountry) {
      return {
        allowed: true,
        freedom: 0,
        trafficRight: "DOMESTIC_HOME",
        localOdMultiplier: 1,
        continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
        sourceStatus: "home_domestic"
      };
    }
    if (countryA !== countryB && (countryA === operatorCountry || countryB === operatorCountry)) {
      const foreignCountry = countryA === operatorCountry ? countryB : countryA;
      const relation = getRelationScore(operatorCountry, foreignCountry, year, gameState2);
      if (relation < TRAFFIC_RIGHT_RELATION_THRESHOLD) {
        return { allowed: false, freedom: countryA === operatorCountry ? 3 : 4, relation, reason: `${operatorCountry}-${foreignCountry} 항공관계가 단절·제한 상태입니다.` };
      }
      return {
        allowed: true,
        freedom: countryA === operatorCountry ? 3 : 4,
        trafficRight: countryA === operatorCountry ? "THIRD_FREEDOM" : "FOURTH_FREEDOM",
        localOdMultiplier: 1,
        continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
        relation,
        sourceStatus: "bilateral_base"
      };
    }
    if (countryA !== countryB && sameFullTrafficMarket(countryA, countryB, operatorCountry, year, false)) {
      return {
        allowed: true,
        freedom: 7,
        trafficRight: "SEVENTH_REGIONAL_MARKET",
        localOdMultiplier: 1,
        continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
        sourceStatus: "regional_market"
      };
    }
    const isContinuingService = Boolean(opts.serviceChainId && (opts.serviceChainHomeCountry ?? operatorCountry) === operatorCountry);
    if (countryA === countryB) {
      if (sameFullTrafficMarket(countryA, countryB, operatorCountry, year, true)) {
        return {
          allowed: true,
          freedom: 9,
          trafficRight: "NINTH_REGIONAL_CABOTAGE",
          localOdMultiplier: 1,
          continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
          sourceStatus: "regional_market"
        };
      }
      if (isContinuingService) {
        const granted = hasExplicitTrafficRightsGrant(gameState2, operatorCountry, countryA, countryB, 8, opts);
        return {
          allowed: true,
          freedom: 8,
          trafficRight: granted ? "EIGHTH_CABOTAGE_GRANTED" : "EIGHTH_THROUGH_ONLY",
          localOdMultiplier: granted ? 1 : 0,
          continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
          throughServiceOnly: !granted,
          sourceStatus: granted ? "explicit_grant" : "conservative_through_only"
        };
      }
      return {
        allowed: false,
        freedom: 9,
        reason: `${operatorCountry} 항공사에는 ${countryA} 국내선의 제9자유·카보타주 권리가 없습니다.`
      };
    }
    if (isContinuingService) {
      const relationA = getRelationScore(operatorCountry, countryA, year, gameState2);
      const relationB = getRelationScore(operatorCountry, countryB, year, gameState2);
      if (Math.min(relationA, relationB) < TRAFFIC_RIGHT_RELATION_THRESHOLD) {
        return { allowed: false, freedom: 5, reason: "계속운항 구간의 외교·항공관계가 제한되어 있습니다." };
      }
      const granted = hasExplicitTrafficRightsGrant(gameState2, operatorCountry, countryA, countryB, 5, opts);
      return {
        allowed: true,
        freedom: 5,
        trafficRight: granted ? "FIFTH_FREEDOM_GRANTED" : "FIFTH_THROUGH_ONLY",
        localOdMultiplier: granted ? 1 : 0,
        continuingInternationalAllowed: true,
        throughServiceOnly: !granted,
        sourceStatus: granted ? "explicit_grant" : "conservative_through_only"
      };
    }
    return {
      allowed: false,
      freedom: 7,
      reason: `${operatorCountry} 항공사의 ${countryA}-${countryB} 독립 외국간 노선은 제7자유 권리가 없어 개설할 수 없습니다.`
    };
  }
  function applyTrafficRightsToRouteOptions(airline, airportA, airportB, gameState2, opts) {
    const decision = resolveRouteTrafficRights(airline, airportA, airportB, gameState2, opts);
    if (!decision.allowed) throw new Error(`운수권 불허: ${decision.reason}`);
    Object.assign(opts, {
      trafficRight: decision.trafficRight,
      trafficFreedom: decision.freedom,
      localOdMultiplier: decision.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
      continuingInternationalAllowed: decision.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? false,
      throughServiceOnly: decision.throughServiceOnly ?? false,
      technicalStopOnly: decision.technicalStopOnly ?? Boolean(opts.technicalStopOnly),
      legalSourceStatus: decision.sourceStatus,
      legalEngineVersion: LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION
    });
    return decision;
  }
  var DETAILED_ROUTE_HISTORY_QUARTERS = 12;
  function compactAirlineRouteHistory(airline, keepDetailedQuarters = DETAILED_ROUTE_HISTORY_QUARTERS) {
    if (!airline?.history?.length) return 0;
    const compactUntil = Math.max(0, airline.history.length - keepDetailedQuarters);
    let compacted = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < compactUntil; i++) {
      const statement = airline.history[i];
      if (!statement || statement.routesCompacted || !Array.isArray(statement.routes) || statement.routes.length === 0) continue;
      statement.routeSummary = {
        routeCount: statement.routes.length,
        passengerCount: statement.routes.reduce((sum, route) => sum + (route.paxCarried ?? 0), 0),
        localPassengerCount: statement.routes.reduce((sum, route) => sum + (route.localPax ?? 0), 0),
        selfConnectingPassengerCount: statement.routes.reduce((sum, route) => sum + (route.connectingPax ?? 0), 0),
        allianceConnectingPassengerCount: statement.routes.reduce((sum, route) => sum + (route.allianceConnectingPax ?? 0), 0),
        routeProfit: statement.routes.reduce((sum, route) => sum + (route.profit ?? 0), 0)
      };
      statement.routes = [];
      statement.routesCompacted = true;
      compacted += 1;
    }
    return compacted;
  }
  function compactGameRouteHistory(gameState2) {
    let compacted = 0;
    for (const airline of gameState2?.airlines ?? []) compacted += compactAirlineRouteHistory(airline);
    return compacted;
  }
  function migrateLegalEngineState(gameState2) {
    if (!gameState2) return;
    gameState2.legalEngineVersion = LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION;
    gameState2.trafficRightsGrants ?? (gameState2.trafficRightsGrants = {});
    gameState2.legalDecisionLog ?? (gameState2.legalDecisionLog = []);
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines ?? []) {
      annotateAirlineLegalIdentity(airline, gameState2.year);
      for (const route of airline.routes ?? []) {
        route.localOdMultiplier ?? (route.localOdMultiplier = 1);
        route.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? (route.continuingInternationalAllowed = false);
        route.legalEngineVersion ?? (route.legalEngineVersion = 0);
      }
      compactAirlineRouteHistory(airline);
    }
  }

  // src/core/simulation.js
  var WEEKS_PER_QUARTER = 13;
  var QUARTER_TO_MONTHS = 3;
  function createAirline({ id, name, headquarters, foundedYear, startingCash = 35e7 }) {
    return {
      id,
      name,
      headquarters,
      foundedYear,
      cash: startingCash,
      fleet: [],
      // { aircraftId, tailNumber, ownership: 'owned'|'leased', acquiredYear }
      routes: [],
      // { id, a, b, aircraftId, flightsPerWeekOneWay, fareMultiplier }
      history: []
      // 분기별 재무제표
    };
  }
  var STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA = {
    // 항공기 가격 재조정(price_musd x7, 2026-07 실측 검증으로 발견된 마진 문제 해결의
    // 일환)에 맞춰 시작 자금도 같은 배율로 조정 — 이전과 동일한 "그 시대 주력기 대략
    // 10대" 설계 의도를 그대로 유지함(가격만 오르고 시작 자금이 안 오르면 항공기를
    // 거의 못 사는 상태가 됨).
    1955: 196e6,
    // DC-3($5.6M)류 35대, DC-6($17.5M)급도 11대 근접
    1970: 385e6,
    // Il-18($24.5M)류 15대 이상, 707($49M)급도 7~8대 가능
    1985: 455e6,
    // BAC-111/Il-18급 11대 이상, 737-200($56M)급도 8대 가능
    2e3: 77e7,
    // BAe146($54.6M)류 14대 이상, 737-800($102.9M)급도 7대 가능
    2015: 98e7,
    // E190($63M)류 15대 이상, 737-900ER($120.4M)급도 8대 가능
    2020: 1015e6,
    // 위와 비슷한 수준 + 약간의 물가상승 반영
    2035: 112e7
    // 미래 신형기 10대 여유
  };
  var DEFAULT_STARTING_CASH = 665e6;
  function startingCashForYear(year) {
    if (STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[year] != null) return STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[year];
    const years = Object.keys(STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (year <= years[0]) return STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[years[0]];
    if (year >= years[years.length - 1]) return STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[years[years.length - 1]];
    for (let i = 0; i < years.length - 1; i++) {
      if (year >= years[i] && year <= years[i + 1]) {
        const t = (year - years[i]) / (years[i + 1] - years[i]);
        return Math.round(STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[years[i]] + t * (STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[years[i + 1]] - STARTING_CASH_BY_ERA[years[i]]));
      }
    }
    return DEFAULT_STARTING_CASH;
  }
  function createAirlineFromTemplate(template, { startingYear, startingCash = 35e7 } = {}) {
    const airline = createAirline({
      id: template.id,
      name: template.name,
      headquarters: template.headquarters,
      foundedYear: template.founded_year,
      startingCash
    });
    airline.iataCode = template.iata_code;
    airline.icaoCode = template.icao_code;
    airline.country = template.country;
    const allianceMeta = template.alliance ? ALLIANCES[template.alliance] : null;
    const eraAllowsAlliance = allianceMeta ? (startingYear ?? template.founded_year) >= allianceMeta.founded : true;
    airline.alliance = eraAllowsAlliance ? template.alliance : null;
    airline.allianceJoinedYear = airline.alliance ? allianceMeta.founded : null;
    airline.segment = template.segment ?? "full_service";
    airline.startingYear = startingYear ?? template.founded_year;
    airline.focusCities = [...template.focus_cities ?? []];
    airline.cargoBusinessModel = template.cargo_business_model ?? (airline.segment === "cargo" ? "general_freight" : null);
    airline.cargoHubAirports = [...template.cargo_hub_airports ?? []];
    airline.integratedExpressNetworkV600B = ["integrator","postal_integrator"].includes(airline.cargoBusinessModel);
    airline.cargoSourceUrlV600B = template.cargo_source_url ?? null;
    airline.cargoDataQualityV600B = template.cargo_data_quality ?? null;
    airline.networkAbstractionV600B = Boolean(template.network_abstraction);
    airline.operatingCountry = template.operating_country ?? template.country;
    airline.aocCountry = template.aoc_country ?? template.country;
    airline.ownerGroupId = template.owner_group_id ?? ownerGroupAtYear(template.id, airline.startingYear) ?? template.subsidiary_of ?? template.id;
    annotateAirlineLegalIdentity(airline, airline.startingYear);
    return airline;
  }
  function createSubsidiary(parent, gameState2, { name, iataCode, headquarters, segment, investment }) {
    if (!name || name.trim().length === 0) throw new Error("\uC790\uD68C\uC0AC \uC774\uB984\uC744 \uC785\uB825\uD558\uC138\uC694.");
    if (!headquarters) throw new Error("\uC790\uD68C\uC0AC \uD5C8\uBE0C \uACF5\uD56D\uC744 \uC120\uD0DD\uD558\uC138\uC694.");
    if (investment < 0) throw new Error("\uD22C\uC790\uAE08\uC740 0 \uC774\uC0C1\uC774\uC5B4\uC57C \uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    if (parent.cash < investment) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: \uC790\uD68C\uC0AC \uC124\uB9BD\uC5D0 $${Math.round(investment).toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694(\uBCF4\uC720 $${Math.round(parent.cash).toLocaleString()})`);
    }
    const subsidiaryCountry = legalCountryOfAirport(headquarters) ?? parent.country;
    const formationDecision = evaluateSubsidiaryFormation(parent, subsidiaryCountry, gameState2.year);
    if (!formationDecision.ok) throw new Error(formationDecision.reason);
    const id = `${parent.id}-sub-${Date.now()}-${randomUnitV253().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
    const subsidiary = createAirline({
      id,
      name,
      headquarters,
      foundedYear: gameState2.year,
      startingCash: investment
    });
    subsidiary.iataCode = (iataCode || name.replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, "").slice(0, 2).toUpperCase() || "XX").padEnd(2, "X").slice(0, 2);
    subsidiary.icaoCode = `${subsidiary.iataCode}S`;
    subsidiary.country = subsidiaryCountry;
    subsidiary.operatingCountry = subsidiaryCountry;
    subsidiary.aocCountry = subsidiaryCountry;
    subsidiary.ownerGroupId = parent.ownerGroupId ?? parent.id;
    subsidiary.legalEngineVersion = LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION;
    subsidiary.alliance = null;
    subsidiary.segment = segment ?? "lcc";
    subsidiary.startingYear = gameState2.year;
    subsidiary.parentAirlineId = parent.id;
    parent.cash -= investment;
    gameState2.airlines.push(subsidiary);
    return subsidiary;
  }
  function subsidiariesOf(airline, gameState2) {
    return gameState2.airlines.filter((a) => a.parentAirlineId === airline.id);
  }
  function fundSubsidiary(parent, subsidiary, amount) {
    if (subsidiary.parentAirlineId !== parent.id) {
      throw new Error(`${subsidiary.name}\uC740(\uB294) ${parent.name}\uC758 \uC790\uD68C\uC0AC\uAC00 \uC544\uB2D9\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
    }
    if (!(amount > 0)) {
      throw new Error("\uC9C0\uC6D0\uD560 \uAE08\uC561\uC740 0\uBCF4\uB2E4 \uCEE4\uC57C \uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    }
    if (parent.cash < amount) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: $${Math.round(amount).toLocaleString()} \uC9C0\uC6D0\uC5D0 \uD544\uC694\uD558\uB098 \uBCF4\uC720 $${Math.round(parent.cash).toLocaleString()}`);
    }
    parent.cash -= amount;
    subsidiary.cash += amount;
  }
  function mergeAirlineAssets(source, target) {
    const existingTails = new Set(target.fleet.map((f) => f.tailNumber));
    const tailNumberMap = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const entry of source.fleet) {
      let tailNumber = entry.tailNumber;
      while (existingTails.has(tailNumber)) tailNumber = `${tailNumber}-M`;
      existingTails.add(tailNumber);
      tailNumberMap.set(entry.tailNumber, tailNumber);
      target.fleet.push({ ...entry, tailNumber });
    }
    for (const route of source.routes) {
      const newId = route.id.replace(`${source.id}:`, `${target.id}:`);
      const remappedAssignedTails = (route.assignedTails ?? []).map((tail) => tailNumberMap.get(tail) ?? tail).filter((tail) => existingTails.has(tail));
      const transferred = {
        ...route,
        id: newId,
        ...remappedAssignedTails.length > 0 ? { assignedTails: remappedAssignedTails } : {}
      };
      if (remappedAssignedTails.length === 0) delete transferred.assignedTails;
      const overlapping = target.routes.find((existing) => existing.id === newId);
      if (!overlapping) {
        target.routes.push(transferred);
        continue;
      }
      if ((transferred.flightsPerWeekOneWay ?? 0) > (overlapping.flightsPerWeekOneWay ?? 0)) {
        overlapping.flightsPerWeekOneWay = transferred.flightsPerWeekOneWay;
        overlapping.fareMultiplier = transferred.fareMultiplier;
        overlapping.cabinMix = transferred.cabinMix;
        overlapping.mealTierId = transferred.mealTierId;
        overlapping.cargoFareMultiplier = transferred.cargoFareMultiplier;
        overlapping.routeType = transferred.routeType;
      }
      const combinedTails = [.../* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
        ...overlapping.assignedTails ?? [],
        ...transferred.assignedTails ?? []
      ])].filter((tail) => existingTails.has(tail));
      if (combinedTails.length > 0) overlapping.assignedTails = combinedTails;
      else delete overlapping.assignedTails;
    }
    target.cash += source.cash;
  }
  function reparentSubsidiaries(gameState2, oldParentId, newParentId) {
    const changed = [];
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines) {
      if (airline.parentAirlineId === oldParentId) {
        airline.parentAirlineId = newParentId;
        // 모회사가 소멸하면 잔존 법인은 자기 자신을 새 기업집단으로 삼는다.
        airline.ownerGroupId = newParentId || airline.id;
        changed.push(airline.id);
      }
    }
    return changed;
  }
  function createGameState({ year, quarter = 1, airlines = [] } = {}) {
    const state = {
