      tmaResortClusterState: { activeNodeIds: [], dormantNodeIds: [], lastExpansionTurn: -999 },
      tmaDemandState: { totalRepresentedResorts: 80, coveredWeight: 0 }, mergerEventsV253: []
    };
    restoreRandomStateV253(state);
    return state;
  }
  function pairKey3(a, b) {
    return [a, b].sort().join("-");
  }
  var MAX_FLEET_PER_MODEL = 99;
  function tailNumberExistsV253(state, tail, excludeEntry=null) {
    if (!tail) return false;
    for (const airline of state?.airlines || []) for (const entry of airline.fleet || []) if (entry !== excludeEntry && entry.tailNumber === tail) return true;
    return false;
  }
  function allocateTailNumberV253(airline, state, prefix=null, suffix="") {
    const targetState=state || gameState || {airlines:[airline]};
    targetState.tailNumberLedgerV253??=[];
    if(!Number.isFinite(targetState.nextAircraftSerialV253)){
      targetState.nextAircraftSerialV253=(targetState.airlines||[]).reduce((sum,a)=>sum+(a.fleet?.length||0),0);
    }
    const base=String(prefix || airline?.iataCode || airline?.id || "AC").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g,"").toUpperCase() || "AC";
    let tail;
    do { targetState.nextAircraftSerialV253 += 1; tail=`${base}-${String(targetState.nextAircraftSerialV253).padStart(6,"0")}${suffix||""}`; } while(tailNumberExistsV253(targetState,tail));
    return tail;
  }
  function normalizeTailNumberV253(airline,state,requested,suffix=""){
    if(requested && !tailNumberExistsV253(state,requested))return requested;
    return allocateTailNumberV253(airline,state,airline?.iataCode||airline?.id,suffix);
  }
  function repairDuplicateTailNumbersV253(state){
    state.tailNumberLedgerV253??=[];const seen=new Map(),actions=[];
    for(const airline of state?.airlines||[]){
      for(const entry of airline.fleet||[]){
        const old=entry.tailNumber;const duplicate=!old||seen.has(old);
        if(!duplicate){seen.set(old,{airlineId:airline.id,entry});continue;}
        const next=allocateTailNumberV253(airline,state,airline.iataCode||airline.id,entry.ownership==='leased'?'L':entry.ageYearsAtAcquisition>0?'U':'');
        entry.tailNumber=next;
        const sameAirlineRetainsOld=!!old&&(airline.fleet||[]).some(other=>other!==entry&&other.tailNumber===old);
        for(const route of airline.routes||[])if(Array.isArray(route.assignedTails)){
          let replaced=false;
          route.assignedTails=route.assignedTails.map(t=>{
            if(t!==old)return t;
            if(sameAirlineRetainsOld){if(replaced)return t;replaced=true;return next;}
            return next;
          });
          route.assignedTails=[...new Set(route.assignedTails.filter(t=>t&&((airline.fleet||[]).some(f=>f.tailNumber===t))))];
        }
        const row={turn:state.turnNumber,year:state.year,quarter:state.quarter,airlineId:airline.id,oldTail:old||null,newTail:next,reason:old?'duplicate':'missing'};
        state.tailNumberLedgerV253.push(row);actions.push(row);seen.set(next,{airlineId:airline.id,entry});
      }
    }
    state._v253LastTailRepairActions=actions;return actions;
  }
  function buyAircraft(airline, aircraft, { tailNumber, year, ownership = "owned", gameState: gameState2 = null, checkNationality = true, checkProductionEnd = true } = {}) {
    if (checkNationality && !canPurchaseAircraft(airline.country, aircraft.country, year, gameState2)) {
      throw new Error(
        `${aircraft.manufacturer} ${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) \uAD6C\uB9E4\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4 (${airline.country}-${normalizeManufacturerCountry(aircraft.country)} \uC678\uAD50\uAD00\uACC4\uC0C1 \uB3C4\uC785\uC774 \uBD88\uAC00\uB2A5\uD55C \uC2DC\uAE30).`
      );
    }
    const owned = airline.fleet.filter((f) => f.aircraftId === aircraft.id).length;
    if (owned >= MAX_FLEET_PER_MODEL) {
      throw new Error(`${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) \uAE30\uC885\uB2F9 \uBCF4\uC720 \uC0C1\uD55C(${MAX_FLEET_PER_MODEL}\uB300)\uC5D0 \uB3C4\uB2EC\uD588\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
    }
    if (checkProductionEnd && ownership === "owned" && !canBuyNewAircraft(aircraft, year)) {
      throw new Error(`${aircraft.model}\uC740(\uB294) ${aircraft.production_end_year}\uB144\uC5D0 \uB2E8\uC885\uB418\uC5B4 \uC2E0\uC870 \uAD6C\uB9E4\uAC00 \uBD88\uAC00\uB2A5\uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4(\uC911\uACE0 \uAD6C\uB9E4\uB098 \uB9AC\uC2A4\uB294 \uAC00\uB2A5).`);
    }
    if (ownership === "owned") {
      const price = aircraft.price_musd * 1e6;
      if (airline.cash < price) {
        throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: ${aircraft.model} \uAD6C\uB9E4\uC5D0 $${price.toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694, \uBCF4\uC720 $${airline.cash.toLocaleString()}`);
      }
      airline.cash -= price;
      addManufacturerGoodwill(airline, aircraft.manufacturer, 2);
    }
    tailNumber = normalizeTailNumberV253(airline, gameState2, tailNumber, ownership === "leased" ? "L" : "");
    const entry = { aircraftId: aircraft.id, tailNumber, ownership, acquiredYear: year };
    airline.fleet.push(entry);
    return entry;
  }
  function sellAircraft(airline, tailNumber, aircraft, { salvageRatio = 0.6, force = false, year, gameState: gameState2 = null } = {}) {
    const idx = airline.fleet.findIndex((f) => f.tailNumber === tailNumber);
    if (idx === -1) throw new Error(`기체를 찾을 수 없음: ${tailNumber}`);
    if (!force) {
      const capacityAfterSale = fleetCapacityHours(airline, aircraft.id, { [aircraft.id]: aircraft }) - weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft);
      const used = usedHoursByType(airline, aircraft.id, { [aircraft.id]: aircraft });
      if (used > capacityAfterSale) throw new Error(`${tailNumber}(${aircraft.model}) 매각 불가: 먼저 노선을 감편하거나 폐지하십시오.`);
    }
    const [removed] = airline.fleet.splice(idx, 1);
    if (removed.ownership === "owned") {
      const valueMusd = year != null ? currentFleetEntryValue(aircraft, removed, year) : aircraft.price_musd * salvageRatio;
      airline.cash += valueMusd * 1e6;
      if (gameState2 && aircraft?.id && year != null && (!aircraft.retire_year || year < aircraft.retire_year)) {
        const age = fleetEntryAge(removed, year);
        addUsedAircraftListing(gameState2, aircraft, {
          buildYear: Math.round(year - age),
          condition: usedAircraftCondition(age),
          priceMusd: Math.max(0.1, valueMusd * 1.08),
          source: airline.name,
          quartersRemaining: 8
        });
      }
    } else if (removed.ownership === "leased" && gameState2 && aircraft?.id) {
      returnLeasedAircraftToMarket(gameState2, removed);
    }
    return removed;
  }
  function fleetCountByType(airline, aircraftId, { excludeGrounded = false } = {}) {
    return airline.fleet.filter(
      (f) => f.aircraftId === aircraftId && (!excludeGrounded || !f.groundedUntilQuarterCount)
    ).length;
  }
  function fleetCapacityHours(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, opts = {}) {
    const aircraft = aircraftById2[aircraftId];
    if (!aircraft) return 0;
    const base = fleetCountByType(airline, aircraftId, opts) * weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft);
    if (opts.currentYear == null) return base;
    const avgAge = fleetAverageAge(airline, aircraftId, opts.currentYear);
    return base * conditionUtilizationMultiplier(avgAge);
  }
  function usedHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, excludeRouteId = null) {
    const aircraft = aircraftById2[aircraftId];
    if (!aircraft) return 0;
    return airline.routes.filter((r) => r.aircraftId === aircraftId && r.id !== excludeRouteId).reduce((sum, r) => sum + weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, r.distanceKm, r.flightsPerWeekOneWay, r.officialFlightTimeMin), 0);
  }
  function idleHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, currentYear = null) {
    return fleetCapacityHours(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, { currentYear }) - usedHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2);
  }
  function capacityUtilizationRatio(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, currentYear = null) {
    const usedHours = usedHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2);
    if (usedHours === 0) return 1;
    const availableHours = fleetCapacityHours(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, { excludeGrounded: true, currentYear });
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, availableHours / usedHours));
  }
  var MAX_ROUTES_PER_AIRLINE = 30;
  function addRoute(airline, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2, { a, b, aircraftId, flightsPerWeekOneWay, fareMultiplier = 1, cabinMix, mealTierId, assignedTails, currentYear, routeType = "passenger", cargoFareMultiplier = 1, serviceChainId = null, serviceChainOrder = null, serviceChainHomeCountry = null, technicalStopOnly = false, fifthFreedomGranted = false, cabotageGranted = false, trafficRight = null, trafficFreedom = null, localOdMultiplier = null, continuingInternationalAllowed = null, throughServiceOnly = null, legalSourceStatus = null, legalEngineVersion = null, officialFlightTimeMin = null, resortClusterId = null, representedResortCount = null, contractRateUsdOneWay = null, operationMode = null, supportMissionId = null }) {
    const A = airportsByIata2[a];
    const B = airportsByIata2[b];
    const AC = aircraftById2[aircraftId];
    if (!A || !B) throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uACF5\uD56D: ${a} / ${b}`);
    if (!AC) throw new Error(`알 수 없는 기종: ${aircraftId}`);
    if (!aircraftRoleCompatibleV600(AC, routeType)) {
      throw new Error(routeType === "cargo" ? `${AC.model}은(는) 전용 화물기가 아닙니다. 여객기 벨리카고는 여객노선에서만 운송됩니다.` : `${AC.model}은(는) 전용 화물기이므로 여객노선에 배정할 수 없습니다.`);
    }
    // requestRoute()에서 실제 게임 상태(명시적 운수권 부여 포함)로 이미 심사한
    // 노선은 승인 결과를 그대로 사용한다. 슬롯 대기 후 활성화될 때 빈 임시 상태로
    // 재심사하면 유효한 제5·8자유 부여가 사라질 수 있기 때문이다.
    const hasPreResolvedLegalDecision = legalEngineVersion === LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION && trafficRight != null;
    if (!hasPreResolvedLegalDecision) {
      const legalOpts = {
        serviceChainId,
        serviceChainOrder,
        serviceChainHomeCountry,
        technicalStopOnly,
        fifthFreedomGranted,
        cabotageGranted,
        currentYear
      };
      const legalDecision = resolveRouteTrafficRights(
        airline,
        A,
        B,
        { year: currentYear ?? airline.startingYear ?? (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getFullYear(), trafficRightsGrants: {} },
        legalOpts
      );
      if (!legalDecision.allowed) throw new Error(`운수권 불허: ${legalDecision.reason}`);
      trafficRight ?? (trafficRight = legalDecision.trafficRight);
      trafficFreedom ?? (trafficFreedom = legalDecision.freedom);
      localOdMultiplier ?? (localOdMultiplier = legalDecision.localOdMultiplier ?? 1);
      continuingInternationalAllowed ?? (continuingInternationalAllowed = legalDecision.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? false);
      throughServiceOnly ?? (throughServiceOnly = legalDecision.throughServiceOnly ?? false);
      legalSourceStatus ?? (legalSourceStatus = legalDecision.sourceStatus);
      legalEngineVersion ?? (legalEngineVersion = LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION);
    }
    if (currentYear != null) {
      if (A.opened_year && currentYear < A.opened_year) {
        throw new Error(`${A.iata}(${A.city ?? A.name})\uB294 ${A.opened_year}\uB144\uC5D0 \uAC1C\uD56D\uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4. \uC544\uC9C1 \uC774\uC6A9\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
      }
      if (B.opened_year && currentYear < B.opened_year) {
        throw new Error(`${B.iata}(${B.city ?? B.name})\uB294 ${B.opened_year}\uB144\uC5D0 \uAC1C\uD56D\uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4. \uC544\uC9C1 \uC774\uC6A9\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
      }
      if (A.closed_year && currentYear >= A.closed_year) {
        throw new Error(`${A.iata}(${A.city ?? A.name})\uB294 ${A.closed_year}\uB144\uC5D0 \uD3D0\uD56D\uD588\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4. \uB354 \uC774\uC0C1 \uC774\uC6A9\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
      }
      if (B.closed_year && currentYear >= B.closed_year) {
        throw new Error(`${B.iata}(${B.city ?? B.name})\uB294 ${B.closed_year}\uB144\uC5D0 \uD3D0\uD56D\uD588\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4. \uB354 \uC774\uC0C1 \uC774\uC6A9\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.`);
      }
    }
    if (airline.routes.length >= MAX_ROUTES_PER_AIRLINE) {
      throw new Error(`\uB178\uC120 \uC218 \uC0C1\uD55C(${MAX_ROUTES_PER_AIRLINE}\uAC1C)\uC5D0 \uB3C4\uB2EC\uD588\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4. \uAE30\uC874 \uB178\uC120\uC744 \uD3D0\uC9C0\uD55C \uB4A4 \uB2E4\uC2DC \uC2DC\uB3C4\uD558\uC138\uC694.`);
    }
    const hasAircraft = airline.fleet.some((f) => f.aircraftId === aircraftId);
    if (!hasAircraft) throw new Error(`\uBCF4\uC720\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC740 \uAE30\uC885\uC73C\uB85C\uB294 \uB178\uC120\uC744 \uAC1C\uC124\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC74C: ${aircraftId}`);
    const distanceKm2 = distanceBetweenAirports(A, B);
    const operationFailure = routeOperationFailureReason(AC, A, B, distanceKm2, currentYear);
    if (operationFailure) {
      throw new Error(`${AC.model}\uC740(\uB294) ${a}-${b} \uAD6C\uAC04\uC744 \uC6B4\uD56D\uD560 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4: ${operationFailure}`);
    }
    const requiredHours = weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(AC, distanceKm2, flightsPerWeekOneWay, officialFlightTimeMin);
    if (assignedTails && assignedTails.length > 0) {
      for (const tn of assignedTails) {
        const entry = airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tn);
        if (!entry) throw new Error(`\uBCF4\uC720\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC740 \uAE30\uCCB4: ${tn}`);
        if (entry.aircraftId !== aircraftId) throw new Error(`${tn}\uC740(\uB294) ${AC.model} \uAE30\uC885\uC774 \uC544\uB2D8`);
      }
      const year = currentYear ?? (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).getFullYear();
      let totalCapacity = 0;
      let totalUsedElsewhere = 0;
      for (const tn of assignedTails) {
        const entry = airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tn);
        const age = fleetEntryAge(entry, year);
        totalCapacity += weeklyUtilizationHours(AC) * conditionUtilizationMultiplier(age);
        totalUsedElsewhere += usedHoursByTail(airline, tn, aircraftById2);
      }
      if (totalUsedElsewhere + requiredHours > totalCapacity) {
        throw new Error(
          `\uBC30\uC815\uD55C \uAE30\uCCB4 ${assignedTails.length}\uB300(${AC.model})\uB85C\uB294 \uAC00\uB3D9\uC2DC\uAC04 \uBD80\uC871: \uC774 \uB178\uC120 \uD3EC\uD568 \uC8FC ${(totalUsedElsewhere + requiredHours).toFixed(0)}\uC2DC\uAC04 \uD544\uC694, \uBC30\uC815 \uAE30\uCCB4\uB85C\uB294 \uC8FC ${totalCapacity.toFixed(0)}\uC2DC\uAC04\uAE4C\uC9C0 \uAC00\uB2A5. \uAE30\uCCB4\uB97C \uB354 \uC120\uD0DD\uD558\uAC70\uB098 \uD3B8\uC218\uB97C \uC904\uC774\uC138\uC694.`
        );
      }
    } else {
      const capacityHours = fleetCapacityHours(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2);
      const usedHours = usedHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2);
      if (usedHours + requiredHours > capacityHours) {
        const shortfall = usedHours + requiredHours - capacityHours;
        const extraNeeded = Math.ceil(shortfall / weeklyUtilizationHours(AC));
        throw new Error(
          `${AC.model} \uAC00\uB3D9\uC2DC\uAC04 \uBD80\uC871: \uC774 \uB178\uC120\uAE4C\uC9C0 \uD3EC\uD568\uD558\uBA74 \uC8FC ${(usedHours + requiredHours).toFixed(0)}\uC2DC\uAC04 \uD544\uC694, \uD604\uC7AC \uBCF4\uC720 ${fleetCountByType(airline, aircraftId)}\uB300\uB85C\uB294 \uC8FC ${capacityHours.toFixed(0)}\uC2DC\uAC04\uAE4C\uC9C0 \uAC00\uB2A5. ${AC.model} ${extraNeeded}\uB300\uB97C \uB354 \uAD6C\uB9E4\uD558\uAC70\uB098 \uD3B8\uC218\uB97C \uC904\uC774\uC138\uC694.`
        );
      }
    }
    const id = `${airline.id}:${pairKey3(a, b)}:${aircraftId}`;
    if (airline.routes.some((r) => r.id === id)) {
      throw new Error(`\uC774\uBBF8 ${a}-${b} \uAD6C\uAC04\uC5D0 ${AC.model} \uB178\uC120\uC774 \uC788\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4. \uD3B8\uC218\uB97C \uB298\uB9AC\uB824\uBA74 "\uB178\uC120 \uC218\uC815"\uC5D0\uC11C \uAE30\uC874 \uB178\uC120\uC744 \uBC14\uAFB8\uC138\uC694.`);
    }
    const resolvedCabinMix = routeType === "cargo" ? { economy: 0, business: 0, first: 0 } : cabinMix ?? defaultCabinMix(AC);
    const resolvedMealTierId = routeType === "cargo" ? "none" : mealTierId ?? recommendMealTier(estimateFlightHours(AC, distanceKm2));
    const route = {
      id,
      a,
      b,
      aircraftId,
      flightsPerWeekOneWay,
      fareMultiplier,
      distanceKm: distanceKm2,
      cabinMix: resolvedCabinMix,
      mealTierId: resolvedMealTierId,
      routeType,
      cargoFareMultiplier,
      serviceChainId,
      serviceChainOrder,
      serviceChainHomeCountry,
      technicalStopOnly,
      fifthFreedomGranted,
      cabotageGranted,
      trafficRight,
      trafficFreedom,
      localOdMultiplier,
      continuingInternationalAllowed,
      throughServiceOnly,
      legalSourceStatus, legalEngineVersion, officialFlightTimeMin, resortClusterId, representedResortCount, contractRateUsdOneWay, operationMode, supportMissionId
    };
    if (assignedTails && assignedTails.length > 0) route.assignedTails = [...assignedTails];
    airline.routes.push(route);
    return route;
  }
  function updateRoute(airline, routeId, patch, { aircraftById: aircraftById2, currentYear } = {}) {
    const route = airline.routes.find((r) => r.id === routeId);
    if (!route) throw new Error(`\uB178\uC120\uC744 \uCC3E\uC744 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC74C: ${routeId}`);
    const touchesCapacity = patch.flightsPerWeekOneWay !== void 0 || patch.aircraftId !== void 0;
    if (touchesCapacity && aircraftById2) {
      const newAircraftId = patch.aircraftId ?? route.aircraftId;
      const newFreq = patch.flightsPerWeekOneWay ?? route.flightsPerWeekOneWay;

          totalCapacity += weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft) * conditionUtilizationMultiplier(age);
        }

  function usedHoursByTail(airline, tailNumber, aircraftById2, excludeRouteId = null) {
    return airline.routes.filter((r) => r.id !== excludeRouteId && (r.assignedTails ?? []).includes(tailNumber)).reduce((sum, r) => {

    }, 0);
  }
  function assignTailsToRoute(airline, aircraftById2, routeId, tailNumbers, currentYear) {
    const route = airline.routes.find((r) => r.id === routeId);
    if (!route) throw new Error(`\uB178\uC120\uC744 \uCC3E\uC744 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC74C: ${routeId}`);
    const aircraft = aircraftById2[route.aircraftId];
    if (!aircraft) throw new Error(`\uC54C \uC218 \uC5C6\uB294 \uAE30\uC885: ${route.aircraftId}`);
    for (const tn of tailNumbers) {
      const entry = airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tn);
      if (!entry) throw new Error(`\uBCF4\uC720\uD558\uC9C0 \uC54A\uC740 \uAE30\uCCB4: ${tn}`);
      if (entry.aircraftId !== route.aircraftId) throw new Error(`${tn}\uC740(\uB294) ${aircraft.model} \uAE30\uC885\uC774 \uC544\uB2D8`);
    }
    const requiredHours = weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, route.distanceKm, route.flightsPerWeekOneWay, route.officialFlightTimeMin);
    let totalCapacity = 0;
    let totalUsedElsewhere = 0;
    for (const tn of tailNumbers) {
      const entry = airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tn);
      const age = fleetEntryAge(entry, currentYear);
      totalCapacity += weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft) * conditionUtilizationMultiplier(age);
      totalUsedElsewhere += usedHoursByTail(airline, tn, aircraftById2, routeId);
    }
    if (totalUsedElsewhere + requiredHours > totalCapacity) {
      throw new Error(
        `\uBC30\uC815 \uAE30\uCCB4 ${tailNumbers.length}\uB300\uC758 \uAC00\uB3D9\uC2DC\uAC04 \uBD80\uC871: \uC774 \uB178\uC120 \uD3EC\uD568 \uC8FC ${(totalUsedElsewhere + requiredHours).toFixed(0)}\uC2DC\uAC04 \uD544\uC694, \uBC30\uC815 \uAE30\uCCB4\uB85C\uB294 \uC8FC ${totalCapacity.toFixed(0)}\uC2DC\uAC04\uAE4C\uC9C0 \uAC00\uB2A5`
      );
    }
    route.assignedTails = [...tailNumbers];
    return route;
  }

  function setCabinMix(airline, routeId, cabinMix) {

  }
  function removeRoute(airline, routeId, gameState2 = null) {

    }
  }
  function requestRoute(airline, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2, gameState2, opts) {

  function processPendingRoutes(gameState2, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2) {
    gameState2.pendingRoutes ?? (gameState2.pendingRoutes = []);
    const stillPending = [];
    const activated = [];
    const failed = [];
    for (const pr of gameState2.pendingRoutes) {
      pr.turnsRemaining -= 1;
      if (pr.turnsRemaining > 0) {
        stillPending.push(pr);
        continue;
      }
      const airline = gameState2.airlines.find((a) => a.id === pr.airlineId);
      if (!airline) continue;
      try {
        const route = addRoute(airline, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2, pr.opts);
        activated.push({ airlineId: pr.airlineId, route });
      } catch (err) {
        failed.push({ airlineId: pr.airlineId, opts: pr.opts, reason: err.message });
      }
    }
    gameState2.pendingRoutes = stillPending;
    return { activated, failed };
  }
  function countCompetitors(gameState2, selfAirlineId, a, b, routeType = "passenger") {
    const key = pairKey3(a, b);
    const self = gameState2.airlines.find((x) => x.id === selfAirlineId);
    const selfSegment = self?.segment ?? "full_service";
    const serviceScore = self ? getCapabilityScore(self, "service") : 50;
    let count = 0;
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines) {
      if (airline.id === selfAirlineId) continue;
      if (!airline.routes.some((r) => pairKey3(r.a, r.b) === key && ((r.routeType ?? "passenger") === routeType))) continue;
      let weight = 1;
      if (selfSegment === "full_service" || selfSegment === "hybrid") {
        if (airline.segment === "lcc") weight = Math.max(0.55, 0.86 - serviceScore * 0.0032);
        else if (airline.segment === "commuter" || airline.segment === "regional") weight = 0.82;
      } else if (selfSegment === "lcc" && (airline.segment === "full_service" || airline.segment === "hybrid")) {
        weight = 1.08;
      }
      count += weight;
    }
    return count;
  }
  function processClosedAirports(gameState2, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2) {
    const isClosed = (iata) => {
      const airport = airportsByIata2[iata];
      return Boolean(airport?.closed_year && gameState2.year >= airport.closed_year);
    };
    const successorOf = (iata) => {
      const airport = airportsByIata2[iata];
      const normalizeName = (value) => String(value ?? "").normalize("NFKD").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
      const replacementName = normalizeName(airport?.replacement_airport);
      const namedSuccessor = replacementName ? Object.values(airportsByIata2).find((candidate) => normalizeName(candidate.name) === replacementName) : null;
      const successorIata = airport?.successor_iata ?? airport?.replacement_iata ?? namedSuccessor?.iata ?? null;
      const successor = successorIata ? airportsByIata2[successorIata] : null;
      if (!successor) return null;
      if (successor.opened_year && gameState2.year < successor.opened_year) return null;
      if (successor.closed_year && gameState2.year >= successor.closed_year) return null;
      return successor;
    };
    const closedRoutes = [];
    const migratedRoutes = [];
    const relocatedAirlines = [];
    const policyInvalidRoutes = [];
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines) {
      const template = airlineTemplateById[airline.id];
      const timelineHeadquarters = template ? resolveHeadquarters(template, gameState2.year) : null;
      const timelineAirport = timelineHeadquarters ? airportsByIata2[timelineHeadquarters] : null;

        airline.headquarters = timelineHeadquarters;

      } else if (isClosed(airline.headquarters)) {

          airline.headquarters = successor.iata;
          relocatedAirlines.push({ airlineId: airline.id, airline: airline.name, from: oldHeadquarters, to: successor.iata, reason: "closure" });
        }

      airline.focusCities = (airline.focusCities ?? []).map((iata) => isClosed(iata) ? (successorOf(iata)?.iata ?? iata) : iata).filter((iata, index, list) => !isClosed(iata) && list.indexOf(iata) === index);

              continue;
            } catch {
            }
          }
          closedRoutes.push({ airlineId: airline.id, routeId: route.id, a: route.a, b: route.b, closedAirport: closedIata });

        }

  function scaleFareMultiplier(fareMultiplier, factor) {
    if (typeof fareMultiplier === "number" || fareMultiplier == null) {
      return (fareMultiplier ?? 1) * factor;
    }
    return {
      economy: (fareMultiplier.economy ?? 1) * factor,
      business: (fareMultiplier.business ?? 1) * factor,
      first: (fareMultiplier.first ?? 1) * factor
    };
  }
  function serviceClassFareMultiplierV227(airline, fareMultiplier) {
    const score = getCapabilityScore(airline, "service");
    const stanceFactor = serviceFareToleranceMultiplier(airline);
    const base = typeof fareMultiplier === "number" || fareMultiplier == null
      ? { economy: fareMultiplier ?? 1, business: fareMultiplier ?? 1, first: fareMultiplier ?? 1 }
      : { economy: fareMultiplier.economy ?? 1, business: fareMultiplier.business ?? 1, first: fareMultiplier.first ?? 1 };
    const quality = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (score - 50) / 50));
    const premiumEligible = airline.segment === "full_service" || airline.segment === "hybrid";
    const economyFactor = premiumEligible ? (1 + quality * 0.025) : stanceFactor;
    const businessFactor = premiumEligible ? stanceFactor * (1 + quality * 0.16) : stanceFactor;
    const firstFactor = premiumEligible ? stanceFactor * (1 + quality * 0.28) : stanceFactor;
    const businessBaseFactor = premiumEligible ? (3.10 / 2.85) : 1;
    const firstBaseFactor = premiumEligible ? (6.50 / 5.75) : 1;
    return {
      economy: base.economy * economyFactor,
      business: base.business * businessBaseFactor * businessFactor,
      first: base.first * firstBaseFactor * firstFactor
    };
  }

  // src/core/acquisitions.js
  var FLEET_ANNUAL_DEPRECIATION_RATE = 0.06;
  var FLEET_MIN_VALUE_RATIO = 0.15;
  var ROUTE_NETWORK_EARNINGS_MULTIPLE = 5;
  var FLEET_LIQUIDATION_DISCOUNT = 0.7;
  var PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID = "__public_float__";
  function computeAirlineValuation(airline, gameState2, { aircraftById: aircraftById2 }) {
    const fleetValue = airline.fleet.reduce((sum, entry) => {
      if (entry.ownership === "leased") return sum;
      const ac = aircraftById2[entry.aircraftId];
      if (!ac) return sum;
      const age = fleetEntryAge(entry, gameState2.year);
      const depreciated = ac.price_musd * 1e6 * Math.max(FLEET_MIN_VALUE_RATIO, Math.pow(1 - FLEET_ANNUAL_DEPRECIATION_RATE, age));
      return sum + depreciated;
    }, 0);
    const last = airline.history?.[airline.history.length - 1];
    const quarterlyProfit = last ? last.profit : 0;
    const annualProfit = quarterlyProfit * 4;
    const routeNetworkValue = Math.max(0, annualProfit * ROUTE_NETWORK_EARNINGS_MULTIPLE);
    const raw = airline.cash + fleetValue * FLEET_LIQUIDATION_DISCOUNT + routeNetworkValue;
    return Math.max(0, raw);
  }
  function stakeCost(airline, percentPoints, gameState2, context2) {
    const valuation = computeAirlineValuation(airline, gameState2, context2);
    return valuation * (percentPoints / 100);
  }
  function materializeImplicitSubsidiaryOwnership(gameState2, targetAirline) {
    var _a, _b;
    if (!targetAirline?.parentAirlineId) return;
    gameState2.stakes ?? (gameState2.stakes = {});
    const register = gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id];
    if (register && Object.keys(register).length > 0) return;
    gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id] = { [targetAirline.parentAirlineId]: 100 };
    gameState2.stakeCostBasis ?? (gameState2.stakeCostBasis = {});
    (_a = gameState2.stakeCostBasis)[_b = targetAirline.id] ?? (_a[_b] = {});
  }
  function getStake(gameState2, targetAirlineId, acquirerId) {
    if (acquirerId === targetAirlineId) {
      const others = gameState2.stakes?.[targetAirlineId] ?? {};
      const soldToOthers = Object.entries(others).filter(([holderId]) => holderId !== targetAirlineId).reduce((sum, [, percent]) => sum + percent, 0);
      return Math.max(0, 100 - soldToOthers);
    }
    const register = gameState2.stakes?.[targetAirlineId];
    const explicitStake = register?.[acquirerId];
    if (explicitStake != null) return explicitStake;
    const target = gameState2.airlines?.find((a) => a.id === targetAirlineId);
    if (target?.parentAirlineId === acquirerId && (!register || Object.keys(register).length === 0)) return 100;
    return 0;
  }
  function buyStake(acquirer, targetAirline, percentPoints, gameState2, context2) {
    var _a, _b, _c, _d;
    if (targetAirline.id === acquirer.id) {
      throw new Error("\uC790\uAE30 \uC790\uC2E0\uC758 \uC9C0\uBD84\uC740 \uC0B4 \uC218 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    }
    materializeImplicitSubsidiaryOwnership(gameState2, targetAirline);
    const currentAcquirerStake = getStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, acquirer.id);
    const legalDecision = evaluateStakePurchase(acquirer, targetAirline, percentPoints, gameState2);
    if (!legalDecision.ok) throw new Error(legalDecision.reason);
    if (currentAcquirerStake >= 100) {
      throw new Error("\uC774\uBBF8 \uC9C0\uBD84\uC744 \uC804\uBD80 \uBCF4\uC720\uD558\uACE0 \uC788\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    }
    const sellablePool = 100 - currentAcquirerStake;
    const actualPercent = Math.min(percentPoints, sellablePool, legalDecision.maxPurchasable);
    const totalCost = stakeCost(targetAirline, actualPercent, gameState2, context2);
    if (acquirer.cash < totalCost) {
      throw new Error(`\uC790\uAE08 \uBD80\uC871: \uC9C0\uBD84 ${actualPercent.toFixed(1)}% \uB9E4\uC785\uC5D0 $${Math.round(totalCost).toLocaleString()} \uD544\uC694, \uBCF4\uC720 $${Math.round(acquirer.cash).toLocaleString()}`);
    }
    acquirer.cash -= totalCost;
    if (sellablePool > 0 && actualPercent > 0) {
      const pricePerPoint = totalCost / actualPercent;
      const selfStake = getStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, targetAirline.id);
      if (selfStake > 0) {
        const contribution = actualPercent * (selfStake / sellablePool);
        targetAirline.cash += contribution * pricePerPoint;
      }
      const externalHolders = Object.entries(gameState2.stakes?.[targetAirline.id] ?? {}).filter(
        ([holderId, percent]) => percent > 0 && holderId !== acquirer.id
      );
      for (const [holderId, holderStake] of externalHolders) {
        const contribution = actualPercent * (holderStake / sellablePool);
        if (contribution <= 0) continue;
        const holder = gameState2.airlines.find((a) => a.id === holderId);
        if (holder) holder.cash += contribution * pricePerPoint;
        gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][holderId] = holderStake - contribution;
      }
    }
    gameState2.stakes ?? (gameState2.stakes = {});
    (_a = gameState2.stakes)[_b = targetAirline.id] ?? (_a[_b] = {});
    gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][acquirer.id] = currentAcquirerStake + actualPercent;
    gameState2.stakeCostBasis ?? (gameState2.stakeCostBasis = {});
    (_c = gameState2.stakeCostBasis)[_d = targetAirline.id] ?? (_c[_d] = {});
    gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetAirline.id][acquirer.id] = (gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetAirline.id][acquirer.id] ?? 0) + totalCost;
    gameState2.legalDecisionLog ?? (gameState2.legalDecisionLog = []);
    gameState2.legalDecisionLog.push({
      type: "stake_purchase_legal_review",
      year: gameState2.year,
      quarter: gameState2.quarter,
      acquirerId: acquirer.id,
      targetId: targetAirline.id,
      requestedPercent: percentPoints,
      approvedPercent: actualPercent,
      capped: legalDecision.capped,
      ruleSourceStatus: legalDecision.rule?.sourceStatus ?? legalDecision.status?.sourceStatus
    });
    return { cost: totalCost, newStake: gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][acquirer.id], legalDecision };
  }
  function getStakeCostBasis(gameState2, targetAirlineId, acquirerId) {
    return gameState2.stakeCostBasis?.[targetAirlineId]?.[acquirerId] ?? 0;
  }
  function transferStakePortfolio(gameState2, oldHolderId, newHolderId) {
    var _a;
    const transferred = [];
    if (!oldHolderId || !newHolderId || oldHolderId === newHolderId) return transferred;
    gameState2.stakes ?? (gameState2.stakes = {});
    gameState2.stakeCostBasis ?? (gameState2.stakeCostBasis = {});
    for (const [targetId, holders] of Object.entries(gameState2.stakes)) {
      if (targetId === oldHolderId) continue;
      const percent = holders?.[oldHolderId] ?? 0;
      if (!(percent > 0)) continue;
      holders[newHolderId] = (holders[newHolderId] ?? 0) + percent;
      delete holders[oldHolderId];
      const oldBasis = gameState2.stakeCostBasis?.[targetId]?.[oldHolderId] ?? 0;
      if (oldBasis > 0) {
        (_a = gameState2.stakeCostBasis)[targetId] ?? (_a[targetId] = {});
        gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetId][newHolderId] = (gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetId][newHolderId] ?? 0) + oldBasis;
        delete gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetId][oldHolderId];
      }
      transferred.push({ targetAirlineId: targetId, percent, costBasis: oldBasis });
    }
    return transferred;
  }
  function clearTargetShareRegister(gameState2, targetAirlineId) {
    const extinguishedShareholders = Object.entries(gameState2.stakes?.[targetAirlineId] ?? {}).filter(([, percent]) => percent > 0).map(([holderAirlineId, percent]) => ({ holderAirlineId, percent }));
    if (gameState2.stakes) delete gameState2.stakes[targetAirlineId];
    if (gameState2.stakeCostBasis) delete gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetAirlineId];
    if (gameState2.stakeDividendLog) delete gameState2.stakeDividendLog[targetAirlineId];
    return extinguishedShareholders;
  }
  function removeAirlineFromOwnershipRegistry(gameState2, airlineId, { successorId = "" } = {}) {
    const transferredPortfolio = successorId ? transferStakePortfolio(gameState2, airlineId, successorId) : [];
    const forfeitedPortfolio = [];
    if (!successorId) {
      for (const [targetId, holders] of Object.entries(gameState2.stakes ?? {})) {
        if (targetId === airlineId) continue;
        const percent = holders?.[airlineId] ?? 0;
        if (!(percent > 0)) continue;
        const costBasis = gameState2.stakeCostBasis?.[targetId]?.[airlineId] ?? 0;
        forfeitedPortfolio.push({ targetAirlineId: targetId, percent, costBasis });
        delete holders[airlineId];
        if (gameState2.stakeCostBasis?.[targetId]) {
          delete gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetId][airlineId];
        }
      }
    }
    const extinguishedShareholders = clearTargetShareRegister(gameState2, airlineId);
    for (const holderMap of Object.values(gameState2.stakeDividendLog ?? {})) {
      if (holderMap && typeof holderMap === "object") delete holderMap[airlineId];
    }
    return { transferredPortfolio, forfeitedPortfolio, extinguishedShareholders };
  }
  function sellStake(seller, targetAirline, percentPoints, gameState2, context2) {
    materializeImplicitSubsidiaryOwnership(gameState2, targetAirline);
    const currentStake = getStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, seller.id);
    if (currentStake <= 0) {
      throw new Error("\uBCF4\uC720\uD55C \uC9C0\uBD84\uC774 \uC5C6\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    }
    if (percentPoints <= 0) {
      throw new Error("\uB9E4\uAC01\uD560 \uC9C0\uBD84\uC728\uC740 0\uBCF4\uB2E4 \uCEE4\uC57C \uD569\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    }
    const actualPercent = Math.min(percentPoints, currentStake);
    const proceeds = stakeCost(targetAirline, actualPercent, gameState2, context2);
    seller.cash += proceeds;
    const remainingStake = currentStake - actualPercent;
    if (remainingStake > 0) {
      gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][seller.id] = remainingStake;
    } else {
      delete gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][seller.id];
    }
    if (targetAirline.parentAirlineId) {
      gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID] = (gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID] ?? 0) + actualPercent;
      if (targetAirline.parentAirlineId === seller.id && remainingStake < 50) {
        targetAirline.parentAirlineId = "";
      }
    }
    const basis = getStakeCostBasis(gameState2, targetAirline.id, seller.id);
    const soldRatio = currentStake > 0 ? actualPercent / currentStake : 0;
    if (gameState2.stakeCostBasis?.[targetAirline.id]?.[seller.id] != null) {
      if (remainingStake > 0) {
        gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetAirline.id][seller.id] = basis * (1 - soldRatio);
      } else {
        delete gameState2.stakeCostBasis[targetAirline.id][seller.id];
      }
    }
    return { proceeds, remainingStake };
  }
  var STAKE_DIVIDEND_RATIO = 0.20;
  var STAKE_DIVIDEND_FREQUENCY = "annual_q4";
  function annualStakeDividendBaseV700AB(target, statement) {
    if (!statement || Number(statement.quarter) !== 4) return { eligible:false, reason:"not_q4", year:Number(statement?.year)||0, rows:[], profit:0 };
    const year = Number(statement.year) || 0;
    const byQuarter = new Map();
    for (const row of target?.history ?? []) {
      if (Number(row?.year) !== year) continue;
      const quarter = Number(row?.quarter);
      if (quarter < 1 || quarter > 4) continue;
      byQuarter.set(quarter, row);
    }
    const rows = [1,2,3,4].map(q=>byQuarter.get(q)).filter(Boolean);
    if (rows.length !== 4) return { eligible:false, reason:"incomplete_year", year, rows, profit:0 };
    const profit = rows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+(Number(row?.preDividendProfit ?? row?.profit)||0),0);
    return { eligible:profit>0, reason:profit>0?"annual_profit":"annual_loss_or_zero", year, rows, profit };
  }
  function stakeDividendReserveV700AB(target) {
    try { return Math.max(0, Number(subsidiaryReserveTargetV253(target)) || 0); }
    catch { return Math.max(10e6, Math.max(0, Number(target?.ai?.cashReserve)||0)*0.25); }
  }
  function distributeStakeDividends(gameState2) {
    var _a, _b;
    gameState2.stakeDividendLog = {};
    const plans = [];
    for (const target of gameState2.airlines ?? []) {
      const statement = target.history?.[target.history.length - 1];
      const annual = annualStakeDividendBaseV700AB(target, statement);
      if (!annual.eligible) {
        if (statement) {
          statement.annualDividendPolicyV700AB = STAKE_DIVIDEND_FREQUENCY;
          statement.annualDividendBaseV700AB = annual.profit;
          statement.annualDividendReasonV700AB = annual.reason;
        }
        continue;
      }
      const explicitHolders = { ...gameState2.stakes?.[target.id] ?? {} };
      if (target.parentAirlineId && explicitHolders[target.parentAirlineId] == null) {
        explicitHolders[target.parentAirlineId] = 100;
      }
      const validHolders = Object.entries(explicitHolders).filter(([, percent]) => percent > 0).filter(([holderId]) => holderId === PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID || gameState2.airlines.some((a) => a.id === holderId));
      if (validHolders.length === 0) continue;
      const reserve = stakeDividendReserveV700AB(target);
      const cashAvailable = Math.max(0, (Number(target.cash)||0) - reserve);
      const requestedPool = annual.profit * STAKE_DIVIDEND_RATIO;
      const pool = Math.max(0, Math.min(requestedPool, cashAvailable));
      statement.annualDividendPolicyV700AB = STAKE_DIVIDEND_FREQUENCY;
      statement.annualDividendBaseV700AB = annual.profit;
      statement.annualDividendRequestedV700AB = requestedPool;
      statement.annualDividendReserveV700AB = reserve;
      statement.annualDividendCashAvailableV700AB = cashAvailable;
      statement.annualDividendReasonV700AB = pool>0?"paid_or_allocated":"reserve_limited";
      if (!(pool > 0)) continue;
      plans.push({ target, statement, pool, holders: validHolders });
    }
    for (const { target, statement, pool, holders } of plans) {
      let totalPaid = 0;
      for (const [holderId, percent] of holders) {
        const dividend = pool * (percent / 100);
        if (!(dividend > 0)) continue;
        target.cash -= dividend;
        totalPaid += dividend;
        (_a = gameState2.stakeDividendLog)[_b = target.id] ?? (_a[_b] = {});
        gameState2.stakeDividendLog[target.id][holderId] = dividend;
        if (holderId === PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID) {
          statement.publicShareholderDividendPaid = (statement.publicShareholderDividendPaid ?? 0) + dividend;
          continue;
        }
        const holder = gameState2.airlines.find((a) => a.id === holderId);
        if (!holder) continue;
        holder.cash += dividend;
        const holderStatement = holder.history?.[holder.history.length - 1];
        if (holderStatement) {
          holderStatement.dividendIncome = (holderStatement.dividendIncome ?? 0) + dividend;
          if (target.parentAirlineId === holderId) {
            holderStatement.subsidiaryDividendIncome = (holderStatement.subsidiaryDividendIncome ?? 0) + dividend;
          } else {
            holderStatement.strategicStakeDividendIncome = (holderStatement.strategicStakeDividendIncome ?? 0) + dividend;
          }
        }
        if (target.parentAirlineId === holderId) {
          statement.dividendPaidToParent = (statement.dividendPaidToParent ?? 0) + dividend;
        }
      }
      statement.dividendsPaid = (statement.dividendsPaid ?? 0) + totalPaid;
      statement.annualDividendActuallyPaidV700AB = totalPaid;
    }
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines ?? []) {
      const statement = airline.history?.[airline.history.length - 1];
      if (!statement) continue;
      const base = statement.preDividendProfit ?? statement.profit ?? 0;
      statement.dividendIncome ?? (statement.dividendIncome = 0);
      statement.subsidiaryDividendIncome ?? (statement.subsidiaryDividendIncome = 0);
      statement.strategicStakeDividendIncome ?? (statement.strategicStakeDividendIncome = 0);
      statement.dividendsPaid ?? (statement.dividendsPaid = 0);
      statement.profit = base - statement.dividendsPaid + statement.dividendIncome;
      statement.cashAfter = airline.cash;
    }
  }

  function executeMergerAcquisition(acquirer, targetAirline, gameState2) {

    }

    );

  }
  function executeSubsidiaryAcquisition(acquirer, targetAirline, gameState2) {
    var _a, _b;

    if (!controlDecision.ok) throw new Error(controlDecision.reason);
    if (!hasMajorityStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, acquirer.id)) {
      throw new Error("\uACFC\uBC18 \uC9C0\uBD84\uC744 \uD655\uBCF4\uD574\uC57C \uC790\uD68C\uC0AC\uB85C \uD3B8\uC785\uD560 \uC218 \uC788\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.");
    }
    targetAirline.parentAirlineId = acquirer.id;
    const controllingStake = getStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, acquirer.id);
    const residualSelfStake = getStake(gameState2, targetAirline.id, targetAirline.id);
    if (residualSelfStake > 0) {
      gameState2.stakes ?? (gameState2.stakes = {});
      (_a = gameState2.stakes)[_b = targetAirline.id] ?? (_a[_b] = {});
      gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID] = (gameState2.stakes[targetAirline.id][PUBLIC_FLOAT_HOLDER_ID] ?? 0) + residualSelfStake;

    return { controllingStake, minorityShareholders, freeFloatPercent };
  }
  function ultimateParentOf(airline, gameState2) {
    let current = airline;
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([airline?.id]);
    while (current?.parentAirlineId) {
      const parent = gameState2.airlines?.find((a) => a.id === current.parentAirlineId);
      if (!parent || seen.has(parent.id)) break;
      seen.add(parent.id);
      current = parent;
    }
    return current;
  }
  function groupDescendantsOf(parentAirline, gameState2) {
    const rows = [];
    const walk = (parentId, depth) => {
      const children = (gameState2.airlines ?? []).filter((a) => a.parentAirlineId === parentId).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
      for (const child of children) {
        rows.push({ airline: child, depth, parentId, controllingStake: getStake(gameState2, child.id, parentId) });
        walk(child.id, depth + 1);
      }
    };
    walk(parentAirline.id, 1);
    return rows;
  }
  function buildAirlineGroups(gameState2) {
    const groups = [];
    for (const root of (gameState2.airlines ?? []).filter((a) => !a.parentAirlineId)) {
      const descendants = groupDescendantsOf(root, gameState2);
      if (descendants.length === 0) continue;
      const members = [root, ...descendants.map((d) => d.airline)];
      const consolidatedProfit = members.reduce((sum, a) => {
        const st = a.history?.[a.history.length - 1];
        return sum + (st?.preDividendProfit ?? st?.profit ?? 0);
      }, 0);
      groups.push({
        root,
        descendants,
        members,
        memberCount: members.length,
        totalCash: members.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.cash ?? 0), 0),
        totalFleet: members.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.fleet?.length ?? 0), 0),
        totalRoutes: members.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.routes?.length ?? 0), 0),
        consolidatedProfit
      });
    }
    return groups.sort(
      (a, b) => b.totalFleet - a.totalFleet || b.totalRoutes - a.totalRoutes || b.totalCash - a.totalCash
    );
  }

  var STATE_CACHE = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakMap();

  function safe(value) {

  }
  function cacheFor(gameState2) {
    const stamp = `${gameState2.turnNumber ?? 0}|${gameState2.year}|${gameState2.quarter}|${gameState2.airlines?.length ?? 0}`;
    let entry = STATE_CACHE.get(gameState2);
    if (!entry || entry.stamp !== stamp) {
      entry = { stamp, baseline: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map(), candidate: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map() };
      STATE_CACHE.set(gameState2, entry);
    }
    return entry;
  }
  function temporaryState(gameState2, originalAirline, replacementAirline) {
    return {
      ...gameState2,
      airlines: (gameState2.airlines ?? []).map((airline) => airline.id === originalAirline.id ? replacementAirline : airline)
    };
  }
  function baselineNetwork(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    const cache = cacheFor(gameState2);
    if (cache.baseline.has(airline.id)) {
      updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, { planningCacheHits: 1 });
      return cache.baseline.get(airline.id);
    }
    updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, { planningCacheMisses: 1, exactNetworkEvaluations: 1 });
    const value = computeNetworkConnectingDemand(airline, gameState2, {
      airportsByIata: context2.airportsByIata,
      aircraftById: context2.aircraftById
    });
    cache.baseline.set(airline.id, value);
    return value;
  }
  function simulateCandidateRoute(candidate, airline, gameState2, context2, selfDemand, allianceDemand) {
    return simulateRouteOperation({
      airportA: candidate.origin,
      airportB: candidate.dest,
      distanceKm: candidate.distanceKm,
      aircraft: candidate.aircraft,
      flightsPerWeekOneWay: candidate.freq,

      year: gameState2.year,
      cabinMix: candidate.cabinMix,
      mealTierId: candidate.mealTierId,
      tourismLevelA: gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[candidate.origin.iata] ?? 0,
      tourismLevelB: gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[candidate.dest.iata] ?? 0,
      gameState: gameState2,
      airportsByIata: context2.airportsByIata,
      selfConnectingDemand: selfDemand,
      allianceConnectingDemand: allianceDemand,
      localOdMultiplier: candidate.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
      overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
    });
  }
  function incrementalExistingRouteContribution(airline, baseline, expanded, mode) {
    const lastStatement = airline.history?.at?.(-1);
    const reports = new Map((lastStatement?.routes ?? []).map((report) => [report.routeId, report]));
    const before = mode === "self" ? baseline.selfBonus : baseline.allianceBonus;
    const after = mode === "self" ? expanded.selfBonus : expanded.allianceBonus;
    let total = 0;
    for (const route of airline.routes ?? []) {
      const deltaWeeklyPax = Math.max(0, safe(after?.[route.id]) - safe(before?.[route.id]));
      if (deltaWeeklyPax <= 0) continue;
      const report = reports.get(route.id);
      const fare = Math.max(20, safe(report?.farePerPax));
      total += deltaWeeklyPax * fare * 0.45;
    }
    return total;
  }
  function scoreExpansionCandidates(airline, candidates, gameState2, context2, {
    exactLimit = EXACT_CANDIDATE_LIMIT,
    allianceWeight = 0.6
  } = {}) {
    if (!candidates.length) return [];
    updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, { candidateEvaluations: candidates.length });
    const baseline = baselineNetwork(airline, gameState2, context2);
    const chosen = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const add = (candidate) => {
      const key = routeKey(candidate);
      if (seen.has(key) || chosen.length >= exactLimit) return;
      seen.add(key);
      chosen.push(candidate);
    };
    [...candidates].sort((a, b) => b.estProfit - a.estProfit).slice(0, exactLimit).forEach(add);
    [...candidates].filter((c) => c.isDomestic).sort((a, b) => b.estProfit - a.estProfit).slice(0, 6).forEach(add);
    const origins = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const candidate of [...candidates].sort((a, b) => b.estProfit - a.estProfit)) {
      if (!origins.has(candidate.origin.iata)) origins.set(candidate.origin.iata, []);
      origins.get(candidate.origin.iata).push(candidate);
    }
    for (const bucket of origins.values()) bucket.slice(0, 2).forEach(add);
    const scoreByKey = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const candidate of chosen.slice(0, exactLimit)) {
      const key = `${airline.id}|${routeKey(candidate)}`;
      const cache = cacheFor(gameState2);
      if (cache.candidate.has(key)) {
        updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, { planningCacheHits: 1 });
        scoreByKey.set(routeKey(candidate), cache.candidate.get(key));
        continue;
      }
      updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, { planningCacheMisses: 1, exactNetworkEvaluations: 1 });
      const candidateRoute = {
        id: `__network_candidate__${routeKey(candidate)}`,
        a: candidate.origin.iata,
        b: candidate.dest.iata,
        aircraftId: candidate.aircraft.id,
        flightsPerWeekOneWay: candidate.freq,
        fareMultiplier: 1,
        cabinMix: candidate.cabinMix,
        mealTierId: candidate.mealTierId,
        routeType: "passenger",
        distanceKm: candidate.distanceKm,
        trafficRight: candidate.trafficRight,
        trafficFreedom: candidate.trafficFreedom,
        localOdMultiplier: candidate.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
        continuingInternationalAllowed: candidate.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? false,
        throughServiceOnly: candidate.throughServiceOnly ?? false,
        legalEngineVersion: LEGAL_ENGINE_VERSION
      };
      const expandedAirline = { ...airline, routes: [...airline.routes ?? [], candidateRoute] };
      const expandedState = temporaryState(gameState2, airline, expandedAirline);
      const expanded = computeNetworkConnectingDemand(expandedAirline, expandedState, {
        airportsByIata: context2.airportsByIata,
        aircraftById: context2.aircraftById
      });
      const selfDemand = expanded.selfByType?.[candidateRoute.id] ?? null;
      const allianceDemand = expanded.allianceByType?.[candidateRoute.id] ?? null;
      const direct = safe(candidate.estProfit);
      const selfSimulation = simulateCandidateRoute(candidate, airline, gameState2, context2, selfDemand, null);
      const allSimulation = simulateCandidateRoute(candidate, airline, gameState2, context2, selfDemand, allianceDemand);
      const selfOnCandidate = Math.max(0, safe(selfSimulation.weeklyProfit) - direct);
      const allianceOnCandidate = Math.max(0, safe(allSimulation.weeklyProfit) - safe(selfSimulation.weeklyProfit));
      const downstreamSelf = incrementalExistingRouteContribution(airline, baseline, expanded, "self");
      const downstreamAlliance = incrementalExistingRouteContribution(airline, baseline, expanded, "alliance");
      const selfContribution = selfOnCandidate + downstreamSelf;
      const allianceContribution = allianceOnCandidate + downstreamAlliance;
      const networkScore = direct + selfContribution + allianceWeight * allianceContribution;
      const value = {
        directProfit: direct,
        selfConnectionContribution: selfContribution,
        allianceConnectionContribution: allianceContribution,
        networkContribution: networkScore,
        candidateSelfPax: safe(expanded.selfBonus?.[candidateRoute.id]),
        candidateAlliancePax: safe(expanded.allianceBonus?.[candidateRoute.id])
      };
      cache.candidate.set(key, value);
      scoreByKey.set(routeKey(candidate), value);
    }
    return candidates.map((candidate) => {
      const score = scoreByKey.get(routeKey(candidate));
      return score ? { ...candidate, ...score, estProfit: score.networkContribution } : {
        ...candidate,
        directProfit: candidate.estProfit,
        selfConnectionContribution: 0,
        allianceConnectionContribution: 0,
        networkContribution: candidate.estProfit
      };
    });
  }

  function estimatedAcquisitionWeeklyCashCost(plan, aircraft, units) {
    if (!plan || units <= 0) return 0;
    if (plan.mode === "lease") return (aircraft.lease_musd_month ?? 0) * 1e6 * 12 / 52 * units;
    if (plan.mode === "loan_buy" && (plan.loanAmount ?? 0) > 0) {
      return quarterlyPayment(plan.loanAmount, DEFAULT_ANNUAL_RATE_PERCENT, DEFAULT_TERM_YEARS) * 4 / 52;
    }
    return 0;
  }
  function recentOperatingCashFlow(airline, quarters = 4) {
    const recent = (airline.history ?? []).slice(-quarters);

  }
  function expansionMeetsReturnThreshold({ weeklyRevenue, weeklyContribution, acquisitionWeeklyCashCost = 0, cashInvestment = 0 }) {
    const netWeekly = weeklyContribution - acquisitionWeeklyCashCost;
    const minimumWeekly = Math.max(5e3, weeklyRevenue * 0.02);
    if (netWeekly < minimumWeekly) return false;
    if (cashInvestment > 0 && netWeekly * 52 / cashInvestment < 0.04) return false;
    return true;
  }
  var PERSONALITIES = {
    aggressive: { expansionChance: 0.8, cashReserve: 35e6, maxRoutesPerTurn: 2, acquisitionChance: 0.15 },
    balanced: { expansionChance: 0.5, cashReserve: 105e6, maxRoutesPerTurn: 1, acquisitionChance: 0.06 },

  var LCC_LONG_HAUL_CAPABLE = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["tway_air", "scoot", "airasia", "jetblue", "norwegian", "french_bee", "norse_atlantic", "condor"]);
