  function decideAcquisitionPlan(airline, aircraft, unitsNeeded, profile, aircraftById2, gameState2) {
    if (unitsNeeded === 0) return { mode: "none", netCashRequired: 0 };
    if (!canBuyNewAircraft(aircraft, gameState2.year)) {
      return { mode: "lease", netCashRequired: 0 };
    }
    if (unitsNeeded >= 5) {
      const goodwill = getManufacturerGoodwill(airline, aircraft.manufacturer);
      const discount = Math.min(0.35, goodwillDiscount(goodwill) + bulkOrderDiscount(unitsNeeded));
      const cost = aircraft.price_musd * 1e6 * (1 - discount) * unitsNeeded;
      return { mode: "bulk", netCashRequired: cost };
    }
    const buyCost = aircraft.price_musd * 1e6 * unitsNeeded;
    if (airline.cash - buyCost < profile.cashReserve * 1.5) {
      if (profile.personality !== "conservative") {
        const shortfall = buyCost - (airline.cash - profile.cashReserve);
        const loanCapacity = availableLoanCapacity(airline, aircraftById2, gameState2.year);
        if (shortfall > 0 && shortfall <= loanCapacity) {
          return { mode: "loan_buy", netCashRequired: buyCost - shortfall, loanAmount: shortfall };
        }
      }
      return { mode: "lease", netCashRequired: 0 };
    }
    return { mode: "buy", netCashRequired: buyCost };
  }
  function servedPairs(airline, gameState2) {
    const pairs = new Set(airline.routes.map((r) => [r.a, r.b].sort().join("-")));
    for (const pr of gameState2?.pendingRoutes ?? []) {
      if (pr.airlineId !== airline.id) continue;
      pairs.add([pr.opts.a, pr.opts.b].sort().join("-"));
    }
    for (const order of gameState2?.pendingAircraftOrders ?? []) {
      if (order.airlineId !== airline.id) continue;
      for (const plan of order.plannedRoutes ?? []) pairs.add([plan.a, plan.b].sort().join("-"));
    }
    return pairs;
  }
  function aiServicePreference(airline, aircraft, distanceKm2) {
    if (airline.segment === "lcc" || airline.segment === "commuter") {
      const flightHours = estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2);
      return {
        cabinMix: { economy: 1, business: 0, first: 0 },
        mealTierId: flightHours > 4 ? "snack" : "none"
        // 초장거리에서도 유상 스낵 수준까지만
      };
    }
    return { cabinMix: defaultCabinMix(aircraft), mealTierId: recommendMealTier(estimateFlightHours(aircraft, distanceKm2)) };
  }
  function pickBestAircraftForDistance(aircraftList, airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year, budgetUsd, airlineCountry, gameState2, maxSeats = Infinity) {
    const candidates = aircraftList.filter(
      (ac) => !isFreighterAircraftV600(ac) && isAircraftAvailable(ac, year) && canOperateRoute(ac, airportA, airportB, distanceKm2, year) && ac.price_musd * 1e6 <= budgetUsd && ac.seats <= maxSeats && canPurchaseAircraft(airlineCountry, ac.country, year, gameState2)
    );
    if (candidates.length === 0) return null;
    candidates.sort((a, b) => b.seats - a.seats);
    return candidates[0];
  }
  function aiAdjustFares(airline) {
    const last = airline.history[airline.history.length - 1];
    if (!last) return;
    for (const routeReport of last.routes) {
      const route = airline.routes.find((r) => r.id === routeReport.routeId);
      if (!route) continue;
      const isLcc = airline.segment === "lcc";
      const scaleBy = (factor) => {
        const floor = isLcc ? 0.72 : 0.8;
        const ceiling = isLcc ? 1.02 : 2;
        if (typeof route.fareMultiplier === "number" || route.fareMultiplier == null) {
          return Math.max(floor, Math.min(ceiling, (route.fareMultiplier ?? 1) * factor));
        }
        const clamp = (v) => Math.max(floor, Math.min(ceiling, v * factor));
        return {
          economy: clamp(route.fareMultiplier.economy ?? 1),
          business: clamp(route.fareMultiplier.business ?? 1),
          first: clamp(route.fareMultiplier.first ?? 1)
        };
      };
      if (isLcc) {
        // LCCs defend volume: high loads trigger capacity growth elsewhere, not yield harvesting.
        if (routeReport.loadFactor > 0.88) {
          route.fareMultiplier = scaleBy(0.995);
        } else if (routeReport.loadFactor < 0.72) {
          route.fareMultiplier = scaleBy(0.965);
        } else if (typeof route.fareMultiplier === "number" && route.fareMultiplier > 1) {
          route.fareMultiplier = scaleBy(0.99);
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (routeReport.loadFactor > 0.9) route.fareMultiplier = scaleBy(1.05);
      else if (routeReport.loadFactor < 0.45) route.fareMultiplier = scaleBy((routeReport.profit ?? 0) < 0 ? 1.03 : 0.97);
    }
  }
  function aiDestinationPool(airline, origin, gameState2, airports, airportsByIata2, served) {
    const eligible = [];
    for (const dest of airports) {
      if (dest.iata === origin.iata) continue;
      if (dest.opened_year && gameState2.year < dest.opened_year) continue;
      if (dest.closed_year && gameState2.year >= dest.closed_year) continue;
      const key = [origin.iata, dest.iata].sort().join("-");
      if (served.has(key)) continue;
      const distanceKm2 = distanceBetweenAirports(origin, dest);
      if (airline.segment === "lcc" && !LCC_LONG_HAUL_CAPABLE.has(airline.id) && !airline.longHaulCapable && distanceKm2 > 5e3) continue;
      if (airline.segment === "commuter" && distanceKm2 > 1e3) continue;
      const legalDecision = resolveRouteTrafficRights(airline, origin, dest, gameState2, {});
      if (!legalDecision.allowed) continue;
      if (getRelationScore(origin.country, dest.country, gameState2.year, gameState2) < 15) continue;
      const demand = estimateWeeklyDemandBreakdown(
        origin,
        dest,
        distanceKm2,
        gameState2.year,
        gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[origin.iata] ?? 0,
        gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[dest.iata] ?? 0,
        gameState2,
        { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }
      ).total;
      eligible.push({ dest, distanceKm: distanceKm2, demand, legalDecision });
    }
    const picked = [];
    const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const add = (entry) => {
      if (!entry || seen.has(entry.dest.iata) || picked.length >= 45) return;
      seen.add(entry.dest.iata);
      picked.push(entry);
    };
    [...eligible].sort((a, b) => b.demand - a.demand).slice(0, 20).forEach(add);
    [...eligible].filter((entry) => entry.dest.country === airline.country).sort((a, b) => b.demand - a.demand).slice(0, 10).forEach(add);
    [...eligible].sort(
      (a, b) => (b.dest.hub_tier ?? 0) - (a.dest.hub_tier ?? 0) || transferConvenienceIndex(b.dest, gameState2.year) - transferConvenienceIndex(a.dest, gameState2.year) || b.demand - a.demand
    ).slice(0, 10).forEach(add);
    if (airline.alliance) {
      const allianceHubs = new Set(
        (gameState2.airlines ?? []).filter((other) => other.id !== airline.id && other.alliance === airline.alliance).flatMap((other) => [other.headquarters, ...other.focusCities ?? []])
      );
      [...eligible].filter((entry) => allianceHubs.has(entry.dest.iata)).sort((a, b) => b.demand - a.demand).slice(0, 5).forEach(add);
    }
    return picked;
  }
  function aiConsiderExpansion(airline, gameState2, { airports, airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, aircraftById: aircraftById2 }, { forceReentry = false, recoveryModeV2210 = false } = {}) {
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (!profile) return [];
    const existingPendingOrders = (gameState2.pendingAircraftOrders ?? []).filter((order) => order.airlineId === airline.id).length;
    const trulyDormantReentry = ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) === 0 && (airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) === 0);
    const activeRecoveryV2210 = recoveryModeV2210 && (airline.routes?.length ?? 0) <= 3 && (airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) <= 4;
    const dormantReentry = forceReentry || activeRecoveryV2210 || trulyDormantReentry;
    if (!dormantReentry && existingPendingOrders >= 2) return [];
    if (!dormantReentry && randomUnitV253() > profile.expansionChance) return [];
    const reentryReserve = Math.min(profile.cashReserve, Math.max(15e6, Math.max(0, airline.cash) * 0.25));
    const effectiveReserve = dormantReentry ? reentryReserve : profile.cashReserve;
    const effectiveProfile = dormantReentry ? { ...profile, cashReserve: effectiveReserve, maxRoutesPerTurn: 1 } : profile;
    if (airline.cash < effectiveReserve) {
      if (dormantReentry) airline._reentryFailureQuarters = (airline._reentryFailureQuarters ?? 0) + 1;
      return [];
    }
    const hq = airportsByIata2[airline.headquarters];
    if (!hq) {
      if (dormantReentry) airline._reentryFailureQuarters = (airline._reentryFailureQuarters ?? 0) + 1;
      return [];
    }
    const served = servedPairs(airline, gameState2);
    const aircraftList = Object.values(aircraftById2);
    const budget = Math.max(0, airline.cash - effectiveReserve);
    const origins = [hq];
    if (!dormantReentry) for (const iata of airline.focusCities ?? []) {
      if (iata === hq.iata) continue;
      const fc = airportsByIata2[iata];
      if (fc) origins.push(fc);
    }
    if (!dormantReentry && (airline.segment === "lcc" || airline.segment === "commuter")) {
      const servedAirportIatas = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      for (const r of airline.routes) {
        servedAirportIatas.add(r.a);
        servedAirportIatas.add(r.b);
      }
      servedAirportIatas.delete(hq.iata);
      for (const o of origins) servedAirportIatas.delete(o.iata);
      const otherBases = [...servedAirportIatas].map((iata) => airportsByIata2[iata]).filter(Boolean);
      const sampledBases = otherBases.sort(() => randomUnitV253() - 0.5).slice(0, 4);
      origins.push(...sampledBases);
    }
    let candidates = [];
    const FREQ = dormantReentry ? 3 : 7;
    for (const origin of origins) {
      const destinationPool = aiDestinationPool(airline, origin, gameState2, airports, airportsByIata2, served);
      for (const entry of destinationPool) {
        const dest = entry.dest;
        const distanceKm2 = entry.distanceKm;
        if (airline.segment === "lcc" && !LCC_LONG_HAUL_CAPABLE.has(airline.id) && !airline.longHaulCapable && distanceKm2 > 5e3) continue;
        if (airline.segment === "commuter" && distanceKm2 > 1e3) continue;
        const relation = getRelationScore(origin.country, dest.country, gameState2.year, gameState2);
        if (relation < 15) continue;
        const maxSeatsForSegment = airline.segment === "commuter" ? 80 : dormantReentry ? airline.segment === "regional" ? 120 : airline.segment === "lcc" ? 190 : 220 : Infinity;
        // alpha7.0-j: an OD's competitor count is invariant across aircraft/frequency
        // alternatives. Compute it once and reuse the winning evaluation instead of
        // repeating the same route simulation after selection.
        const competitorCount = countCompetitors(gameState2, airline.id, origin.iata, dest.iata);
        const selectionV226 = window.selectExpansionAircraftFrequencyV226({
          airline,
          origin,
          dest,
          distanceKm: distanceKm2,
          demand: entry.demand,
          dormantReentry,
          maxSeats: maxSeatsForSegment,
          aircraftList,
          aircraftById: aircraftById2,
          gameState: gameState2,
          budget,
          reserve: effectiveReserve,
          profile: effectiveProfile,
          localOdMultiplier: entry.legalDecision?.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          competitorCount
        });
        let aircraft = selectionV226?.aircraft ?? null;
        const routeFrequencyV226 = selectionV226?.frequency ?? (dormantReentry ? 3 : 7);
        if (!aircraft) continue;
        const requiredHours = selectionV226.requiredHours;
        const extraUnitsNeeded = selectionV226.unitsNeeded;
        const acquisitionPlan = selectionV226.acquisitionPlan;
        if (airline.cash - acquisitionPlan.netCashRequired < effectiveReserve) continue;
        const { cabinMix, mealTierId } = selectionV226.service;
        const est = selectionV226.est;
        const acquisitionWeeklyCashCost = estimatedAcquisitionWeeklyCashCost(acquisitionPlan, aircraft, extraUnitsNeeded);
        const estimatedFleetWeeklyCostV226 = selectionV226?.fleetWeeklyCost ?? window.estimateCandidateFleetWeeklyCostV226(airline, aircraft, requiredHours, extraUnitsNeeded, acquisitionPlan, gameState2, aircraftById2);
        const estimatedSharedWeeklyCostV226 = selectionV226?.sharedWeeklyCost ?? window.estimateSharedCorporateWeeklyCostV226(airline, est.weeklyRevenue);
        const estimatedFullCostWeeklyV226 = est.weeklyProfit - estimatedFleetWeeklyCostV226 - estimatedSharedWeeklyCostV226;
        const maximumDirectNetworkLoss = Math.min(25e3, est.weeklyRevenue * 0.05);
        const reentryMinimumProfit = recoveryModeV2210
          ? Math.max(250, est.weeklyRevenue * 0.001)
          : Math.max(2500, est.weeklyRevenue * 0.01);
        const recoveryIncrementalWeeklyV2210 = est.weeklyProfit - estimatedFleetWeeklyCostV226;
        const candidateAccepted = recoveryModeV2210
          ? recoveryIncrementalWeeklyV2210 >= reentryMinimumProfit
          : dormantReentry
            ? estimatedFullCostWeeklyV226 >= reentryMinimumProfit
            : estimatedFullCostWeeklyV226 >= -maximumDirectNetworkLoss;
        if (candidateAccepted) {
          candidates.push({
            origin,
            dest,
            aircraft,
            distanceKm: distanceKm2,
            estProfit: est.weeklyProfit,
            extraUnitsNeeded,
            acquisitionPlan,
            freq: routeFrequencyV226,
            competitorCount,
            weeklyRevenue: est.weeklyRevenue,
            acquisitionWeeklyCashCost,
            estimatedFleetWeeklyCostV226,
            estimatedSharedWeeklyCostV226,
            estimatedFullCostWeeklyV226,
            cashInvestment: acquisitionPlan.netCashRequired ?? 0,
            // "국내선"은 항공사 자국(원래 "국내선을 안 만든다"는 문제의식은 자국 피더망
            // 얘기였음) 기준이어야 함 — 그냥 출발/도착국이 같기만 하면 되는 걸로 잡으면,
            // LCC가 어쩌다 외국에 거점을 얻었을 때 그 나라 국내선만 계속 불려나가는 부작용이
            // 생김(실제로 발견 — 라이언에어가 캐나다에 거점을 얻자 캐나다 국내선을 눈덩이처럼
            // 계속 우선 배정받음). 자국-자국 노선일 때만 "국내선"으로 침.
            isDomestic: origin.country === airline.country && dest.country === airline.country,
            cabinMix,
            mealTierId,
            trafficRight: entry.legalDecision?.trafficRight,
            trafficFreedom: entry.legalDecision?.freedom,
            localOdMultiplier: entry.legalDecision?.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
            continuingInternationalAllowed: entry.legalDecision?.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? false,
            throughServiceOnly: entry.legalDecision?.throughServiceOnly ?? false
          });
        }
      }
    }
    candidates = scoreExpansionCandidates(airline, candidates, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, aircraftById: aircraftById2 }, {
      exactLimit: 5,
      allianceWeight: 0.6
    }).map((candidate) => ({
      ...candidate,
      netNetworkContribution: candidate.networkContribution - (candidate.estimatedFleetWeeklyCostV226 ?? candidate.acquisitionWeeklyCashCost ?? 0) - (candidate.estimatedSharedWeeklyCostV226 ?? 0)
    })).filter((candidate) => {
      if (!dormantReentry) return expansionMeetsReturnThreshold({
        weeklyRevenue: candidate.weeklyRevenue ?? 0,
        weeklyContribution: candidate.netNetworkContribution,
        acquisitionWeeklyCashCost: 0,
        cashInvestment: candidate.cashInvestment ?? 0
      });
      const netWeekly = recoveryModeV2210
        ? (candidate.networkContribution ?? 0) - (candidate.estimatedFleetWeeklyCostV226 ?? candidate.acquisitionWeeklyCashCost ?? 0)
        : (candidate.netNetworkContribution ?? 0);
      const minimumWeekly = recoveryModeV2210
        ? Math.max(250, (candidate.weeklyRevenue ?? 0) * 0.001)
        : Math.max(2500, (candidate.weeklyRevenue ?? 0) * 0.01);
      if (netWeekly < minimumWeekly) return false;
      const failedQuarters = airline._reentryFailureQuarters ?? 0;
      if (!recoveryModeV2210 && failedQuarters < 4 && (candidate.cashInvestment ?? 0) > 0 && netWeekly * 52 / candidate.cashInvestment < 0.025) return false;
      return true;
    });
    candidates.sort((a, b) => b.netNetworkContribution - a.netNetworkContribution);
    const domesticCandidates = candidates.filter((c) => c.isDomestic);
    const domesticSlots = domesticCandidates.length >= 2 ? effectiveProfile.maxRoutesPerTurn >= 2 ? Math.ceil(effectiveProfile.maxRoutesPerTurn / 2) : 1 : 0;
    const domesticPicked = domesticCandidates.slice(0, domesticSlots);
    const remainingByOrigin = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    for (const c of candidates) {
      if (domesticPicked.includes(c)) continue;
      if (!remainingByOrigin.has(c.origin.iata)) remainingByOrigin.set(c.origin.iata, []);
      remainingByOrigin.get(c.origin.iata).push(c);
    }
    airline._focusCityRotation = (airline._focusCityRotation ?? 0) + 1;
    const rotation = airline._focusCityRotation % Math.max(1, origins.length);
    const rotatedOrigins = [...origins.slice(rotation), ...origins.slice(0, rotation)];
    const originOrder = rotatedOrigins.map((o) => o.iata).filter((iata) => remainingByOrigin.has(iata));
    const roundRobinPicked = [];
    let stillHasCandidates = true;
    while (stillHasCandidates) {
      stillHasCandidates = false;
      for (const iata of originOrder) {
        const bucket = remainingByOrigin.get(iata);
        if (bucket.length === 0) continue;
        roundRobinPicked.push(bucket.shift());
        stillHasCandidates = stillHasCandidates || bucket.length > 0;
      }
    }
    const priorityOrdered = [...domesticPicked, ...roundRobinPicked];
    const opened = [];
    const maxAttempts = Math.max(20, effectiveProfile.maxRoutesPerTurn * 10);
    let attempts = 0;
    for (const c of priorityOrdered) {
      if (opened.length >= effectiveProfile.maxRoutesPerTurn) break;
      if (attempts >= maxAttempts) break;
      attempts++;
      const fleetLenBefore = airline.fleet.length;
      const cashBefore = airline.cash;
      const loansLenBefore = (airline.loans ?? []).length;
      const orderLenBefore = (gameState2.pendingAircraftOrders ?? []).length;
      try {
        const routePlan = {
          a: c.origin.iata,
          b: c.dest.iata,
          aircraftId: c.aircraft.id,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: c.freq,
          cabinMix: c.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: c.mealTierId
        };
        if (c.extraUnitsNeeded > 0 && c.acquisitionPlan.mode !== "lease") {
          const order = placeNewAircraftOrder(airline, c.aircraft, c.extraUnitsNeeded, {
            gameState: gameState2,
            tailPrefix: airline.iataCode ?? airline.id,
            plannedRoutes: [routePlan],
            aiOrder: true,
            plannedLoanAmount: c.acquisitionPlan.mode === "loan_buy" ? (c.acquisitionPlan.loanAmount ?? 0) : 0
          });
          opened.push({ id: `planned:${order.id}`, pendingDelivery: true, turns: order.turnsRemaining });
        } else {
          if (c.extraUnitsNeeded > 0 && c.acquisitionPlan.mode === "lease") {
            for (let i = 0; i < c.extraUnitsNeeded; i++) leaseAircraftFromMarket(airline, c.aircraft, gameState2, { emergency: dormantReentry });
          }
          const result = requestRoute(airline, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2, gameState2, routePlan);
          opened.push(result.status === "active" ? result.route : { id: result.id, pending: true, turns: result.turns });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        airline.fleet.length = fleetLenBefore;
        airline.cash = cashBefore;
        if (airline.loans) airline.loans.length = loansLenBefore;
        if (gameState2.pendingAircraftOrders) gameState2.pendingAircraftOrders.length = orderLenBefore;
        continue;
      }
    }
    if (dormantReentry) {
      if (opened.length > 0) {
        airline._reentryFailureQuarters = 0;
        airline._dormantSinceTurn = null;
        airline.operatingStatus = "active";
        gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
        gameState2.eventLog.push({
          type: recoveryModeV2210 ? "ai_post_transition_recovery_v2210" : "ai_dormant_reentry",
          year: gameState2.year,
          quarter: gameState2.quarter,
          airlineId: airline.id,
          airline: airline.name,
          openedRoutes: opened.map((item) => item.id),
          label: recoveryModeV2210
            ? `🌱 ${airline.name}, 모델전환 후 회복을 위해 신규노선 ${opened.length}개 확장`
            : `🌱 ${airline.name}, 휴면 상태에서 기재 도입 및 신규노선 재진입`
        });
      } else {
        airline._reentryFailureQuarters = (airline._reentryFailureQuarters ?? 0) + 1;
        if (trulyDormantReentry) {
          airline._dormantSinceTurn ??= gameState2.turnNumber ?? 0;
          airline.operatingStatus = "dormant";
        } else {
          airline.operatingStatus = "active";
        }
      }
    }
    return opened;
  }
  function aiConsiderTwoLegExpansion(airline, gameState2, { airports, airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, aircraftById: aircraftById2 }) {
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (!profile) return [];
    if (airline.segment !== "full_service") return [];
    if (airline.cash < profile.cashReserve * 3) return [];
    if ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) < 2 || (airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) < 2) return [];
    if ((airline._lastTwoLegPlanYear ?? -Infinity) > gameState2.year - 5) return [];
    if ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) > 8) return [];
    const plansThisYear = (gameState2.eventLog ?? []).filter(
      (event) => event.type === "ai_two_leg_network_opening" && event.year === gameState2.year
    ).length;
    if (plansThisYear >= 4) return [];
    const origin = airportsByIata2[airline.headquarters];
    if (!origin) return [];
    const aircraftList = Object.values(aircraftById2);
    const budget = airline.cash - profile.cashReserve;
    const served = servedPairs(airline, gameState2);
    const FREQ = 3;
    const isActive = (airport) => (!airport.opened_year || gameState2.year >= airport.opened_year) && (!airport.closed_year || gameState2.year < airport.closed_year) && airportRoleAtYear(airport, gameState2.year) !== "inactive";
    const targets = airports.filter((airport) => isActive(airport) && airport.iata !== origin.iata).filter((airport) => airport.country !== origin.country && (airport.hub_tier ?? 0) >= 4).map((target) => {
      const distanceKm2 = distanceBetweenAirports(origin, target);
      const directAircraft = pickBestAircraftForDistance(
        aircraftList,
        origin,
        target,
        distanceKm2,
        gameState2.year,
        budget,
        airline.country,
        gameState2
      );
      if (directAircraft) return null;
      const demand = estimateWeeklyDemandBreakdown(
        origin,
        target,
        distanceKm2,
        gameState2.year,
        gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[origin.iata] ?? 0,
        gameState2.cityTourismLevel?.[target.iata] ?? 0,
        gameState2,
        { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }
      ).total;
      return { target, distanceKm: distanceKm2, demand };
    }).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => b.demand - a.demand).slice(0, 10);
    if (!targets.length) return [];
    const transferHubs = airports.filter((airport) => isActive(airport) && airport.iata !== origin.iata).filter((airport) => ["transfer_hub", "primary_gateway"].includes(airportRoleAtYear(airport, gameState2.year))).filter((airport) => transferConvenienceIndex(airport, gameState2.year) >= 0.65).sort(
      (a, b) => transferConvenienceIndex(b, gameState2.year) - transferConvenienceIndex(a, gameState2.year) || (b.hub_tier ?? 0) - (a.hub_tier ?? 0)
    ).slice(0, 18);
    let best = null;
    const MAX_EXACT_TWO_LEG_EVALUATIONS = 3;
    let exactTwoLegEvaluations = 0;
    for (const targetInfo of targets) {
      const target = targetInfo.target;
      for (const hub of transferHubs) {
        if (hub.iata === target.iata) continue;
        if (metroId(origin) === metroId(hub) || metroId(hub) === metroId(target) || metroId(origin) === metroId(target)) continue;
        const leg1Distance = distanceBetweenAirports(origin, hub);
        const leg2Distance = distanceBetweenAirports(hub, target);
        if (leg1Distance < 300 || leg2Distance < 300) continue;
        const detourRatio = (leg1Distance + leg2Distance) / Math.max(1, targetInfo.distanceKm);
        if (detourRatio > 2) continue;
        if (getRelationScore(origin.country, hub.country, gameState2.year, gameState2) < 15) continue;
        if (getRelationScore(hub.country, target.country, gameState2.year, gameState2) < 15) continue;
        const serviceChainId = `chain:${airline.id}:${origin.iata}-${hub.iata}-${target.iata}:${gameState2.year}`;
        const leg1Legal = resolveRouteTrafficRights(airline, origin, hub, gameState2, {
          serviceChainId,
          serviceChainOrder: 1,
          serviceChainHomeCountry: airline.aocCountry ?? airline.country
        });
        const leg2Legal = resolveRouteTrafficRights(airline, hub, target, gameState2, {
          serviceChainId,
          serviceChainOrder: 2,
          serviceChainHomeCountry: airline.aocCountry ?? airline.country
        });
        if (!leg1Legal.allowed || !leg2Legal.allowed) continue;
        if (served.has([origin.iata, hub.iata].sort().join("-"))) continue;
        if (served.has([hub.iata, target.iata].sort().join("-"))) continue;
        const ac1 = pickBestAircraftForDistance(
          aircraftList,
          origin,
          hub,
          leg1Distance,
          gameState2.year,
          budget,
          airline.country,
          gameState2
        );
        const ac2 = pickBestAircraftForDistance(
          aircraftList,
          hub,
          target,
          leg2Distance,
          gameState2.year,
          budget,
          airline.country,
          gameState2
        );
        if (!ac1 || !ac2) continue;
        if (exactTwoLegEvaluations >= MAX_EXACT_TWO_LEG_EVALUATIONS) continue;
        exactTwoLegEvaluations += 1;
        const service1 = aiServicePreference(airline, ac1, leg1Distance);
        const service2 = aiServicePreference(airline, ac2, leg2Distance);
        const competitor1 = countCompetitors(gameState2, airline.id, origin.iata, hub.iata);
        const competitor2 = countCompetitors(gameState2, airline.id, hub.iata, target.iata);
        const base1 = simulateRouteOperation({
          airportA: origin,
          airportB: hub,
          distanceKm: leg1Distance,
          aircraft: ac1,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
          competitorCount: competitor1,
          year: gameState2.year,
          cabinMix: service1.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: service1.mealTierId,
          gameState: gameState2,
          airportsByIata: airportsByIata2,
          localOdMultiplier: leg1Legal.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
        });
        const base2 = simulateRouteOperation({
          airportA: hub,
          airportB: target,
          distanceKm: leg2Distance,
          aircraft: ac2,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
          competitorCount: competitor2,
          year: gameState2.year,
          cabinMix: service2.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: service2.mealTierId,
          gameState: gameState2,
          airportsByIata: airportsByIata2,
          localOdMultiplier: leg2Legal.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
        });
        const route1 = {
          id: `__twoleg1__${origin.iata}-${hub.iata}-${target.iata}`,
          a: origin.iata,
          b: hub.iata,
          aircraftId: ac1.id,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
          routeType: "passenger",
          distanceKm: leg1Distance,
          cabinMix: service1.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: service1.mealTierId,
          fareMultiplier: 1,
          serviceChainId,
          serviceChainOrder: 1,
          serviceChainHomeCountry: airline.aocCountry ?? airline.country,
          trafficRight: leg1Legal.trafficRight,
          trafficFreedom: leg1Legal.freedom,
          localOdMultiplier: leg1Legal.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          continuingInternationalAllowed: leg1Legal.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? true,
          throughServiceOnly: leg1Legal.throughServiceOnly ?? false
        };
        const route2 = {
          id: `__twoleg2__${origin.iata}-${hub.iata}-${target.iata}`,
          a: hub.iata,
          b: target.iata,
          aircraftId: ac2.id,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
          routeType: "passenger",
          distanceKm: leg2Distance,
          cabinMix: service2.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: service2.mealTierId,
          fareMultiplier: 1,
          serviceChainId,
          serviceChainOrder: 2,
          serviceChainHomeCountry: airline.aocCountry ?? airline.country,
          trafficRight: leg2Legal.trafficRight,
          trafficFreedom: leg2Legal.freedom,
          localOdMultiplier: leg2Legal.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          continuingInternationalAllowed: leg2Legal.continuingInternationalAllowed ?? true,
          throughServiceOnly: leg2Legal.throughServiceOnly ?? false
        };
        const expandedAirline = { ...airline, routes: [...airline.routes, route1, route2] };
        const expandedState = {
          ...gameState2,
          airlines: gameState2.airlines.map((item) => item.id === airline.id ? expandedAirline : item)
        };
        const network = computeNetworkConnectingDemand(expandedAirline, expandedState, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, aircraftById: aircraftById2 });
        const net1 = simulateRouteOperation({
          airportA: origin,
          airportB: hub,
          distanceKm: leg1Distance,
          aircraft: ac1,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
          competitorCount: competitor1,
          year: gameState2.year,
          cabinMix: service1.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: service1.mealTierId,
          gameState: gameState2,
          airportsByIata: airportsByIata2,
          selfConnectingDemand: network.selfByType?.[route1.id],
          allianceConnectingDemand: network.allianceByType?.[route1.id],
          localOdMultiplier: leg1Legal.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
        });
        const net2 = simulateRouteOperation({
          airportA: hub,
          airportB: target,
          distanceKm: leg2Distance,
          aircraft: ac2,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
          competitorCount: competitor2,
          year: gameState2.year,
          cabinMix: service2.cabinMix,
          mealTierId: service2.mealTierId,
          gameState: gameState2,
          airportsByIata: airportsByIata2,
          selfConnectingDemand: network.selfByType?.[route2.id],
          allianceConnectingDemand: network.allianceByType?.[route2.id],
          localOdMultiplier: leg2Legal.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
        });
        const directProfit = base1.weeklyProfit + base2.weeklyProfit;
        const networkProfit = net1.weeklyProfit + net2.weeklyProfit;
        const connectionContribution = networkProfit - directProfit;
        const weakLegLoss = Math.abs(Math.min(0, base1.weeklyProfit)) + Math.abs(Math.min(0, base2.weeklyProfit));
        if (!(networkProfit > 0) || weakLegLoss <= 0) continue;
        if (connectionContribution < Math.max(weakLegLoss * 1.3, networkProfit * 0.15)) continue;
        const requirements = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
        for (const [aircraft, distance] of [[ac1, leg1Distance], [ac2, leg2Distance]]) {
          requirements.set(aircraft.id, (requirements.get(aircraft.id) ?? 0) + weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, distance, FREQ));
        }
        const acquisitions = [];
        let totalCashRequired = 0;
        for (const [aircraftId, hours] of requirements) {
          const aircraft = aircraftById2[aircraftId];
          const idle = idleHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2, gameState2.year);
          const units = idle >= hours ? 0 : Math.ceil((hours - idle) / weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft));
          const plan = decideAcquisitionPlan(airline, aircraft, units, profile, aircraftById2, gameState2);
          totalCashRequired += plan.netCashRequired;
          acquisitions.push({ aircraft, units, plan });
        }
        if (airline.cash - totalCashRequired < profile.cashReserve) continue;
        const acquisitionWeeklyCashCost = acquisitions.reduce(
          (sum, item) => sum + estimatedAcquisitionWeeklyCashCost(item.plan, item.aircraft, item.units),
          0
        );
        const combinedWeeklyRevenue = net1.weeklyRevenue + net2.weeklyRevenue;
        if (!expansionMeetsReturnThreshold({
          weeklyRevenue: combinedWeeklyRevenue,
          weeklyContribution: networkProfit,
          acquisitionWeeklyCashCost,
          cashInvestment: totalCashRequired
        })) continue;
        const candidate = {
          origin,
          hub,
          target,
          ac1,
          ac2,
          leg1Distance,
          leg2Distance,
          service1,
          service2,
          acquisitions,
          acquisitionWeeklyCashCost,
          netNetworkProfit: networkProfit - acquisitionWeeklyCashCost,
          directProfit,
          connectionContribution,
          networkProfit,
          detourRatio,
          leg1DirectProfit: base1.weeklyProfit,
          leg2DirectProfit: base2.weeklyProfit
        };
        if (!best || candidate.netNetworkProfit > best.netNetworkProfit) best = candidate;
      }
    }
    if (!best) return [];
    const fleetBefore = airline.fleet.length;
    const routesBefore = airline.routes.length;
    const cashBefore = airline.cash;
    const loansBefore = (airline.loans ?? []).length;
    const pendingBefore = structuredClone(gameState2.pendingRoutes ?? []);
    const slotRequestsBefore = structuredClone(gameState2.slotRequests ?? []);
    const slotsBefore = structuredClone(gameState2.slots ?? {});
    try {
      for (const acquisition of best.acquisitions) {
        const { aircraft, units, plan } = acquisition;
        if (plan.mode === "bulk") {
          buyAircraftBulk(airline, aircraft, units, { year: gameState2.year, tailPrefix: airline.id, gameState: gameState2, acquisitionIntentV253:{unitsNeeded:units,reason:"two_leg_network"} });
        } else if (plan.mode === "loan_buy") {
          takeLoan(airline, plan.loanAmount, { year: gameState2.year, aircraftById: aircraftById2 });
          for (let i = 0; i < units; i++) buyAircraft(airline, aircraft, {
            tailNumber: `${airline.id}-${airline.fleet.length + 1}`,
            year: gameState2.year,
            gameState: gameState2,
            acquisitionIntentV253:{unitsNeeded:units,reason:"two_leg_network"}
          });
        } else {
          const ownership = plan.mode === "lease" ? "leased" : "owned";
          for (let i = 0; i < units; i++) buyAircraft(airline, aircraft, {
            tailNumber: `${airline.id}-${airline.fleet.length + 1}${ownership === "leased" ? "L" : ""}`,
            year: gameState2.year,
            ownership,
            gameState: gameState2,
            acquisitionIntentV253:{unitsNeeded:units,reason:"two_leg_network"}
          });
        }
      }
      const first = requestRoute(airline, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2, gameState2, {
        a: best.origin.iata,
        b: best.hub.iata,
        aircraftId: best.ac1.id,
        flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
        cabinMix: best.service1.cabinMix,
        mealTierId: best.service1.mealTierId,
        serviceChainId: `chain:${airline.id}:${best.origin.iata}-${best.hub.iata}-${best.target.iata}:${gameState2.year}`,
        serviceChainOrder: 1,
        serviceChainHomeCountry: airline.aocCountry ?? airline.country
      });
      const second = requestRoute(airline, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2, gameState2, {
        a: best.hub.iata,
        b: best.target.iata,
        aircraftId: best.ac2.id,
        flightsPerWeekOneWay: FREQ,
        cabinMix: best.service2.cabinMix,
        mealTierId: best.service2.mealTierId,
        serviceChainId: `chain:${airline.id}:${best.origin.iata}-${best.hub.iata}-${best.target.iata}:${gameState2.year}`,
        serviceChainOrder: 2,
        serviceChainHomeCountry: airline.aocCountry ?? airline.country
      });
      airline._lastTwoLegPlanYear = gameState2.year;
      updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2, { simultaneousTwoLegPlansOpened: 1 });
      gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
      gameState2.eventLog.push({
        type: "ai_two_leg_network_opening",
        year: gameState2.year,
        quarter: gameState2.quarter,
        airlineId: airline.id,
        airline: airline.name,
        origin: best.origin.iata,
        hub: best.hub.iata,
        destination: best.target.iata,
        directProfit: best.directProfit,
        connectionContribution: best.connectionContribution,
        networkProfit: best.networkProfit,
        detourRatio: best.detourRatio,
        leg1DirectProfit: best.leg1DirectProfit,
        leg2DirectProfit: best.leg2DirectProfit,
        label: `\u{1F501} ${airline.name}, ${best.origin.iata}-${best.hub.iata}-${best.target.iata} \uACBD\uC720\uB9DD \uB3D9\uC2DC \uAC1C\uC124`
      });
      return [
        first.status === "active" ? first.route : { id: first.id, pending: true, turns: first.turns },
        second.status === "active" ? second.route : { id: second.id, pending: true, turns: second.turns }
      ];
    } catch {
      airline.fleet.length = fleetBefore;
      airline.routes.length = routesBefore;
      airline.cash = cashBefore;
      if (airline.loans) airline.loans.length = loansBefore;
      gameState2.pendingRoutes = pendingBefore;
      gameState2.slotRequests = slotRequestsBefore;
      gameState2.slots = slotsBefore;
      return [];
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderCatering(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, cateringById: cateringById2 }) {
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (!profile || !cateringById2) return;
    if (randomUnitV253() > 0.2) return;
    const hq = airportsByIata2[airline.headquarters];
    if (!hq || hasCateringAt(airline, hq.iata)) return;
    const companies = Object.values(cateringById2);
    if (companies.length === 0) return;
    let pick2;
    if (airline.segment === "lcc" || airline.segment === "commuter") {
      pick2 = companies.reduce((a, b) => a.price_multiplier < b.price_multiplier ? a : b);
    } else {
      const affordable = companies.filter((c) => airline.cash - cateringContractSigningFee(c) >= profile.cashReserve);
      if (affordable.length === 0) return;
      pick2 = affordable.reduce((a, b) => a.quality_tier > b.quality_tier ? a : b);
    }
    try {
      contractCatering(airline, hq, pick2, { year: gameState2.year, gameState: gameState2 });
    } catch {
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderAncillaryInvestment(airline, gameState2, { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2 }) {
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (!profile) return;
    if (randomUnitV253() > profile.expansionChance * 0.3) return;
    const hq = airportsByIata2[airline.headquarters];
    if (!hq) return;
    const universalCandidates = ["grand_hotel", "leisure_land", "travel_agency"];
    const specialtyCandidates = availableFacilityTypesAt(hq).filter((t) => FACILITY_TYPES[t].regional);
    const candidates = [...universalCandidates, ...specialtyCandidates].filter((type2) => !hasFacility(airline, hq.iata, type2));
    if (candidates.length === 0) return;
    const type = candidates[Math.floor(randomUnitV253() * candidates.length)];
    const cost = FACILITY_TYPES[type].cost(hq);
    if (airline.cash - cost < profile.cashReserve * 1.5) return;
    try {
      buildFacility(airline, hq, gameState2, type, { year: gameState2.year, gameState: gameState2 });
    } catch {
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderFleetModernization(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    const aircraftById2 = context2.aircraftById;
    const airportsByIata2 = context2.airportsByIata;
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (!profile) return { replaced: [], closed: [], disposed: [] };
    const replaced = [];
    const closed = [];
    const disposed = [];
    const obsoleteRoutes = [...airline.routes].filter((route) => {
      const aircraft = aircraftById2[route.aircraftId];
      return !aircraft || !isAircraftAvailable(aircraft, gameState2.year);
    });
    for (const route of obsoleteRoutes) {
      const airportA = airportsByIata2[route.a];
      const airportB = airportsByIata2[route.b];
      if (!airportA || !airportB) {
        removeRoute(airline, route.id, gameState2);
        closed.push(route.id);
        continue;
      }
      const availableTypes = Object.values(aircraftById2).filter((aircraft) =>
        aircraftRoleCompatibleV600(aircraft, route.routeType ?? "passenger") &&
        isAircraftAvailable(aircraft, gameState2.year) &&
        canOperateRoute(aircraft, airportA, airportB, route.distanceKm, gameState2.year) &&
        canPurchaseAircraft(airline.country, aircraft.country, gameState2.year, gameState2) &&
        (airline.segment !== "commuter" || aircraft.seats <= 80)
      ).sort((a, b) => {
        const aOwned = airline.fleet.some((entry) => entry.aircraftId === a.id) ? 0 : 1;
        const bOwned = airline.fleet.some((entry) => entry.aircraftId === b.id) ? 0 : 1;
        return aOwned - bOwned || Math.abs(a.seats - (aircraftById2[route.aircraftId]?.seats ?? a.seats)) - Math.abs(b.seats - (aircraftById2[route.aircraftId]?.seats ?? b.seats)) || a.opcost_usd_hr - b.opcost_usd_hr;
      });
      let converted = false;
      for (const replacement of availableTypes.slice(0, 12)) {
        const requiredHours = weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(replacement, route.distanceKm, route.flightsPerWeekOneWay, route.officialFlightTimeMin);
        const idle = idleHoursByType(airline, replacement.id, aircraftById2, gameState2.year);
        const unitsNeeded = idle >= requiredHours ? 0 : Math.ceil((requiredHours - idle) / weeklyUtilizationHours(replacement));
        const plan = decideAcquisitionPlan(airline, replacement, unitsNeeded, profile, aircraftById2, gameState2);
        if (airline.cash - (plan.netCashRequired ?? 0) < -profile.cashReserve) continue;
        const fleetBefore = airline.fleet.length;
        const cashBefore = airline.cash;
        const loansBefore = (airline.loans ?? []).length;
        try {
          if (unitsNeeded > 0) {
            for (let i = 0; i < unitsNeeded; i++) leaseAircraftFromMarket(airline, replacement, gameState2, { emergency: true });
          }
          updateRoute(airline, route.id, { aircraftId: replacement.id }, { aircraftById: aircraftById2, currentYear: gameState2.year });
          replaced.push({ routeId: route.id, from: route.aircraftId, to: replacement.id });
          converted = true;
          break;
        } catch {
          airline.fleet.length = fleetBefore;
          airline.cash = cashBefore;
          if (airline.loans) airline.loans.length = loansBefore;
        }
      }
      if (!converted) {
        removeRoute(airline, route.id, gameState2);
        closed.push(route.id);
      }
    }
    const stillUsedTypes = new Set(airline.routes.map((route) => route.aircraftId));
    for (const entry of [...airline.fleet]) {
      const aircraft = aircraftById2[entry.aircraftId];
      if (aircraft && isAircraftAvailable(aircraft, gameState2.year)) continue;
      if (stillUsedTypes.has(entry.aircraftId)) continue;
      try {
        sellAircraft(airline, entry.tailNumber, aircraft ?? { id: entry.aircraftId, model: entry.aircraftId, price_musd: 0 }, { year: gameState2.year, force: true, gameState: gameState2 });
        disposed.push(entry.tailNumber);
      } catch {
      }
    }
    if (replaced.length || closed.length || disposed.length) {
      gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
      gameState2.eventLog.push({
        type: "ai_fleet_modernization",
        year: gameState2.year,
        quarter: gameState2.quarter,
        airlineId: airline.id,
        airline: airline.name,
        replacedRoutes: replaced.length,
        closedRoutes: closed.length,
        disposedAircraft: disposed.length,
        label: `🔄 ${airline.name}, 퇴역기종 전환: 노선 ${replaced.length}개 기종교체·${closed.length}개 폐지·기재 ${disposed.length}대 처분`
      });
    }
    return { replaced, closed, disposed };
  }
  function aiConsiderFleetRotation(airline, gameState2, aircraftById2) {
    if (randomUnitV253() > 0.15) return;
    const last = airline.history[airline.history.length - 1];
    if (!last || last.routes.length === 0) return;
    const top = [...last.routes].sort((a, b) => b.profit - a.profit)[0];
    const route = airline.routes.find((r) => r.id === top.routeId);
    if (!route) return;
    const sameType = airline.fleet.filter((f) => f.aircraftId === route.aircraftId);
    if (sameType.length === 0) return;
    const acSpec = aircraftById2[route.aircraftId];
    if (!acSpec) return;
    const newestFirst = [...sameType].sort(
      (a, b) => fleetEntryAge(a, gameState2.year) - fleetEntryAge(b, gameState2.year)
    );
    const requiredHours = weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(acSpec, route.distanceKm, route.flightsPerWeekOneWay, route.officialFlightTimeMin);
    const capacityPerAircraft = weeklyUtilizationHours(acSpec);
    const desiredCount = Math.min(newestFirst.length, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(requiredHours / capacityPerAircraft)));
    const chosenTails = newestFirst.slice(0, desiredCount).map((f) => f.tailNumber);
    try {
      assignTailsToRoute(airline, aircraftById2, route.id, chosenTails, gameState2.year);
    } catch {
    }
  }
  var FREQUENCY_STEP = 7;
  var MAX_FREQUENCY = 21;
  function aiConsiderFrequencyIncrease(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    const { airportsByIata: airportsByIata2, aircraftById: aircraftById2 } = context2;
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (recentOperatingCashFlow(airline, 4) < 0) return;
    for (const route of airline.routes) {
      if (route.flightsPerWeekOneWay >= MAX_FREQUENCY) continue;
      const airportA = airportsByIata2[route.a];
      const airportB = airportsByIata2[route.b];
      const aircraft = aircraftById2[route.aircraftId];
      if (!airportA || !airportB || !aircraft) continue;
      const competitorCount = countCompetitors(gameState2, airline.id, route.a, route.b, route.routeType ?? "passenger");
      const baseArgs = {
        airportA,
        airportB,
        distanceKm: route.distanceKm,
        aircraft,
        year: gameState2.year,
        competitorCount,
        cabinMix: route.cabinMix,
        mealTierId: route.mealTierId,
        gameState: gameState2,
        localOdMultiplier: route.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
        overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
      };
      const currentEst = simulateRouteOperation({ ...baseArgs, flightsPerWeekOneWay: route.flightsPerWeekOneWay });
      const nextFreq = route.flightsPerWeekOneWay + FREQUENCY_STEP;
      const nextEst = simulateRouteOperation({ ...baseArgs, flightsPerWeekOneWay: nextFreq });
      const currentProfit = currentEst.weeklyRevenue - currentEst.weeklyOperatingCost;
      const nextProfit = nextEst.weeklyRevenue - nextEst.weeklyOperatingCost;
      const marginalProfit = nextProfit - currentProfit;
      if (nextProfit <= 0 || marginalProfit < Math.max(2500, nextEst.weeklyRevenue * 0.01)) continue;
      const idle = idleHoursByType(airline, route.aircraftId, aircraftById2, gameState2.year);
      const extraHoursNeeded = weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, route.distanceKm, nextFreq, route.officialFlightTimeMin) - weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, route.distanceKm, route.flightsPerWeekOneWay, route.officialFlightTimeMin);
      if (extraHoursNeeded > idle) {
        const price = aircraft.price_musd * 1e6;
        if (airline.cash - price < profile.cashReserve * 1.5) continue;
        if (marginalProfit * 52 / price < 0.04) continue;
        try {
          buyAircraft(airline, aircraft, { tailNumber: `${airline.id}-${airline.fleet.length + 1}`, year: gameState2.year, gameState: gameState2, acquisitionIntentV253:{unitsNeeded:1,reason:"frequency_increase"} });
        } catch {
          continue;
        }
      }
      try {
        updateRoute(airline, route.id, { flightsPerWeekOneWay: nextFreq }, { aircraftById: aircraftById2, currentYear: gameState2.year });
      } catch {
      }
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderHistoricalAllianceMembership(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    if (gameState2.quarter !== 1 || airline.alliance) return;
    const template = context2.airlineTemplateById?.[airline.id];
    const intendedAlliance = template?.alliance;
    if (!intendedAlliance) return;
    const status = allianceJoinStatus(airline, intendedAlliance, gameState2);
    if (!status.ok) return;
    try {
      joinAlliance(airline, intendedAlliance, gameState2);
      gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
      gameState2.eventLog.push({
        type: "ai_alliance_join",
        year: gameState2.year,
        quarter: gameState2.quarter,
        airlineId: airline.id,
        airline: airline.name,
        alliance: intendedAlliance,
        label: `\u{1F310} ${airline.name}, ${intendedAlliance} \uAC00\uC785`
      });
    } catch {
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderAcquisitions(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    if (gameState2.quarter !== 1) return;
    const profile = airline.ai;
    if (randomUnitV253() > (profile.acquisitionChance ?? 0)) return;
    const surplusThreshold = profile.cashReserve * 8;
    if (airline.cash < surplusThreshold) return;
    const availableBudget = airline.cash - profile.cashReserve * 2;
    const myValuation = computeAirlineValuation(airline, gameState2, context2);
    if (myValuation <= 0) return;
    const candidates = gameState2.airlines.filter((a) => {
      if (a.id === airline.id) return false;
      if (a.id === context2.mainPlayerAirlineId) return false;
      if (a.parentAirlineId) return false;
      if (!a.ai) return false;
      const legalReview = evaluateStakePurchase(airline, a, 1, gameState2);
      if (!legalReview.ok) return false;
      const val = computeAirlineValuation(a, gameState2, context2);
      return val > 0 && val < myValuation * 0.6;
    });
    if (candidates.length === 0) return;
    const inProgress = candidates.filter((c) => getStake(gameState2, c.id, airline.id) > 0);
    const target = inProgress.length > 0 ? inProgress[0] : candidates[Math.floor(randomUnitV253() * candidates.length)];
    const currentStake = getStake(gameState2, target.id, airline.id);
    if (currentStake >= 100) return;
    const desiredPercent = Math.min(30, 100 - currentStake);
    const cost = stakeCost(target, desiredPercent, gameState2, context2);
    if (cost > availableBudget) return;
    let purchase;
    try {
      purchase = buyStake(airline, target, desiredPercent, gameState2, context2);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
    gameState2.eventLog.push({
      type: "stake_purchase",
      year: gameState2.year,
      quarter: gameState2.quarter,
      acquirerId: airline.id,
      acquirer: airline.name,
      targetId: target.id,
      target: target.name,
      purchasedPercent: purchase.newStake - currentStake,
      stakeBefore: currentStake,
      stakeAfter: purchase.newStake,
      cost: purchase.cost,
      label: `\u{1F4C8} [\uD56D\uACF5\uC5C5\uACC4 \uC9C0\uBD84\uB9E4\uC785] ${airline.name}, ${target.name} \uC9C0\uBD84 ${(purchase.newStake - currentStake).toFixed(1)}%p \uB9E4\uC785(\uB204\uC801 ${purchase.newStake.toFixed(1)}%)`
    });
    if (!hasMajorityStake(gameState2, target.id, airline.id)) return;
    const sameSegment = target.segment === airline.segment;
    const targetName = target.name;
    const targetId = target.id;
    try {
      if (sameSegment) {
        const mergerResult = executeMergerAcquisition(airline, target, gameState2);
        gameState2.eventLog.push({
          type: "ai_full_merger",
          year: gameState2.year,
          quarter: gameState2.quarter,
          acquirerId: airline.id,
          acquirer: airline.name,
          targetId,
          target: targetName,
          stakeAtDecision: purchase.newStake,
          transferredSubsidiaryIds: mergerResult.transferredSubsidiaries,
          transferredStakePortfolio: mergerResult.ownershipSettlement.transferredPortfolio,
          label: `\u{1F91D} [\uD56D\uACF5\uC5C5\uACC4 \uC778\uC218\uD569\uBCD1] ${gameState2.year}\uB144 ${airline.name}, ${targetName} \uC778\uC218\xB7\uC644\uC804\uD569\uBCD1(\uC790\uD68C\uC0AC ${mergerResult.transferredSubsidiaries.length}\uACF3\xB7\uBCF4\uC720\uC9C0\uBD84 ${mergerResult.ownershipSettlement.transferredPortfolio.length}\uAC74 \uC2B9\uACC4)`
        });
      } else {
        const subsidiaryResult = executeSubsidiaryAcquisition(airline, target, gameState2);
        gameState2.eventLog.push({
          type: "ai_subsidiary_acquisition",
          year: gameState2.year,
          quarter: gameState2.quarter,
          acquirerId: airline.id,
          acquirer: airline.name,
          targetId,
          target: targetName,
          stakeAtDecision: purchase.newStake,
          controllingStake: subsidiaryResult.controllingStake,
          minorityShareholders: subsidiaryResult.minorityShareholders,
          freeFloatPercent: subsidiaryResult.freeFloatPercent,
          label: `\u{1F3E2} [\uD56D\uACF5\uC5C5\uACC4 \uC778\uC218\uD569\uBCD1] ${gameState2.year}\uB144 ${airline.name}, ${targetName} \uC9C0\uBD84 ${subsidiaryResult.controllingStake.toFixed(1)}% \uD655\uBCF4\xB7\uC790\uD68C\uC0AC \uD3B8\uC785(\uC18C\uC218\uC9C0\uBD84 \uC720\uC9C0)`
        });
      }
    } catch {
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderSubsidiarySupport(airline, gameState2) {
    if (gameState2.quarter !== 1) return;
    const subs = subsidiariesOf(airline, gameState2).filter((s) => s.ai);
    if (subs.length === 0) return;
    const parentReserve = airline.ai?.cashReserve ?? PERSONALITIES.balanced.cashReserve;
    for (const sub of subs) {
      const subReserve = sub.ai?.cashReserve ?? PERSONALITIES.balanced.cashReserve;
      if (sub.cash >= subReserve) continue;
      const affordable = airline.cash - parentReserve * 2;
      if (affordable <= 0) continue;
      const support = Math.min(subReserve * 1.5 - sub.cash, affordable);
      if (support <= 0) continue;
      try {
        fundSubsidiary(airline, sub, support);
      } catch {
      }
    }
  }
  function aiConsiderRouteRightSizing(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    const last = airline.history?.at?.(-1);
    if (!last?.routes?.length) return [];
    const aircraftById2 = context2.aircraftById;
    const airportsByIata2 = context2.airportsByIata;
    const changes = [];
    for (const report of last.routes) {
      const route = airline.routes.find((item) => item.id === report.routeId);
      if (!route) continue;
      const reportedFullCostV226 = Number.isFinite(report.fullCostNetworkContributionProfitV226) ? report.fullCostNetworkContributionProfitV226 : (report.networkContributionAfterLease ?? report.networkContributionProfit ?? report.profit ?? 0);
      if ((route._networkLossQuarters ?? 0) < 2 && (report.loadFactor ?? 1) >= 0.55 && reportedFullCostV226 >= 0) continue;
      const airportA = airportsByIata2[route.a];
      const airportB = airportsByIata2[route.b];
      const currentAircraft = aircraftById2[route.aircraftId];
      if (!airportA || !airportB || !currentAircraft) continue;
      const currentAssigned = [...(route.assignedTails ?? [])];
      const currentLeaseWeekly = currentAssigned.reduce((sum, tail) => {
        const entry = airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tail);
        const aircraft = entry ? aircraftById2[entry.aircraftId] : null;
        return sum + (entry?.ownership === "leased" ? (aircraft?.lease_musd_month ?? 0) * 1e6 * 12 / 52 : 0);
      }, 0);
      const currentNetWeekly = reportedFullCostV226 / WEEKS_PER_QUARTER;
      const sharedWeeklyV226 = ((report.allocatedInterestCostV226 ?? 0) + (report.allocatedCapabilityCostV226 ?? 0) + (report.allocatedOwnershipCostV226 ?? 0) + (report.allocatedAncillaryNetCostV226 ?? 0)) / WEEKS_PER_QUARTER;
      const competitorCount = countCompetitors(gameState2, airline.id, route.a, route.b, route.routeType ?? "passenger");
      const demandHint = Math.max(1, report.weeklyDemand ?? 0);
      const candidateTypes = Object.values(aircraftById2).filter((aircraft) =>
        aircraftRoleCompatibleV600(aircraft, route.routeType ?? "passenger") &&
        isAircraftAvailable(aircraft, gameState2.year) &&
        canOperateRoute(aircraft, airportA, airportB, route.distanceKm, gameState2.year) &&
        canPurchaseAircraft(airline.country, aircraft.country, gameState2.year, gameState2) &&
        (airline.segment !== "commuter" || aircraft.seats <= 80)
      ).sort((a, b) => {
        const targetSeats = Math.max(6, demandHint / (2 * 3 * 0.75));
        return Math.abs(a.seats - targetSeats) - Math.abs(b.seats - targetSeats) || a.opcost_usd_hr - b.opcost_usd_hr;
      }).slice(0, 14);
      let best = null;
      for (const aircraft of candidateTypes) {
        for (const frequency of [1, 2, 3, 4, 7].filter((f) => f <= Math.max(7, route.flightsPerWeekOneWay))) {
          const est = simulateRouteOperation({
            airportA,
            airportB,
            distanceKm: route.distanceKm,
            aircraft,
            flightsPerWeekOneWay: frequency,
            fareMultiplier: route.fareMultiplier,
            competitorCount,
            year: gameState2.year,
            cabinMix: defaultCabinMix(aircraft),
            mealTierId: recommendMealTier(estimateFlightHours(aircraft, route.distanceKm)),
            routeType: route.routeType ?? "passenger",
            cargoFareMultiplier: route.cargoFareMultiplier ?? 1,
            gameState: gameState2,
            airportsByIata: airportsByIata2,
            localOdMultiplier: route.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
            demandMultiplier: marketingDemandMultiplier(airline),
            overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
          });
          const sameType = aircraft.id === route.aircraftId;
          const requiredHours = weeklyHoursRequiredForRoute(aircraft, route.distanceKm, frequency);
          const idle = sameType ? requiredHours : idleHoursByType(airline, aircraft.id, aircraftById2, gameState2.year);
          const unitsNeeded = sameType || idle >= requiredHours ? 0 : Math.ceil((requiredHours - idle) / weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft));
          const acquisitionWeekly = unitsNeeded > 0 ? (aircraft.lease_musd_month ?? 0) * 1e6 * 12 / 52 * unitsNeeded : 0;
          const fleetWeeklyV226 = window.estimateCandidateFleetWeeklyCostV226(airline, aircraft, requiredHours, unitsNeeded, unitsNeeded > 0 ? { mode: "lease" } : { mode: "none" }, gameState2, aircraftById2);
          const netWeekly = est.weeklyProfit - fleetWeeklyV226 - sharedWeeklyV226;
          if (!best || netWeekly > best.netWeekly) best = { aircraft, frequency, est, unitsNeeded, netWeekly };
        }
      }
      if (!best) continue;
      const meaningfulImprovement = best.netWeekly > currentNetWeekly + Math.max(2500, Math.abs(currentNetWeekly) * 0.15);
      if (!meaningfulImprovement) continue;
      const leasedCount = (airline.fleet ?? []).filter((entry) => entry.ownership === "leased").length;
      const projectedFleet = Math.max(1, (airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) + best.unitsNeeded);
      if (best.unitsNeeded > 0 && leasedCount + best.unitsNeeded > Math.ceil(projectedFleet * 0.70)) continue;
      const fleetBefore = airline.fleet.length;
      const cashBefore = airline.cash;
      try {
        for (let i = 0; i < best.unitsNeeded; i++) {
          buyAircraft(airline, best.aircraft, {
            tailNumber: `${airline.id}-${airline.fleet.length + 1}L`,
            year: gameState2.year,
            ownership: "leased",
            gameState: gameState2,
            acquisitionIntentV253:{unitsNeeded:best.unitsNeeded,reason:"route_right_sizing"}
          });
        }
        updateRoute(airline, route.id, {
          aircraftId: best.aircraft.id,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: best.frequency,
          cabinMix: defaultCabinMix(best.aircraft),
          mealTierId: recommendMealTier(estimateFlightHours(best.aircraft, route.distanceKm))
        }, { aircraftById: aircraftById2, currentYear: gameState2.year });
        for (const tail of currentAssigned) {
          const stillAssigned = airline.routes.some((r) => (r.assignedTails ?? []).includes(tail));
          if (stillAssigned) continue;
          const entry = airline.fleet.find((f) => f.tailNumber === tail);
          const aircraft = entry ? aircraftById2[entry.aircraftId] : null;
          if (!entry || !aircraft) continue;
          try { sellAircraft(airline, tail, aircraft, { year: gameState2.year, gameState: gameState2 }); } catch {}
        }
        route._networkLossQuarters = 0;
        changes.push({ routeId: route.id, fromAircraft: currentAircraft.id, toAircraft: best.aircraft.id, fromFrequency: route.flightsPerWeekOneWay, toFrequency: best.frequency, estimatedNetWeekly: best.netWeekly });
        gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
        gameState2.eventLog.push({
          type: "ai_route_right_sizing",
          year: gameState2.year,
          quarter: gameState2.quarter,
          airlineId: airline.id,
          airline: airline.name,
          routeId: route.id,
          label: `🛠️ ${airline.name}, ${route.a}-${route.b} 수익성 구조조정: ${currentAircraft.model}→${best.aircraft.model}, 주 ${best.frequency}회`
        });
      } catch {
        airline.fleet.length = fleetBefore;
        airline.cash = cashBefore;
      }
    }
    return changes;
  }
  function aiConsiderRouteClosures(airline, gameState2, canCloseThisQuarter) {
    const last = latestStatement(airline);
    if (!last?.routes?.length) return [];
    const relevantShock = (gameState2.activeEvents || []).some(event => {
      if (!event || !(event.remainingQuarters > 0)) return false;
      const scoped = event.scope === "global" || event.scope === `airline:${airline.id}` || (event.scope?.startsWith?.("region:") && (airline.routes || []).some(route => airportsByIata?.[route.a]?.region === event.scope.slice(7) || airportsByIata?.[route.b]?.region === event.scope.slice(7)));
      if (!scoped) return false;
      return ["strike","accident","regional_disaster","pandemic","covid19","fuel_shock","global_recession"].includes(event.type) || (event.demandMultiplier ?? 1) < 0.85 || (event.costMultiplier ?? 1) > 1.20;
    });
    const recentMargin = (() => {
      const rows = (airline.history || []).slice(-4);
      const revenue = rows.reduce((n,x)=>n+(x.revenue||0),0);
      const profit = rows.reduce((n,x)=>n+(x.profit||0),0);
      return revenue > 0 ? profit/revenue : 0;
    })();
    const distress = airline.cash < -25e6 || recentOperatingCashFlow(airline, 4) < 0 || recentMargin < -0.04;
    const strategic = typeof isStrategicCarrierV225 === "function" && isStrategicCarrierV225(airline);
    const candidates = [];
    for (const report of last.routes) {
      const route = airline.routes.find(item => item.id === report.routeId);
      if (!route) continue;
      if ((route._v2210RecoveryProtectionQuarters || 0) > 0) {
        route._networkLossQuarters = 0;
        route._v253StructuralLossQuarters = 0;
        route._v253NormalObservedQuarters = 0;
        continue;
      }
      const postOrgNetwork = Number.isFinite(report.networkContributionProfit) ? report.networkContributionProfit : (report.profit || 0);
      const preOrgNetwork = Number.isFinite(report.preOrganizationNetworkContributionAlpha5)
        ? report.preOrganizationNetworkContributionAlpha5
        : postOrgNetwork + Math.max(0, report.organizationCostAlpha5 || 0);
      const value = Number.isFinite(report.fullCostNetworkContributionProfitV226)
        ? report.fullCostNetworkContributionProfitV226
        : (report.networkContributionAfterLease ?? postOrgNetwork);
      report.networkContributionAfterFullCostV226 = value;
      report.preOrganizationNetworkContributionAlpha5 = preOrgNetwork;
      const load = Math.max(0, report.loadFactor || 0);
      const protectedHealthyRoute = load >= 0.70 && preOrgNetwork >= 0;
      const abnormalQuarter = relevantShock || Boolean(report.transientShockQuarterV253);
      route._lastNetworkContributionProfit = value;
      route._lastFullCostNetworkContributionProfitV226 = value;
      route._lastPreOrganizationContributionV253 = preOrgNetwork;
      route._lastLoadFactorV253 = load;
      if (abnormalQuarter) {
        route._v253TransientExcludedQuarters = (route._v253TransientExcludedQuarters || 0) + 1;
        route._networkLossQuarters = route._v253StructuralLossQuarters || 0;
        continue;
      }
      route._v253NormalObservedQuarters = (route._v253NormalObservedQuarters || 0) + 1;
      const structuralLoss = preOrgNetwork < 0;
      if (protectedHealthyRoute || !structuralLoss) {
        route._v253StructuralLossQuarters = 0;
        route._networkLossQuarters = 0;
        continue;
      }
      route._v253StructuralLossQuarters = (route._v253StructuralLossQuarters || 0) + 1;
      route._networkLossQuarters = route._v253StructuralLossQuarters;
      const lossRatio = Math.abs(preOrgNetwork) / Math.max(1, report.revenue || 0);
      const highLoad = load >= 0.70;
      let required = highLoad ? 6 : 4;
      if (strategic) required += 2;
      if (recentMargin >= 0) required += 1;
      if ((route._v253NormalObservedQuarters || 0) < required) continue;
      if (route._v253StructuralLossQuarters >= required) candidates.push({route, report, value, preOrgNetwork, load, lossRatio, required});
    }
    const minimumRoutes = airline.segment === "full_service" ? 2 : 1;
    if ((!canCloseThisQuarter && !distress) || airline.routes.length <= minimumRoutes || !candidates.length) return [];
    candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.preOrgNetwork-b.preOrgNetwork);
    const maxShare = strategic ? 0.05 : distress ? 0.10 : 0.04;
    const maxClosures = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(airline.routes.length * maxShare));
    const closed=[];
    for (const target of candidates.slice(0,Math.min(maxClosures,airline.routes.length-minimumRoutes))) {
      removeRoute(airline,target.route.id,gameState2);
      closed.push(target.route.id);
      updateNetworkPerformanceCounters(gameState2,{aiNetworkClosures:1});
      gameState2.eventLog ??= [];
      gameState2.eventLog.push({type:"ai_structural_route_closure_v253",year:gameState2.year,quarter:gameState2.quarter,airlineId:airline.id,airline:airline.name,routeId:target.route.id,a:target.route.a,b:target.route.b,preOrganizationContribution:target.preOrgNetwork,fullCostContribution:target.value,loadFactor:target.load,normalLossQuarters:target.route._v253StructuralLossQuarters,label:`📉 ${airline.name}, ${target.route.a}-${target.route.b} 노선 폐지(정상분기 직접 기여손익 ${target.route._v253StructuralLossQuarters}분기 연속 적자)`});
    }
    return closed;
  }
  function aiApplyFinancialRecovery(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    if (!airline.ai) return;
    const lastFourCashFlow = recentOperatingCashFlow(airline, 4);
    const distressed = airline.cash < 0 || lastFourCashFlow < 0;
    if (!distressed) return;
    ensureCapabilities(airline);
    for (const type of CAPABILITY_TYPES) {
      const cap = airline.capabilities[type];
      if (airline.cash < -25e6) cap.plan = type === "maintenance" ? "maintain" : "stop";
      else if (cap.plan === "max" || cap.plan === "normal") cap.plan = "reduce";
    }
    const aircraftById2 = context2.aircraftById;
    const maxDisposals = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) * 0.1));
    let disposed = 0;
    const byType = new Map();
    for (const entry of airline.fleet ?? []) {
      if (!byType.has(entry.aircraftId)) byType.set(entry.aircraftId, []);
      byType.get(entry.aircraftId).push(entry);
    }
    for (const [aircraftId, entries] of byType) {
      if (disposed >= maxDisposals) break;
      const aircraft = aircraftById2[aircraftId];
      if (!aircraft) continue;
      const used = usedHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2);
      const requiredCount = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(used / Math.max(1, weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft))));
      const excess = Math.max(0, entries.length - requiredCount);
      const candidates = [...entries].sort((a, b) => fleetEntryAge(b, gameState2.year) - fleetEntryAge(a, gameState2.year));
      for (const entry of candidates.slice(0, excess)) {
        if (disposed >= maxDisposals) break;
        try {
          sellAircraft(airline, entry.tailNumber, aircraft, { year: gameState2.year, gameState: gameState2 });
          disposed += 1;
        } catch {
        }
      }
    }
    if (airline.cash < 0 && lastFourCashFlow > 0) {
      const capacity = availableLoanCapacity(airline, aircraftById2, gameState2.year);
      const amount = Math.min(capacity * 0.25, Math.max(0, -airline.cash + 1e7));
      if (amount >= 1e6) {
        try {
          takeLoan(airline, amount, { year: gameState2.year, aircraftById: aircraftById2 });
        } catch {
        }
      }
    }
  }
  function aiTrimExcessFleet(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    const aircraftById2 = context2.aircraftById;
    const maxDisposals = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) * 0.25));
    let disposed = 0;
    const byType = new Map();
    for (const entry of airline.fleet ?? []) {
      if (!byType.has(entry.aircraftId)) byType.set(entry.aircraftId, []);
      byType.get(entry.aircraftId).push(entry);
    }
    for (const [aircraftId, entries] of byType) {
      if (disposed >= maxDisposals) break;
      const aircraft = aircraftById2[aircraftId];
      if (!aircraft) continue;
      const used = usedHoursByType(airline, aircraftId, aircraftById2);
      const routesUsingType = airline.routes.filter((route) => route.aircraftId === aircraftId).length;
      const operationalNeed = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(used / Math.max(1, weeklyUtilizationHours(aircraft))));
      const strategicSpare = routesUsingType >= 4 && entries.length >= 4 ? 1 : 0;
      const keep = Math.min(entries.length, operationalNeed + strategicSpare);
      const excess = Math.max(0, entries.length - keep);
      if (excess <= 0) continue;
      const candidates = [...entries].sort((a, b) => {
        const aLease = a.ownership === "leased" ? 0 : 1;
        const bLease = b.ownership === "leased" ? 0 : 1;
        return aLease - bLease || fleetEntryAge(b, gameState2.year) - fleetEntryAge(a, gameState2.year);
      });
      for (const entry of candidates.slice(0, excess)) {
        if (disposed >= maxDisposals) break;
        const assigned = airline.routes.some((route) => (route.assignedTails ?? []).includes(entry.tailNumber));
        if (assigned) continue;
        try {
          sellAircraft(airline, entry.tailNumber, aircraft, { year: gameState2.year, gameState: gameState2 });
          disposed += 1;
        } catch {}
      }
    }
    if (disposed > 0) {
      gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
      gameState2.eventLog.push({
        type: "ai_excess_fleet_disposal",
        year: gameState2.year,
        quarter: gameState2.quarter,
        airlineId: airline.id,
        airline: airline.name,
        count: disposed,
        label: `♻️ ${airline.name}, 유휴 기재 ${disposed}대 매각·리스 반납`
      });
    }
    return disposed;
  }
  function forceDormantLeaseReentry(airline, gameState2, context2) {
    if ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) > 0 || (airline.fleet?.length ?? 0) > 0) return [];
    if ((airline._reentryFailureQuarters ?? 0) < 8) return [];
    const hq = context2.airportsByIata[airline.headquarters];
    if (!hq || (hq.closed_year && gameState2.year >= hq.closed_year)) return [];
    const profile = airline.ai;
    const reserve = Math.min(profile?.cashReserve ?? 0, Math.max(15e6, Math.max(0, airline.cash) * 0.25));
    if (airline.cash < reserve) return [];
    const maxSeats = airline.segment === "commuter" ? 80 : airline.segment === "regional" ? 120 : airline.segment === "lcc" ? 190 : 260;
    const served = servedPairs(airline, gameState2);
    const destinationPool = aiDestinationPool(airline, hq, gameState2, context2.airports, context2.airportsByIata, served).slice(0, 30);
    const aircraftList = Object.values(context2.aircraftById).filter((ac) => !isFreighterAircraftV600(ac) && isAircraftAvailable(ac, gameState2.year) && ac.seats <= maxSeats);
    let best = null;
    const frequency = 3;
    const slotsNeeded = slotsRequiredForFrequency(frequency);
    for (const entry of destinationPool) {
      const dest = entry.dest;
      const slotType = slotPoolTypeForAirports(hq, dest);
      if (availableSlots(gameState2, hq, slotType) < slotsNeeded || availableSlots(gameState2, dest, slotType) < slotsNeeded) continue;
      for (const aircraft of aircraftList) {
        if (!canOperateRoute(aircraft, hq, dest, entry.distanceKm, gameState2.year)) continue;
        const { cabinMix, mealTierId } = aiServicePreference(airline, aircraft, entry.distanceKm);
        const est = simulateRouteOperation({
          airportA: hq,
          airportB: dest,
          distanceKm: entry.distanceKm,
          aircraft,
          flightsPerWeekOneWay: frequency,
          fareMultiplier: 1,
          competitorCount: countCompetitors(gameState2, airline.id, hq.iata, dest.iata),
          year: gameState2.year,
          cabinMix,
          mealTierId,
          localOdMultiplier: entry.legalDecision?.localOdMultiplier ?? 1,
          overheadRatio: airlineOverheadRatio(airline, gameState2.year)
        });
        const weeklyLeaseCost = (aircraft.lease_musd_month ?? aircraft.price_musd * 0.012) * 1e6 * 12 / 52;
        const netWeekly = est.weeklyProfit - weeklyLeaseCost;
        const failures = airline._reentryFailureQuarters ?? 0;
        const minimumNet = failures >= 12 ? -Infinity : Math.max(-10000, -est.weeklyRevenue * 0.02);
        if (netWeekly <= minimumNet) continue;
        if (!best || netWeekly > best.netWeekly) best = { dest, aircraft, cabinMix, mealTierId, netWeekly };
      }
    }
    if (!best) return [];
    const fleetLengthBefore = airline.fleet.length;
    try {
      leaseAircraftFromMarket(airline, best.aircraft, gameState2, { emergency: true, tailSuffix: "R" });
      const result = requestRoute(airline, context2.airportsByIata, context2.aircraftById, gameState2, {
        a: hq.iata,
        b: best.dest.iata,
        aircraftId: best.aircraft.id,
        flightsPerWeekOneWay: frequency,
        cabinMix: best.cabinMix,
        mealTierId: best.mealTierId
      });
      airline._reentryFailureQuarters = 0;
      airline.operatingStatus = "active";
      gameState2.eventLog ?? (gameState2.eventLog = []);
      gameState2.eventLog.push({ type: "ai_dormant_emergency_lease_reentry", year: gameState2.year, quarter: gameState2.quarter, airlineId: airline.id, airline: airline.name, label: `♻️ ${airline.name}, 장기 휴면 후 ${best.aircraft.model} 리스로 ${hq.iata}-${best.dest.iata} 재진입` });
      return [result.status === "active" ? result.route : { id: result.id, pending: true, turns: result.turns }];
    } catch {
      airline.fleet.length = fleetLengthBefore;
      return [];
    }
  }
  function stableQuarterSlot(id, year) {
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) hash = hash * 31 + id.charCodeAt(i) | 0;
    return Math.abs(hash + year) % 4;
  }
  function runAiDecisions(gameState2, context2) {
    const results = [];
