    __timed("supportRecoveryMs",()=>{for (const airline of gameState2.airlines) {
      aiConsiderSubsidiarySupport(airline, gameState2);
      aiApplyFinancialRecovery(airline, gameState2, context2);
    }});
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines) {
      if (!airline.ai) continue;
      const debug = gameState2._debugAiStages === true;
      const tag = `[ai] ${gameState2.year}Q${gameState2.quarter} ${airline.id}`;
      if (debug) console.log(`${tag} start routes=${airline.routes.length} fleet=${airline.fleet.length} alliance=${airline.alliance ?? "-"}`);
      const mySlot = stableQuarterSlot(airline.id, gameState2.year);
      const isMyQuarter = mySlot === gameState2.quarter - 1;
      if ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) === 0) {
        let reopened = __timed("reentryMs",()=>aiConsiderExpansion(airline, gameState2, context2, { forceReentry: true }));
        if (!reopened.length) reopened = __timed("reentryMs",()=>forceDormantLeaseReentry(airline, gameState2, context2));
        if (reopened.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, openedRoutes: reopened.map((r) => r.id), strategy: "dormant_reentry" });
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} dormant reentry ${reopened.length ? "succeeded" : "deferred"}`);
        continue;
      }
      if (airline.segment === "cargo") {
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} cargo start`);
        airline.operatingStatus = "active";
        airline._reentryFailureQuarters = 0;
        __timed("cargoManagementMs",()=>aiAdjustFares(airline));
        const cargoClosedRoutes = __timed("cargoManagementMs",()=>aiConsiderRouteClosures(airline, gameState2, isMyQuarter));
        if (cargoClosedRoutes.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, closedRoutes: cargoClosedRoutes, strategy: "cargo_profitability" });
        if ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) === 0) {
          const cargoReopened = __timed("reentryMs",()=>aiConsiderExpansion(airline, gameState2, context2, { forceReentry: true }));
          if (cargoReopened.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, openedRoutes: cargoReopened.map((r) => r.id), strategy: "cargo_reentry" });
          continue;
        }
        if (isMyQuarter) {
          __timed("frequencyMs",()=>aiConsiderFrequencyIncrease(airline, gameState2, context2));
          const cargoOpened = __timed("singleExpansionMs",()=>aiConsiderExpansion(airline, gameState2, context2));
          if (cargoOpened.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, openedRoutes: cargoOpened.map((r) => r.id), strategy: "cargo_network_scored" });
        }
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} cargo done`);
        continue;
      }
      airline.operatingStatus = "active";
      airline._reentryFailureQuarters = 0;
      const modernization = __timed("fleetModernizationMs",()=>aiConsiderFleetModernization(airline, gameState2, context2));
      if (modernization.replaced.length || modernization.closed.length || modernization.disposed.length) {
        results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, modernization });
      }
      if (isMyQuarter) {
        const excessFleetDisposed = __timed("fleetTrimMs",()=>aiTrimExcessFleet(airline, gameState2, context2));
        if (excessFleetDisposed) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, excessFleetDisposed });
      }
      __timed("fareAdjustmentMs",()=>aiAdjustFares(airline));
      if (isMyQuarter) {
        const rightSizedRoutes = __timed("routeRightSizingMs",()=>aiConsiderRouteRightSizing(airline, gameState2, context2));
        if (rightSizedRoutes.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, rightSizedRoutes });
      }
      const closedRoutes = __timed("routeClosureMs",()=>aiConsiderRouteClosures(airline, gameState2, isMyQuarter));
      if (closedRoutes.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, closedRoutes });
      if ((airline.routes?.length ?? 0) === 0) {
        let reopenedAfterClosure = __timed("reentryMs",()=>aiConsiderExpansion(airline, gameState2, context2, { forceReentry: true }));
        if (!reopenedAfterClosure.length) reopenedAfterClosure = __timed("reentryMs",()=>forceDormantLeaseReentry(airline, gameState2, context2));
        if (reopenedAfterClosure.length) results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, openedRoutes: reopenedAfterClosure.map((r) => r.id), strategy: "immediate_post_closure_reentry" });
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} post-closure reentry ${reopenedAfterClosure.length ? "succeeded" : "deferred"}`);
        continue;
      }
      __timed("cateringAncillaryRotationMs",()=>{aiConsiderCatering(airline, gameState2, context2);
      aiConsiderAncillaryInvestment(airline, gameState2, context2);
      aiConsiderFleetRotation(airline, gameState2, context2.aircraftById);});
      if (isMyQuarter) {
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} frequency start`);
        __timed("frequencyMs",()=>aiConsiderFrequencyIncrease(airline, gameState2, context2));
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} frequency done`);
      }
      __timed("allianceMs",()=>aiConsiderHistoricalAllianceMembership(airline, gameState2, context2));
      if (debug) console.log(`${tag} acquisition start`);
      __timed("acquisitionMs",()=>aiConsiderAcquisitions(airline, gameState2, context2));
      if (debug) console.log(`${tag} acquisition done`);
      if (isMyQuarter) {
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} twoleg start`);
        const twoLegOpened = __timed("twoLegExpansionMs",()=>aiConsiderTwoLegExpansion(airline, gameState2, context2));
        if (debug) console.log(`${tag} twoleg done`);
        if (twoLegOpened.length) {
          results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, openedRoutes: twoLegOpened.map((r) => r.id), strategy: "two_leg_network" });
        } else {
          if (debug) console.log(`${tag} expansion start`);
          const opened = __timed("singleExpansionMs",()=>aiConsiderExpansion(airline, gameState2, context2));
          if (debug) console.log(`${tag} expansion done`);
          if (opened.length) {
            results.push({ airlineId: airline.id, openedRoutes: opened.map((r) => r.id), strategy: "network_scored" });
          }
        }
      }
      if (debug) console.log(`${tag} done`);

    return results;

  // src/core/victory.js
  var REGIONS2 = [
    "north_america",
    "latin_america",
    "europe",
    "africa",
    "middle_east_south_asia",
    "east_southeast_asia",
    "oceania"
  ];
  var REGION_LABELS = {
    north_america: "\uBD81\uC544\uBA54\uB9AC\uCE74",
    latin_america: "\uC911\uB0A8\uBBF8",
    europe: "\uC720\uB7FD",
    africa: "\uC544\uD504\uB9AC\uCE74",
    middle_east_south_asia: "\uC911\uB3D9\xB7\uB0A8\uC544\uC2DC\uC544",
    east_southeast_asia: "\uB3D9\xB7\uB3D9\uB0A8\uC544\uC2DC\uC544",
    oceania: "\uC624\uC138\uC544\uB2C8\uC544"
  };
  function regionLabel(region) {
    return REGION_LABELS[region] ?? region;
  }
  var CATEGORY_TARGET_REGIONS = {
    piston: 3,
    turboprop: 3,
    early_jet: 4,
    regional_jet: 4,
    narrowbody: 5,
    widebody: 6,
    supersonic: 6,
    electric: 5,
    hydrogen: 6,
    future: 6
  };
  function targetRegionCount(airline, aircraftById2) {
    let best = 3;
    for (const f of airline.fleet) {
      const cat = aircraftById2[f.aircraftId]?.category;
      if (cat && CATEGORY_TARGET_REGIONS[cat] != null) {
        best = Math.max(best, CATEGORY_TARGET_REGIONS[cat]);
      }
    }
    return Math.min(best, REGIONS2.length);
  }
  function regionalPassengerLeaders(gameState2, airportsByIata2) {
    const totals2 = Object.fromEntries(REGIONS2.map((r) => [r, {}]));
    for (const airline of gameState2.airlines) {
      const last = airline.history[airline.history.length - 1];
      if (!last) continue;
      for (const r of last.routes) {
        const airportA = airportsByIata2[r.a];
        const airportB = airportsByIata2[r.b];
        for (const airport of [airportA, airportB]) {
          if (!airport?.region || !totals2[airport.region]) continue;
          totals2[airport.region][airline.id] = (totals2[airport.region][airline.id] ?? 0) + r.paxCarried / 2;
        }
      }
    }
    const leaders = {};
    for (const region of REGIONS2) {
      const entries = Object.entries(totals2[region]);
      if (entries.length === 0) {
        leaders[region] = null;
        continue;
      }
      entries.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
      leaders[region] = entries[0][0];
    }
    return leaders;
  }
  function victoryProgress(airline, gameState2, airportsByIata2, aircraftById2) {
    const leaders = regionalPassengerLeaders(gameState2, airportsByIata2);
    const ledRegions = REGIONS2.filter((r) => leaders[r] === airline.id);
    const homeRegion = airportsByIata2[airline.headquarters]?.region ?? null;
    const target = targetRegionCount(airline, aircraftById2);
    const hasHome = homeRegion ? ledRegions.includes(homeRegion) : false;
    return {
      leaders,
      ledRegions,
      ledCount: ledRegions.length,
      target,
      homeRegion,
      hasHome,
      achieved: ledRegions.length >= target && hasHome
    };
  }
